Abstract: This research evaluates the efficacy of large language models (LLMs) in generating new product ideas. To do so, we compare three pools of ideas for new products targeted toward college students and priced at 50 dollars or less. The first pool of ideas was created by university students in a product design course before the availability of LLMs. The second and third pools of ideas were generated by GPT-4 from OpenAI using zero-shot and few-shot prompting, respectively. We evaluated idea quality using standard market research techniques to predict average purchase intent probability. We used text mining to assess idea similarity and human raters to evaluate idea novelty. We find that AI-generated ideas outperform human-generated ideas in terms of average purchase intent, with few-shot prompting yielding slightly higher intent than zero-shot prompting. However, AI-generated ideas are perceived as less novel and exhibit higher pairwise similarity, particularly with few-shot prompting, indicating a less diverse solution landscape. When focusing on the quality of the best ideas rather than the average ideas, we find that AI-generated ideas are seven times more likely to rank among the top 10 percent of ideas, demonstrating a significant advantage over human-generated ideas. We propose that this seven-to-one advantage is a conservative estimate because it does not account for the greater productivity of AI. Our findings suggest that despite some drawbacks, AI creativity presents a substantial benefit in generating high-quality ideas for new product development.

</details>


### [22] [Evaluating Agentic Bioinformatics through Function, Evidence, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27556)
*Phuc Pham,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型智能体在生物信息学分析中缺乏科学可信度评估的问题，提出Function–Evidence–Validation（FEV）框架，将可检查的工作流轨迹而非最终输出作为主要分析单元，并基于该框架系统梳理了109个智能体系统与28个基准/评估资源（共128篇论文），发现规划与工具执行进展快于可复现性、溯源、科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性实证测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述主要按应用、架构和智能体能力分类生物信息学智能体，但未能统一衡量智能体生成工作流的可问责性。流畅的响应、成功的工具调用和基准性能并不能证明科学可信度，因此需要一个能够联合评估工作流可靠性的分析框架。

Method: 提出FEV（Function–Evidence–Validation）框架：分别分离演示的工作流操作（Function）、行为与主张的可追踪支撑（Evidence）、以及用例特定验证（Validation）。基于FEV对109个智能体/贴近智能体系统及28个基准或评估资源进行系统映射分析，覆盖基因组学、单细胞与空间组学、蛋白质科学、药物发现、计算病理学和通用生物信息学自动化等六个领域。

Result: 跨领域分析表明，规划与工具介导的执行进展快于可复现性、溯源、稳健科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性实证测试。智能体生物信息学的评估应从最终答案正确性转向工作流正确性。

Conclusion: FEV框架为比较智能体系统以及设计透明、可审计、科学可问责的生物信息学工作流提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly plan, execute, and interpret biological analyses, yet fluent responses, successful tool calls, and benchmark performance alone do not establish scientific credibility. Existing reviews primarily organize biological agents by application, architecture, and agentic capability, but do not jointly operationalize the accountability of agent-generated workflows. We address this gap by treating the inspectable workflow trajectory, rather than architecture or final output alone, as the primary unit of analysis. We introduce the Function--Evidence--Validation (FEV) framework, which separates demonstrated workflow operations, traceable support for actions and claims, and use-case-specific validation. Using FEV, we map 109 agentic or agent-adjacent systems and 28 benchmark or evaluation resources, representing 128 unique publications across genomics, single-cell and spatial omics, protein science, drug discovery, computational pathology, and general bioinformatics automation. Across domains, planning and tool-mediated execution have advanced more rapidly than replayability, provenance, robust scientific assessment, external validation, and prospective empirical testing. We therefore argue that agentic bioinformatics should be assessed through workflow correctness rather than final-answer correctness alone. FEV provides a practical basis for comparing systems and designing transparent, auditable, and scientifically accountable bioinformatics workflows.

</details>


### [23] [DeepResearch Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27562)
*Yong Huang,Yulu Huang,for the team Collaboration*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍DeepResearch Agent System，一个基于稀疏激活架构（300亿总参数，仅30亿激活）的LLM智能体系统，在多项检索和智能体基准上达到SOTA表现。系统结合128K上下文窗口、层级注意力、双模式推理引擎（ReAct与IterResearch）以及基于GRPO的强化学习优化框架，实现3.2倍加速推理、31.2%推理精度提升、92.1%工具调用准确率，并在多个基准（Humanity's Last Exam 87.3%，WebWalkerQA 91.2%）上表现优异，全部代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有大语言模型在深度信息检索、多步推理和自主研究任务中面临的推理效率低、长上下文处理能力不足、多工具协调困难以及训练稳定性差等问题，旨在构建一个高效、高性能且可复现的自主研究智能体系统。

Method: 系统采用以下核心方法：(1) 稀疏激活架构（30B总参数/3B激活），提升推理效率；(2) 128K上下文窗口配合层级注意力机制，提升长文本处理能力；(3) 双模式推理引擎（ReAct用于基础多步推理，IterResearch支持最多20步迭代研究）；(4) 多工具协调模块，整合检索、计算、网页搜索和文件解析；(5) 基于GRPO的强化学习框架提供token级策略梯度优化；(6) 自动化数据合成流水线，采用种子式扩展策略。

Result: 系统在多个方面取得显著成果：相比同等规模密集模型推理速度提升3.2倍；层级注意力在长上下文任务上精确率提升18.7%、召回率提升23.4%；双模式推理引擎相比单次推理基线提升31.2%精度；工具使用准确率达92.1%；训练稳定性提升35%，收敛速度加快42%；数据合成可用率达92.5%；基准成绩为Humanity's Last Exam 87.3%、BrowserComp Chinese 85.3%、WebWalkerQA 91.2%。

Conclusion: DeepResearch Agent System通过稀疏激活、层级注意力、双模式推理和GRPO强化学习的综合设计，在效率、准确性和鲁棒性上均大幅超越现有方法，且完全开源（包含数据合成、训练和推理代码），可广泛应用于学术研究、商业分析、研发支持和教育等场景。

Abstract: The DeepResearch Agent System is a large language model system engineered for deep information retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and autonomous research tasks. Built upon a sparse activation architecture with 30 billion total parameters of which only 3 billion are activated per token, the system achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple agent search benchmarks while delivering 3.2 times faster inference compared to dense counterparts of equivalent scale. The system supports a 128K-token context window with hierarchical attention mechanisms that yield 18.7% accuracy and 23.4% recall improvements over standard long-context approaches. A dual-mode reasoning engine provides both a ReAct paradigm for basic multi-step problem solving and an IterResearch mode for high-performance iterative research with up to 20 reasoning steps, collectively delivering a 31.2% accuracy improvement over single-pass baselines. Multi-tool coordination integrates retrieval, computation, web search, and file parsing modules to achieve 92.1% tool-use accuracy. A reinforcement learning optimization framework based on the GRPO algorithm provides token-level policy gradients that improve training stability by 35% and accelerate convergence by 42%. An automated data synthesis pipeline with seed-based expansion achieves a 92.5% usability rate. Benchmark results include 87.3% on Humanity's Last Exam, 85.3% on BrowserComp Chinese, and 91.2% on WebWalkerQA. The system is fully open-sourced, including data synthesis, training, and inference code, and supports applications in academic research, business analysis, R&D support, and education.

</details>


### [24] [What makes prompts a graph: necessary and sufficient conditions for prompt graph engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27578)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过概念分析提出"提示图工程"（Prompt Graph Engineering）的构成性定义，将提示视为显式、可执行、可改进的图节点，并给出四个判定条件（显式结构、结构与内容分离、可执行语义、图作为一等工程产物），建立了包含/排除测试并应用于六个真实系统，同时梳理了该概念的历史谱系并提出了研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中提示词早已不是孤立的字符串，而是以图结构（如LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow）组织成复杂的可执行工作流。然而术语体系严重滞后：图被含糊地称为推理拓扑、多智能体对话或编排产物，提示工程仍被理解为撰写一个好字符串。缺乏一个权威的参考定义来精确命名这一普遍实践。

Method: 采用概念分析方法，基于带持久标识符的来源并进行原始灰色文献补充。具体步骤包括：（1）重建概念的谱系演变（从数据流图、构建系统、提示链到思维拓扑直至编译优化的图）；（2）提出构成性定义和四个条件；（3）将条件操作化为包含/排除测试；（4）与六个邻近概念划清边界；（5）将测试应用于LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow、AutoGen、CrewAI和Claude Code子代理六个真实系统。

Result: 提出了提示图工程的可操作定义，给出了四个必要条件，并建立了包含/排除测试。测试在六个真实系统和六个邻近概念上表现出一致的包含与排除结果，验证了定义的有效性和边界划分的清晰性。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于为业界已在日常实践中广泛使用但缺乏精确命名的"提示图工程"提供了操作化定义和共享词汇，并沿四个设计张力轴线提出了后续研究议程，填补了工程实践与学术术语之间的空白。

Abstract: Prompts stopped being isolated strings some time ago. In real systems, one model call feeds another, retrieval interleaves with generation, routers branch, and aggregators merge parallel results. Practice converged on a single structure to hold this together: the graph. Frameworks such as LangGraph, DSPy, and Prompt Flow expose it openly, and research systems already optimize it automatically. The vocabulary, however, lags behind. Graph names, variously, a reasoning topology inside one sampling strategy, a multi-agent conversation, or an orchestration artifact, while prompt engineering still evokes writing one good string. What is missing is a reference definition treating prompts as nodes of an explicit, executable, improvable graph. We build that definition through conceptual analysis over sources with persistent identifiers, complemented by primary grey literature. We reconstruct the genealogy of the idea, from dataflow graphs and build systems, through prompt chaining and the thought topologies (chain, tree, graph), to graphs compiled and optimized as artifacts. We then propose a constitutive definition of prompt graph engineering, state its four conditions (explicit structure, separation between structure and prompt content, executable semantics, and the graph as a first-class engineering artifact), and operationalize them as an inclusion and exclusion test. We draw the boundary against six neighboring concepts and apply the test to six real systems (LangGraph, DSPy, Prompt Flow, AutoGen, CrewAI, and Claude Code subagents); it includes and excludes consistently. We close with a research agenda organized along four design tension axes. The contribution is an operational definition and a shared vocabulary for a practice that industry already exercises daily without naming precisely.

</details>


### [25] [From Minds to Models: The Intersection of Psychology and LLM Behaviours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27579)
*Oliver Guidetti,Reza Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于内隐联想测验的提示词改编版本，测试ChatGPT在开放文本中是否产生跨种族条件的情感差异。共生成126个提示词，提交至三个GPT模型获得378条回复。双向方差分析显示种族条件有微小主效应(p=.042, partial eta squared=.044)，但在秩变换敏感性分析中不显著，且Tukey校正比较未发现显著配对差异。证据表明情感差异微弱且依赖分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的决策行为复杂、非线性且难以解释，类似于人类心智。研究者认为，用于探究不可观察心智过程的心理学方法可能有助于检验LLM的行为，特别是在政府和医疗等敏感领域。本研究旨在测试ChatGPT在开放文本中是否表现出类似内隐偏见的种族情感差异。

Method: 基于提示词改编的内隐联想测验(IAT)。将14个基础问题与8个种族类别及1个种族无关对照组交叉，生成126个提示词。每个提示词分别提交至GPT-3.5T、GPT-4和GPT-4T各一次，得到378条回复。情感评分由分类标签和来源分数构成：正面标签保留原始分数，负面标签取负值，中性回复记零。使用双向方差分析检验种族条件和模型主效应及交互作用，并进行秩变换敏感性分析和Tukey事后校正比较。

Result: 双向方差分析发现种族条件有微小主效应，F(8,351)=2.04, p=.042, partial eta squared=.044；模型主效应不显著，F(2,351)=0.07, p=.933；无交互作用，F(16,351)=0.23, p=.999。但在秩变换敏感性分析中效应不保留，F(8,351)=1.53, p=.145。Tukey校正比较未发现显著配对差异。一个未校正的欧洲-原住民澳大利亚比较显著，但属于事后选择，仅作为假设生成报告。

Conclusion: ChatGPT在种族条件下产生情感差异的证据弱且依赖分析方法。情感评分无法区分评价性偏见与提示词所引发的历史内容的效价。研究者提出需要设计更改以解决这些局限性，并主张跨学科发展模型偏见的行measures。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compared with the human mind because their decision-making is complex, non-linear and difficult to interpret. Psychological methods developed to investigate unobservable mental processes may therefore help examine LLM behaviour, particularly in government and healthcare. Building on prompt-based adaptations of the Implicit Association Test, this study tested whether ChatGPT produced sentiment differences across racial conditions in open-ended text. Fourteen base questions were crossed with eight racial categories and a race-agnostic control, producing 126 prompts. Each was submitted once to GPT-3.5T, GPT-4 and GPT-4T, yielding 378 responses. Sentiment scores were derived from categorical labels and source scores: positive labels retained the source score, negative labels were assigned its negative, and neutral responses were coded zero. A two-way ANOVA found a small main effect of racial condition, F(8, 351) = 2.04, p = .042, partial-eta squared = .044, but no effect of model, F(2, 351) = 0.07, p = .933, and no interaction, F(16, 351) = 0.23, p = .999. However, the effect was not retained in a rank-transformed sensitivity analysis, F(8, 351) = 1.53, p = .145, and Tukey-corrected comparisons found no significant pairwise differences. An uncorrected European-Indigenous Australian comparison was significant, but was selected post hoc and is reported only as hypothesis-generating. Evidence for sentiment differences was therefore weak and analysis-dependent. Sentiment scoring also cannot distinguish evaluative bias from the valence of historical content elicited by a prompt. We outline design changes needed to address these limitations and argue for interdisciplinary development of behavioural measures of model bias.
  Keywords: Implicit Bias, Psychological Research Methods, Artificial Intelligence, ChatGPT, Large Language Models, Sentiment Analysis

</details>


### [26] [Wiring diagram extraction and gluing: a case study in classifying figure skating jumps using 3D dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27598)
*Jason Lo,Mohammadnima Jafari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Hasse聚类算法在聚类数量增加时因组合复杂度而变得不可行的问题，提出了一种"粘合接线图"理论，通过迭代应用Hasse聚类来获得与单次应用相同的结果，并在花样滑冰跳跃动作视频分类任务中验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hasse聚类算法能从序列数据中提取公共模式并以图形形式表示，但当期望聚类数量增长时，算法会因组合爆炸而变得不可行，限制了其在实际应用中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种粘合接线图（gluing wiring diagrams）的理论框架，使得Hasse聚类可以被迭代地应用，在多次迭代后达到与单次完整应用相同的聚类结果，从而规避组合复杂度问题。

Result: 在花样滑冰跳跃动作视频分类任务中对该理论进行了实验验证，证明了迭代式Hasse聚类方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 粘合接线图理论为Hasse聚类算法的可扩展性问题提供了一种理论解决方案，使得该算法能够通过分布式或迭代方式处理更大规模的聚类任务。

Abstract: Hasse clustering is an algorithm that extracts common patterns in sequential data and represents them in graphical forms. As the number of expected clusters grows, however, the algorithm can become infeasible to run due to combinatorial complexity. In this article, we describe a theory of gluing wiring diagrams, allowing iterative applications of Hasse clustering to achieve the same result as a single application. We test our theory in the context of classifying videos of figure skating jumps.

</details>


### [27] [World Action Planner: Generalizable Decision-Making with Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27599)
*Xiangcheng Zhang,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出World Action Planner，一种结合视觉语言模型推理能力与多任务姿态-图像条件世界模型的机器人规划系统，通过初始规划与迭代优化搜索在想象的世界模型推演中推理，在组合任务、新布局和零样本泛化场景中显著优于VLA和WAM等端到端策略模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建面向多样化应用的通用智能体仍是根本性挑战。基于模仿学习的策略虽然在特定训练环境中表现良好，但往往无法泛化到新场景和新任务，亟需一种能够打破这种局限的通用规划系统。

Method: 该系统利用VLM的推理能力生成初始动作计划，再通过多任务姿态-图像条件的世界模型进行物理接地，在想象的世界模型推演上迭代执行优化与搜索来精炼动作计划，实现物理可行且语义合理的规划。

Result: 在组合任务、新布局和零样本泛化场景中，该方法显著优于VLA（视觉语言动作模型）和WAM（世界动作模型）等最先进的端到端策略模型。

Conclusion: 将VLM的高层推理与经过物理接地的世界模型相结合，能够有效克服模仿学习策略的泛化局限，为构建通用机器人智能体提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Building generalizable agents for diverse applications remains a fundamental challenge. While imitation learning-based policies succeed in specific training environments, they often fail to generalize to novel scenes and tasks. In this work, we propose World Action Planner, a robot planning system that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and the physical grounding of a multi-task pose-image conditioned world model. Our system enables an agent to propose initial action plans and iteratively refine them via optimization and search, reasoning over imagined world model rollouts. We demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance across compositional tasks, new layouts, and zero-shot generalization scenarios, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art end-to-end policy models such as VLAs and WAMs. Project website at worldactionplanner.github.io

</details>


### [28] [CORE: In-Context Reconstruction for Unified Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27615)
*Yunfeng Zhao,Qingfeng Chen,Yue Tan,Shiyuan Li,Yili Wang,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE方法，通过去相关特征对齐模块和上下文重建模块，实现了统一表格异常检测（TAD），无需标签或合成异常即可在不同数据集上泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一TAD中，异构数据的对齐具有挑战性：现有方法依赖基于距离的统一特征构造，可能掩盖原始特征的语义；同时现有方法将异常检测视为二元分类任务，可能忽视不同数据集的多样异常模式，并被不具代表性的合成异常误导。

Method: CORE包含两个核心模块：（1）去相关特征对齐模块（decorrelated feature alignment module），将异构特征直接对齐到统一表示空间，保留其语义信息；（2）上下文重建模块（in-context reconstruction module），利用上下文正常样本来重建每个样本，以捕获数据集特定的分布，使重建误差反映样本偏离正常分布的程度。

Result: CORE将统一TAD形式化为上下文内重建问题，消除了对标注或合成异常样本的需求，重建误差可作为异常评分以实现对任意未见数据集的统一异常检测。

Conclusion: CORE通过保留语义的特征对齐和基于上下文正常样本的重建机制，解决了统一TAD中异构数据对齐困难和异常模式多样性问题，为无监督的统一表格异常检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection (TAD), which focuses on identifying abnormal samples that deviate from the majority in tabular data, has received growing attention. Recently, there has been an emerging trend towards unified TAD, which seeks to detect anomalies across different datasets using a single generalizable model. In unified TAD, aligning heterogeneous data remains challenging. While existing methods often rely on distance-based unified feature construction, they may obscure the semantics of the original features. Moreover, existing approaches typically formulate anomaly detection as a binary classification task, which may overlook diverse anomaly patterns from various datasets and be misled by unrepresentative synthetic anomalies. To address these challenges, we propose an in-COntext REconstruction approach for unified TAD (CORE for short). It introduces a decorrelated feature alignment module to directly align heterogeneous features into a unified representation space, which retains their semantic information. Meanwhile, CORE formulates unified TAD as an in-context reconstruction problem, eliminating the need for labeled or synthesized anomalies. Specifically, the in-context reconstruction module reconstructs each sample by leveraging contextual normal samples to capture dataset-specific distributions, such that reconstruction errors reflect its deviation from normality, facilitating unified TAD on arbitrary unseen datasets.

</details>


### [29] [Hidden APIs in Language Models: Discovering Reusable Causal Interfaces from Forked Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27617)
*SiYuan Ma,Yiqin Luo,Zhangji,Canran Xiao,Albert Gao,Wei-Hsing Huang,Wei Wang,Qiwei Wu,Xinran Li,Jinfeng Wei,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"分叉未来（forked futures）"方法，通过在未来操作采样前比较隐藏状态引发的响应分布，实证地建立隐藏状态间的因果商（causal quotient），并在此框架下比较四种内部接口（共享/局部/混合/分布式）。结果表明Shared接口在多个模型上具有最低的预序因果描述长度和最优的可重用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型内部表征探针仅通过"当前答案"来判断隐藏状态的一致性，但相同的答案可能由支持不同未来计算的隐藏状态产生，因此无法建立可重用的内部接口。需要一个无需研究者预设潜在标签、能够实证比较隐藏状态因果等价性的方法。

Method: 提出"forked futures"方法：在前缀状态形成后才采样未来操作，通过比较不同隐藏状态在相同未来操作下诱导的响应分布来对比状态。引入四种接口候选（Shared、Local、Mixture、Distributed），在预序因果描述长度（prequential causal description length）框架下进行竞争，约束条件包括未来签名保真度（future-signature fidelity）和匹配容量（matched capacity）。

Result: 在两个详细模型评估中，Shared接口的留出描述长度最低：Qwen2.5-1.5B上增益0.216 nats，Llama-3-8B上增益0.294 nats，同时保持紧密聚集的平均未来签名失真。五主干扫描保持Sharedness增益的正方向。移植分析中Shared联合最强（目标正确性、局部性、拷贝保持、复合画像），API对齐路径中介0.749的目标效应（对照空路径仅为0.150）。盲测四类模型-生物体测试恢复14/16架构，12个非Shared生物体中仅1个被误判为Shared。

Conclusion: 结果支持在测试的操作库内存在经济可重用的因果接口（Shared），但结论明确限定于候选架构、干预方式和留出未来操作这一范围内，不对更普遍的内部机制做无条件推广。

Abstract: Identical language-model answers can arise from hidden states that support different future computations, so current-answer probes do not establish a reusable internal interface. We introduce forked futures: future operations are sampled only after a prefix state has formed, and states are compared through the response distributions induced by those operations. This yields an empirical causal quotient over hidden states without requiring researcher-specified latent labels. Shared, Local, Mixture, and Distributed interfaces then compete under prequential causal description length subject to future-signature fidelity and matched capacity constraints. In the two detailed model evaluations, Shared has the lowest held-out description length, with gains of 0.216 nats on Qwen2.5-1.5B and 0.294 nats on Llama-3-8B, while maintaining tightly clustered mean future-signature distortion; a five-backbone sweep preserves the positive direction of Sharedness Gain. The figure-aligned transplantation analysis gives Shared the strongest joint target-correctness, locality, copy-preservation, and composite profile, and API-aligned paths mediate 0.749 of the target effect versus 0.150 for matched null paths. In the blind four-class model-organism test, 14/16 architectures are recovered, with one observed non-Shared to Shared error among 12 non-Shared organisms. These results support an economical reusable causal interface within the tested operation banks, while keeping the claim explicitly conditional on the candidate architectures, interventions, and held-out futures.

</details>


### [30] [SCOPE: Synthetic Conditional Objectives for Policy Evolution in Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27630)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Nguyen Huu Duc,Le Cong Bang,Tran Cong Dao,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE框架，通过学习多种合成条件目标来引导黑盒组合优化的策略演化，在有限评估预算下显著提升搜索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒组合优化中，未知目标函数无法为搜索方向提供有效指导，传统方法在有限评估预算下难以系统性地识别高质量解，需要一种机制来引导搜索探索方向。

Method: SCOPE包含双层循环：外层循环根据累积搜索历史自适应地更新和选择合成目标（每个目标设计为对候选解暴露不同的潜在偏好）；内层循环利用这些合成目标演化搜索策略，生成多样化候选解，并返回一组高性能策略的portfolio以降低依赖单一替代偏好的风险。

Result: 在多个基准问题上，SCOPE在有限评估预算下一致提升了黑盒搜索性能，并能很好地泛化到不同的组合结构。

Conclusion: SCOPE将目标设计重新定义为引导策略探索的机制，使搜索过程既能利用观测证据，又能在离散解空间中保持结构化多样性，为黑盒组合优化提供了有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box combinatorial optimization requires systematically identifying high-quality solutions under a limited evaluation budget, yet the unknown objective function provides little guidance for deciding where the search should explore next. We introduce SCOPE, a general framework for Synthetic Conditional Objectives for Policy Evolution in Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization. Rather than directly optimizing the inaccessible objective, SCOPE learns a set of synthetic objectives conditioned on the accumulated search history, where each objective is designed to expose a distinct and potentially useful preference over candidate solutions. These objectives are then used to evolve search policies that generate diverse candidates, whose true quality is subsequently assessed through black-box evaluations. The outer loop adaptively updates and selects synthetic objectives according to how effectively their induced policies discover promising regions. In contrast, the inner loop returns a portfolio of top-performing policies to reduce the risk of relying on a single surrogate preference. This formulation reframes objective design as a mechanism for guiding policy exploration, enabling the search process to exploit observed evidence while maintaining structured diversity across discrete solution spaces. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark problems demonstrate that SCOPE consistently improves black-box search performance under limited evaluation budgets and generalizes well across diverse combinatorial structures.

</details>


### [31] [ReDiPPO: Reference-Guided Value Calibration and Discrepancy-Aware Token Reweighting for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27631)
*Zhenrong Zhang,Fei Wu,Jun Du,Jianshu Zhang,Si Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDiPPO框架，通过参考引导的critic和差异感知的token级优势加权，提升PPO在数学推理任务中的价值估计精度和最终推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO在数学推理中依赖critic进行token级信用分配，但长推理轨迹和稀疏结果奖励导致标准critic难以准确评估中间推理状态，产生噪声优势估计和次优策略更新。

Method: ReDiPPO包含两个核心组件：1) 参考引导critic，利用参考答案作为训练时的特权信号提供更准确的价值估计；2) 同时保留标准critic，量化两者在token级别的参考-标准差异，将该差异作为困难推理状态的指示器，用于重新加权对应的token级优势。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上的实验表明，ReDiPPO提升了价值估计准确性，并在最终推理性能上一致优于PPO、DAPO和GSPO等强策略优化基线。

Conclusion: ReDiPPO通过引入参考答案作为特权信号并利用差异感知加权机制，有效解决了PPO在数学推理中token级信用分配不准确的问题，验证了该框架的优越性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for enhancing the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models. Among existing policy optimization methods, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remains particularly appealing because its learned critic can, in principle, provide token-level credit assignment. However, in mathematical reasoning tasks characterized by long reasoning horizons and sparse outcome rewards, reliable token-level credit assignment remains challenging. The standard critic often fails to accurately evaluate intermediate reasoning states, resulting in noisy advantage estimates and suboptimal policy updates. In this paper, we propose ReDiPPO, a Reference-guided and Discrepancy-aware PPO framework for mathematical reasoning. ReDiPPO introduces a reference-guided critic that uses reference answers as training-time privileged signals to provide more accurate value estimation. Meanwhile, it retains a standard critic and quantifies the token-level reference-standard discrepancy between the standard value estimate and the reference-guided value estimate. This discrepancy serves as an indicator of difficult reasoning states and is used to reweight the corresponding token-level advantages during PPO optimization. Extensive experiments on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ReDiPPO improves value-estimation accuracy and consistently outperforms strong policy optimization baselines, including PPO, DAPO, and GSPO, in final reasoning performance. Our code is available on https://github.com/cii030/ReDiPPO.

</details>


### [32] [HealthCAT: An Interpretable Encoder-only Transformer Framework for Health Indicator Prediction and Temporal Interpretation of Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27635)
*Xiaotong Yu,Joshua Y. Kim,HaeJin Lee,Kalina Yacef*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HealthCAT框架，将Encoder-only Transformer与Attentive Class Activation Token（AttentiveCAT）结合，在可穿戴传感器多变量时间序列数据上实现高精度预测的同时提供类特定的时间步级可解释性。在306名参与者的两个真实数据集上，HealthCAT在F1分数上最高提升17%，准确率提升12%（p<0.05），且其识别的时间步在掩码实验中比随机选择携带显著更多的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器持续捕获细粒度的多变量时间序列数据，为建模健康相关行为模式提供机会。然而，现有的深度学习方法过度追求预测准确性而忽视可解释性，限制了其在健康研究中的应用。研究者希望既能准确预测健康指标，又能揭示身体活动模式在何时以及如何发生，而非仅依赖聚合摘要指标。

Method: HealthCAT框架由两部分组成：（1）Encoder-only Transformer作为骨干架构处理多变量时间序列；（2）Attentive Class Activation Token（AttentiveCAT）机制生成类特定的、时间步级别的解释。这些解释可映射回领域相关的行为周期（如一天中的时间段），支持个体层面的可穿戴传感器数据分析。

Result: 在两个真实可穿戴传感器数据集（共306名参与者）上，HealthCAT相比深度学习基线在F1分数上最高提升17%，准确率提升12%（p<0.05）。掩码实验表明，HealthCAT识别的时间步在所有掩码条件下比随机选择携带显著更多的预测价值（p<0.05），证明其识别的时间步具有预测信息性。

Conclusion: HealthCAT将卓越的预测性能与经过验证的时间步级可解释性相结合，推动可穿戴传感器分析从聚合指标转向时间模式分析，支持健康监测、行为模式分析和干预设计。其核心贡献在于既能准确预测健康指标，又能提供关于身体活动模式何时及如何发生的深层洞见。

Abstract: Wearable sensors continuously capture fine-grained multivariate time-series data, providing opportunities to model behavioural patterns associated with health outcomes. However, existing deep learning methods prioritise predictive accuracy over interpretability, limiting their application in health research. In this study, we present HealthCAT, a flexible framework that integrates an Encoder-only Transformer with an Attentive Class Activation Token (AttentiveCAT) to generate class-specific, time-step-level interpretations. These interpretations can be mapped back onto behavioural cycles that are relevant to the domain (e.g., time-of-day), supporting individual-level analysis of wearable sensor data. We evaluated HealthCAT using two real-world wearable sensor datasets (306 participants in total). HealthCAT outperformed deep learning baselines by up to 17\% in F1-score and 12\% in accuracy on both datasets ($p<0.05$). In masking experiments, the time steps identified by HealthCAT carried significantly more predictive value than random selection across all masking conditions ($p<0.05$), indicating that the identified time steps are predictively informative. By coupling predictive performance with validated time-step-level interpretability, HealthCAT moves wearable sensor analysis beyond aggregated metrics towards temporal patterns that support health monitoring, behavioural pattern analysis, and intervention design in health research. The significance of this work is that it enables accurate prediction of health indicators from wearable sensor data while providing insights into when and how physical activity patterns occur, rather than relying solely on aggregated summary measures.

</details>


### [33] [HALO: Heterogeneous Admission through Localized Obligations for Safe Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27636)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Kiseok Kim,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HALO运行时协议，在异构Agent响应中仅保留其前置条件仍受支持的组件，在派发前重新检查每个精确动作，并允许受阻动作仅由新候选替换，在96项准入期望、20项协议测试、结构化响应回放及PX4/Gazebo冷启动实验中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent系统返回的异构响应可能同时包含通知、请求、交接和动作等组件，而条件在外部使用前可能发生变化，导致同一响应中的组件不再能同时得到支持。整体拒绝会丢弃可用组件，独立检查又可能留下缺少前置条件的依赖组件。需要一种既能保留支持组件又不会违反依赖关系的准入机制。

Method: 提出异构准入与局部义务（HALO）运行时协议：1) 保留声明了前置条件且该前置条件仍受支持的组件；2) 在派发前对每个精确动作进行重新检查；3) 受阻动作仅允许由全新的候选予以替换。

Result: HALO匹配全部96项准入期望并通过全部20项协议测试；在结构化响应回放中保留了248/248个受支持组件（含128/128个不受无关变更影响的组件），而整响应策略仅保留0/248；在十个PX4/Gazebo冷启动会话中，HALO阻止了所有测试的过期路由，未观察到匹配的过期设定点，并完成了所有新的恢复操作。

Conclusion: HALO在异构Agent响应准入方面显著优于整体拒绝策略，能在动态变化环境中有效保留有效组件、阻断过期组件，同时确保依赖完整性与动作安全性。

Abstract: Recent agentic AI systems may return a heterogeneous response containing notices, requests, handoffs, and actions. Conditions can change before external use, so components from the same response need not remain supported together. Rejecting the whole response discards useful components, whereas checking components independently can leave a dependent without its prerequisite. We present Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations (HALO), a runtime protocol that preserves supported components whose declared prerequisites also remain supported, rechecks each exact action before dispatch, and allows blocked actions to be replaced only by fresh candidates. HALO matched all 96 admission expectations and passed all 20 protocol tests. In structured-response replay, it retained 248/248 supported components, including 128/128 unaffected by unrelated changes, while a whole-response policy retained 0/248. Across ten cold-start PX4/Gazebo sessions, HALO blocked every tested stale route, observed no matching stale setpoint, and completed all fresh recoveries.

</details>


### [34] [Evaluating and Pricing Advertisements in AI-Generated Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27686)
*John L. Turner-Smith,Zimeng Huang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动答案引擎中的广告评测难题，提出基于心理学代理模拟框架构建监督信号，并将其蒸馏为参数高效的可微评估器，用以预测点击意图及三个广告质量维度；在此基础上推导出独特的支付规则定价机制，实现广告效用与商业价值的统一评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着搜索向LLM驱动的答案引擎转型，广告嵌入到生成回复中，需同时评估用户效用与商业价值。核心挑战在于点击意图信号缺失：行为日志不可得、人工标注难以校准、前沿LLM评判者混淆意图与语言流畅度。这些缺口相互叠加——合理的定价需要连续的意图信号，而生成该信号又需要当前不可用的监督数据，形成闭环难题。

Method: 作者通过心理学基础的代理模拟框架构建缺失的监督信号，并将其蒸馏为参数高效的评估器，输出点击意图及广告质量的三个伴随维度作为平滑、可微的估计值。在估计之上直接构建定价层，推导出使真实出价最优的唯一支付规则，并在best-of-k分配上验证，同时将机制扩展至非单调分配。

Result: 经由符号确定的行为扰动验证，该评估器在相关性敏感性上超越前沿零样本评判者（79%对60-67%），能追踪分级内容退化，对103种虚构产品实现零错误泛化，并在五位标注者的两两对比中达到86%的人类偏好一致率，且一致性随评估器置信度上升而提高。

Conclusion: 本文同时解决了监督信号缺失与定价机制设计两大难题，构建了从点击意图评估到广告定价再到广告生成的完整闭环。可微的意图信号不仅支撑了激励相容的支付规则，还可直接作为广告生成的训练目标。

Abstract: As search increasingly shifts toward LLM-driven answer engines, advertising is becoming embedded within the generated response itself and should therefore be evaluated for both user utility and commercial value. The key challenge is click-through intent: behavioural logs are unavailable, human annotation resists calibration, and frontier LLM judges conflate intent with linguistic fluency. These gaps compound, as principled pricing presupposes a continuous intent signal, while generating such a signal presupposes supervision that is currently unavailable. We construct the missing supervision through a psychologically grounded agent simulation framework, and distil it into a parameter-efficient evaluator that predicts click-through intent, together with the three companion dimensions of ad quality, as smooth, differentiable estimates. Validated through sign-certain behavioural perturbations, the evaluator surpasses frontier zero-shot judges on relevance sensitivity (79% versus 60-67%), tracks graded content degradation, generalises without error to 103 fictional products, and agrees with human preference in 86% of pairwise judgements across five annotators, with agreement rising in the evaluator's confidence. Upon its estimates we build the pricing layer directly, deriving the unique payment rule under which truthful bidding is optimal, demonstrating it on a best-of-k allocation, and extending the mechanism to non-monotone allocations. The same differentiable signal stands ready as a training objective for ad generation.

</details>


### [35] [Rehearse: Stepping Back from the Confidence Cliff in Self-Improving Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27687)
*Jiazhen Ji,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自动化研究（autoresearch）循环中"执行前判断"（pre-execution judgment）的可信度变化，发现随着成功的修改不断累积，智能体判断修改是否有效的选择性精度从82.8%骤降至56.9%，称为"信心悬崖"（confidence cliff）。提出的Rehearse方法通过聚焦的过往尝试记忆来缓解此问题，将后期选择性精度提升至83.5%，并在多个任务上以相同训练预算提升最终指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究的效率取决于智能体在花费训练资源之前判断一个修改是否可能有效的可靠性。然而，这种执行前判断的可靠性在整个autoresearch轨迹中如何变化尚不清楚，需要系统研究。

Method: 1) 分析公开AutoSOTA日志中修改成功率随迭代轮次的变化；2) 构建296对"同基线修改对"基准测试，屏蔽测量结果后让LLM法官判断；3) 提出Rehearse方法，核心是聚焦的过往相似尝试与结果记忆（focused outcome memory），在执行前比较多个候选想法并选择最有希望的方案运行。

Result: LLM法官在严格共识子集上达到79.5%准确率；但随着成功修改累积，选择性精度从82.8%降至56.9%（信心悬崖）。Rehearse的聚焦结果记忆将后期选择性精度提升至83.5%，在nanochat、图像分类、时间序列预测上以4000次预算训练运行实现了更好的终端指标。

Conclusion: 执行前判断的可靠性会在autoresearch循环后期显著衰退，这是阻碍自动化研究效率的关键瓶颈。通过记录并聚焦过去类似尝试与结果，可有效缓解这一"信心悬崖"问题，从而在相同训练预算下获得更好的最终性能。

Abstract: Autoresearch improves machine-learning code by proposing changes, running full training jobs, and keeping changes that improve the metric. The efficiency of this loop depends not only on generating ideas, but also on the agent's ability to decide, before spending a training run, whether a proposed modification is likely to work. We study how the reliability of this pre-execution judgment changes over the course of an autoresearch trajectory. In public AutoSOTA logs (Li et al., 2026; Tsinghua FIB Lab, 2026), the fraction of helpful modifications falls from 70% in the first two iterations to 43% by iteration 6+. On 296 same-baseline modification pairs from 39 paper-derived AutoSOTA tasks, each containing one modification that improved the metric and one that did not, with measured outcomes hidden, an LLM judge given candidate rationales but no prior-attempt history reaches 79.5% accuracy on the pairs where strict consensus returns a verdict. On the full 366-pair benchmark, however, this ability weakens substantially late in the loop. As successful changes accumulate, selective accuracy - accuracy conditioned on a strict-consensus verdict - falls from 82.8% to 56.9%, while the judge remains willing to decide. We call this operational pattern the confidence cliff. Rehearse implements the loop change as a lightweight skill for autoresearch loops: propose several ideas, compare them before execution, run the most promising, and judge with a focused memory of similar past attempts and outcomes. This focused outcome memory raises late selective accuracy to 83.5%. Across 4,000 budgeted training runs over three loops, Rehearse improves the endpoint under the same training-run budget on nanochat, image classification, and time-series forecasting.

</details>

*Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种从GP进化启发式规则向LLM决策者反向传递调度知识的方法，通过四种注入机制（特征选择、特征提示、规则参考、规则遵循）引导在线LLM进行动态多模式项目调度决策，实验表明GP引导普遍能改善无引导的LLM性能，其中特征选择最具token效率，规则遵循性能最强但token消耗更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-GP混合方法大多利用LLM辅助启发式规则的进化，而本文探索反向方向：将GP进化出的高质量规则中提取的知识注入LLM在线决策，以弥补零样本LLM决策缺乏领域知识、token消耗大、重复查询结果不稳定等缺陷。

Method: 从高质量GP规则中提取调度知识，通过四种机制注入LLM决策器：特征选择（简化决策上下文）、特征提示（强调重要特征）、规则参考（提供决策逻辑参考）和规则遵循（直接执行显式决策规则）。在动态多模式项目调度场景下评估其调度性能、token消耗、决策稳定性和生成理由中的特征关注。

Result: GP引导普遍提升了无引导LLM的调度性能，但知识表示形式很关键：简化决策上下文或提供显式决策逻辑比仅突出重要特征更有效。特征选择在token效率上最优，规则遵循以更高的token成本获得强性能。引导同时改善了决策稳定性，并改变了生成理由中表达的特征关注。

Conclusion: GP进化的启发式规则可以有效引导LLM在线决策，但知识注入的表示形式对效果影响显著。该工作为LLM与GP的交叉融合提供了新的方向，即利用进化算法的知识改进LLM决策，而非仅限于LLM辅助进化。

Abstract: In dynamic multi-mode project scheduling, activities have alternative execution modes and uncertain durations, while precedence relations and limited resources constrain their execution. Heuristic priority rules support fast online decisions, but their design requires substantial domain expertise. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics can automatically evolve such rules. Large language models (LLMs), meanwhile, provide a flexible interface for interpreting scheduling information and explaining decisions. However, zero-shot LLM decisions may lack domain knowledge, consume many tokens, and vary across repeated queries. GP-evolved rules therefore provide a potential source of scheduling knowledge for guiding LLM decisions. Unlike existing LLM--GP hybrids that use LLMs to support heuristic evolution, we transfer knowledge in the reverse direction, using knowledge extracted from high-quality GP rules to guide an online LLM decision maker. We extract knowledge from high-quality GP rules and inject it through Feature Selection, Feature Hint, Rule Reference, and Rule Follow. These mechanisms are evaluated in terms of scheduling performance, token consumption, decision stability, and the feature focus expressed in generated rationales. GP-derived guidance generally improves the unguided LLM, but its representation matters. Simplifying the decision context or supplying explicit decision logic is more effective than highlighting important features. Feature Selection offers the best token efficiency, whereas Rule Follow achieves strong performance at greater token cost. Guidance also improves decision stability and changes the features expressed in generated rationales.

</details>


### [37] [SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27703)
*Yang Zhou,Zixuan Huang,Sunzhu Li,Zhuo Yang,Chen Zhang,Shunian Chen,Caijun Yan,Jianyao Xu,Shunyu Liu,Weijie Fu,Peiliang Li,Xiaozhi Chen,Yuxiang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialCLI框架，通过三阶段流程（调用空间工具、冷启动SFT+智能体强化学习学习工具使用、内化工具能力）教会视觉语言模型使用专业空间视觉工具并逐步内化其感知能力，解决通用VLM能推理整体任务但缺乏视觉细节、专用视觉模型能捕捉细节但无法做任务级决策的鸿沟。在MindCube基准上将Qwen3-VL-8B从29.3%提升至84.6%（带工具），超越GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%），内化后不带工具仍达73.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（VLM）在具身智能体中可以推理整体任务语义，但常常遗漏决定成败的视觉细节；而专用视觉模型能精确捕捉这些细节（如分割、深度、位姿）却无法将其转化为任务级决策。两者之间存在根本性的能力鸿沟，现有的简单工具调用或提示工程无法弥合这一差距。

Method: SpatialCLI包含三个阶段：(1) Call——将专业视觉模型作为空间工具暴露给VLM，以增强其感知能力；(2) Learn——使用冷启动监督微调（Cold-Start SFT）和智能体强化学习（agentic RL）改进工具使用能力；(3) Internalize——将成功的工具使用轨迹文字化，让VLM逐步内化专业感知能力。此外还构建了SpatialCLI-Bench基准，包含516个涵盖定位、分割、深度和位姿的合成感知任务样例。

Result: 在MindCube基准上，SpatialCLI将Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升至84.6%（带工具），超过了GPT-5.6 Sol的72.1%（同样带工具）；内化阶段后，不带工具时仍保持73.8%的准确率。

Conclusion: SpatialCLI验证了通过"外部调用工具→学习工具使用→内化工具能力"的三阶段范式可以系统性地弥合VLM与专业视觉模型之间的能力鸿沟，既实现了工具辅助下的高性能，又通过内化过程保留了无工具情况下的能力迁移，为具身智能体中的感知-推理协作提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in embodied agents to interpret visual inputs, reason about spatial relationships, and make task-level decisions based on that reasoning. However, a fundamental capability mismatch remains: general VLMs can reason about the overall task but often miss the visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models can capture those details but cannot translate them into task-level decisions. In this work, we propose SpatialCLI, a framework that teaches VLMs to reason with spatial tools and progressively internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities they provide. SpatialCLI proceeds in three stages: (1) Call exposes specialist vision models as spatial tools to augment the VLM's perception; (2) Learn uses Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL to improve tool use; and (3) Internalize verbalizes successful tool-use trajectories to internalize specialist perceptual capabilities. We further introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools (72.1%), while retaining 73.8% without tools after internalization.

</details>

*Ting Gong,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Yong Yang*

TL;DR: Albilich是一个开源的数学自动研究智能体框架，结合长程推理、计算机代数系统（CAS）、文献检索和基于SQLite的持久上下文管理。在RealMath基准上实现10/10（启用CAS）和9/10（无CAS）的解决率，并在Kourovka笔记本的群论开放问题中产出了一个反例和一条定理的加强证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学研究中能提供有价值的想法，但长程证明尝试在协调、评估和复现方面存在困难。作者希望构建一个可复现、可评估且人类可操控的自动化数学研究环境。

Method: 构建Albilich开源智能体框架，集成四方面能力：长程推理（long-horizon reasoning）、计算机代数系统（CAS）、文献检索以及基于SQLite的持久化上下文管理。评估使用RealMath基准和Kourovka笔记本（群论开放问题），并通过消融实验验证各组件的贡献。

Result: 在RealMath上CAS开启时10/10全部解决，无CAS时9/10解决。在Kourovka问题上解决Problem 21.142（产出一个反例）并对Problem 20.2给出加强版的证明。消融实验显示：Problem 17.91上启用CAS可减少32.0%的token消耗；Problem 21.142上缺少advisor agent时验证器拒绝率更高且无法合成证明路线。

Conclusion: Albilich证明了CAS增强的智能体框架能够有效地辅助数学研究，具备人类可操控性、可扩展性和可复现性，为大规模AI辅助数学研究提供了可行的环境基础。

Abstract: Large language models can contribute useful ideas to mathematical research, yet long-horizon proof attempts remain difficult to coordinate, evaluate, and reproduce. We present Albilich, an open-source agentic harness for autoresearch in mathematics that combines long-horizon reasoning, computer algebra systems (CAS), literature retrieval, and persistent SQLite-based context management.
  We evaluate Albilich on the RealMath benchmark (Zhang et al. 2025) and on open problems in group theory from the Kourovka Notebook (Khukhro and Mazurov 2026). It solved 10/10 problems on RealMath with CAS and 9/10 with no CAS. On the Kourovka problems, Albilich produced a counterexample to Problem 21.142 and a proof of a strengthening of Problem20.2. Anablation on Problem 17.91 demonstrates 32.0% token reduction when CAS is enabled. An ablation on Problem 21.142 demonstrates higher verifier-rejection rate and failure to synthesize proof routes in the absence of the advisor agent. These results support Albilich as a human-steerable, CAS-boosted environment for scalable AI-assisted mathematical research.

</details>


### [39] [MECA: A Mechanism-Centered Agent for Constructing Well-Specified and Valuable Mathematical Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27709)
*Wentao Long,Yunfei Zhang,Chenyi Li,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MECA，一个以机制为中心的多智能体框架，用于自动构造数学猜想。通过探索者智能体提出机制、测试应用并修订猜想，评论者智能体评估数学有效性和研究价值，将宽泛研究方向转化为精确且有实质支持的猜想。实验表明MECA能生成当前自动证明器难以解决的、定义良好且有研究价值的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动构造定义良好且有价值的数学猜想仍是AI辅助数学发现的核心挑战。现有的开放问题往往过于宽泛、定义不明确，难以与可行的证明或反驳策略联系起来，因此需要一种新的方法来生成精确且可操作的猜想。

Method: 提出MECA多智能体框架，将数学机制定义为连接假设与结论的结构或推理原理（如不等式、不变量、分解或归约到中间命题）。探索者智能体提出机制、测试其适用性并相应修订候选猜想，评论者智能体评估数学有效性与研究价值，反馈驱动对猜想假设、范围和结论的调整。评估采用两种补充设置：一是基于目标条件但文章盲的源材料重构预设目标论文结论，与生成-修订基线对比；二是从文献种子和现有开放问题构建100个半开放问题，通过独立自动化证明器和反证尝试评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，以机制为中心的细化能生成定义良好且有研究价值的猜想，且这些猜想对当前自动化证明器仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 机制中心化的方法有效地将宽泛研究方向转化为精确猜想，同时保留明确识别的未解决核心，为AI辅助数学猜想发现提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Automatically constructing well-specified and valuable mathematical conjectures remains a central challenge in AI-assisted mathematical discovery. Many existing open problems and conjectures are often too broad, underspecified, or difficult to connect to plausible proof or refutation strategies. We view a mathematical mechanism as a structure or reasoning principle that connects the assumptions of a candidate problem to its target conclusion, such as an inequality, invariant, decomposition, or reduction to an intermediate claim. We present MECA (MEchanism-centered Conjecture Agent), a multi-agent framework that constructs conjectures by jointly developing candidate statements and their supporting mechanisms. Explorer agents propose mechanisms, test how they apply, and revise the candidate conjecture accordingly, while critic agents assess their mathematical validity and research value. Their feedback guides changes to the assumptions, scope, and conclusion. Through this process, MECA transforms broad research directions into precise conjectures with substantive mathematical support while retaining a clearly identified unresolved core. We evaluate MECA in two complementary settings. First, we compare it with a generate-and-revise baseline on reconstructing preselected target-paper conclusions from target-conditioned but article-blind source materials. Second, we construct 100 semi-open problems from literature-derived seeds and existing open problems and evaluate them through independent proof and refutation attempts by automated provers. Our results indicate that mechanism-centered refinement produces well-specified and research-worthy conjectures that remain challenging for current automated provers.

</details>


### [40] [New Synchronous Computation Dynamics for Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27720)
*Francisco Requena-Domínguez,Rafaela Benítez-Rochel,Ezequiel López-Rubio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散微分滤波器（DDF）的同步动力学SD-DDF，用于霍普菲尔德网络，允许一次更新一个或多个神经元，以最大化每步能量下降量，从而缩短总处理时间并保证收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Hopfield网络的动力学是异步（顺序）的，每步只更新一个神经元，处理时间较长。为减少处理时间，需要一种新的动力学机制，允许同时更新多个神经元，同时保证收敛和最大化能量下降。

Method: 提出名为离散微分滤波器（DDF）的新工具，用于求解在同步动力学下如何从当前状态找到能量下降最多的下一个网络状态这一组合优化问题；基于DDF开发新的同步动力学SD-DDF，并提供理论依据。

Result: 通过四个计算实验评估SD-DDF在处理时间上的加速效果，实证验证了该方法能有效缩短总处理时间。

Conclusion: SD-DDF作为一种新的同步动力学，通过每步最大化能量下降并允许多神经元同时更新，在保证收敛的同时显著缩短了Hopfield网络的处理时间。

Abstract: The dynamics of the original Hopfield network is asynchronous (sequential) (updates the state of only one neuron per time step). In this paper, we propose a new tool and a new dynamics to reduce the processing time by updating one or more neurons simultaneously per instant while ensuring process convergence and aiming for the maximum energy decrease at each step, thus guaranteeing the shortest total processing time. From the point of view of synchronous dynamics, calculating the next network state at which energy decreases the most from the current state while ensuring convergence is itself a combinatorial optimization problem. We develop and use a new tool to solve it. We call this new tool Discrete Differential Filter (DDF) and, based upon it, we develop a new synchronous dynamics which we call SD-DDF (Synchronous Dynamics based upon Discrete Differential Filter). In this paper, we review the original asynchronous dynamics for Hopfield networks and present a new tool and a new synchronous dynamics with its theoretical justification and four computational experiments to assess the speed up in processing time empirically.

</details>


### [41] [Baikal: Structured Search for Deep Research over Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27726)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Rishitha Guttapalle Mohan,Aarti Kumari,Ashi Sinha,Athulya Anil,Kavitha Srinivas,Horst Samulowitz,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Baikal框架，将数据湖上的深度研究建模为预算受限的搜索问题。通过将异构证据聚类为语义区域，并采用自适应搜索策略（如贝叶斯ε-greedy和UCB）平衡探索与利用。实验在HybridQA和TAT-QA数据湖上进行，最佳配置相比最强基线分别提升报告得分28%和36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用迭代检索与生成的范式，让累积的上下文决定下一步调研内容，这会导致对局部有前景证据的过度利用（overexploitation），在固定预算下无法覆盖不同的语义区域。

Method: 1) 将数据湖上的深度研究形式化为预算受限的搜索问题；2) Baikal将异构证据聚类为语义区域；3) 在每个选中的区域内生成并调研区域相关的子问题；4) 以发现质量作为奖励更新区域价值估计；5) 支持随机、LLM引导、贝叶斯ε-greedy和UCB等多种区域选择策略；6) 提出包含groundedness、relevance、diversity和utility四维度的新评分标准。

Result: 在HybridQA（10,993个表+227K Wikipedia段落）和TAT-QA（2,757个表+13K金融报告段落）两个数据湖上，各用15个查询进行评估。Baikal在多种区域选择策略下表现强劲，最佳配置在HybridQA上比最强基线提升28%，在TAT-QA上提升36%。消融分析表明收益源自对语义证据区域的组织与探索，改善了groundedness和diversity，在相同子问题预算下产出更有用的发现。

Conclusion: 结构化语义探索对于异构数据湖上的系统性研究和发现具有重要价值，Baikal框架有效解决了深度研究中探索与利用的平衡问题。

Abstract: Deep research over data lakes requires an LLM agent to investigate evidence across thousands of heterogeneous tables and passages to synthesize a report. Existing methods perform iterative retrieval and generation, letting accumulated context determine what to investigate next, which can overexploit locally promising evidence and fail to cover distinct semantic regions under a fixed budget. To address this, we cast deep research over data lakes as a budgeted search problem and present Baikal - a framework that clusters heterogeneous evidence into semantic regions, then searches over them adaptively to balance exploration and exploitation. Within each selected region, Baikal generates and investigates region-grounded subquestions, using finding quality as rewards to update region-level value estimates and guide search under policies ranging from random and LLM-guided selection to Bayesian $ε$-greedy and UCB. We evaluate Baikal on 15 queries each over HybridQA and TAT-QA data lakes containing 10,993 and 2,757 tables, respectively, together with 227K Wikipedia passages and 13K financial report passages. We assess research quality with a new rubric covering groundedness, relevance, diversity, and utility, and use GPT-5-mini to score Baikal and strong baselines, including DeepSearcher and an OpenCode research agent with retrieval and clustering variants. Across both data lakes, Baikal performs strongly under several region-selection policies; its best configuration improves report scores over the strongest baselines by 28% on HybridQA and 36% on TAT-QA. Our analyses attribute these gains to organizing and exploring semantic evidence regions, which improves groundedness and diversity and yields more useful findings under the same subquestion budget. These results demonstrate the value of structured semantic exploration for systematic research and discovery over heterogeneous data lakes.

</details>


### [42] [VeriSkill: A Self-Evolution Framework for Program Verification Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27733)
*Changguo Jia,Tianqi Zhao,Zhiyou Xiao,Weiming Zhang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeriSkill，一个面向程序验证的智能体技能自进化框架，通过将验证失败归因于技能缺陷、提炼可复用经验、并仅接受能提升验证性能且保持程序语义的技能修订，在多种验证工具、智能体框架和大语言模型后端上持续超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用LLM智能体自动化程序验证需要可复用的技能（如生成规范、注释、辅助引理和工具调用），然而现有的技能自进化方法在程序验证任务中效果不佳，因为它们无法可靠地识别技能特定的失败原因，也无法从不透明的验证器反馈中提取可操作的信号。

Method: VeriSkill是一个自进化框架，核心包含三个环节：(1) 将验证失败归因到具体的技能缺陷；(2) 将诊断性签名提炼为可复用的经验教训；(3) 迭代优化候选技能，且仅接受能提升验证性能并保持程序语义的修订。

Result: 实验表明，VeriSkill在多种验证工具、多种智能体框架和多种LLM后端条件下，性能一致地优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将失败归因与保守式技能演化相结合，VeriSkill有效解决了程序验证中技能进化面临的可归因性和可操作反馈缺失两大难题，验证了技能自我进化在程序验证场景中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Automating program verification with LLM agents requires generating specifications, annotations, auxiliary lemmas, and tool invocations, all of which depend on reusable skills. A natural remedy is skill self-evolution: distilling skills from trajectories and refining them through feedback. However, existing evolution methods struggle with program verification tasks because they cannot reliably identify skill-specific failures or extract actionable signals from opaque verifier feedback. In this paper, we propose VeriSkill, a self-evolution framework built for program verification. It attributes verification failures to skill deficiencies, distills diagnostic signatures into reusable lessons, and iteratively refines candidate skills, admitting only revisions that improve verification performance while preserving program semantics. Experiments show that VeriSkill consistently outperforms all baselines across multiple verification tools, agent frameworks, and LLM backends.

</details>


### [43] [SpecCal: Ambiguity-Aware Candidate Calibration for Infrared Spectrum-Based Molecular Structure Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27788)
*Yixuan Chen,Bo Liu,Yusen Tan,Guokun Yang,Wenjie Du,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecCal是一个无需训练的候选校准框架，用于红外光谱到分子结构预测。它通过重新排序现有候选分子并引入光谱一致性引导的结构替代方案，提升预测集质量，且无需微调即可与多种基础模型集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外光谱到分子结构推断具有本质上的模糊性——不同分子可能共享相似的官能团和局部振动模式，导致光谱响应高度相似。现有IR-to-molecule模型虽能生成候选分子集合，但该集合主要由模型的生成偏好决定，未必能充分覆盖满足光谱约束的最优结构。

Method: SpecCal框架是即插即用、模型无关的候选校准方法。它在现有基础模型的候选输出之上运作，通过两部分改进预测集：(1) 对当前候选分子重新排序；(2) 基于光谱一致性引入额外的结构上合理的替代候选分子。整个过程无需参数更新。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SpecCal在不同基础模型上均能一致提升SMILES层面和骨架（scaffold）层面的top-k重建准确率。

Conclusion: 在光谱模糊性条件下校准候选集是为提升IR光谱分子重建提供了一条实用且有效的途径，验证了候选校准策略对IR-to-molecule任务的通用价值。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from infrared (IR) spectra is a fundamental yet challenging problem. A key difficulty is that an IR spectrum provides limited structural information: different molecules may share similar functional groups and local vibrational patterns, leading to highly similar spectral responses. Thus, even when an observed spectrum has a unique underlying structure, reconstructing it from the spectrum remains ambiguous. Existing IR-to-molecule models usually generate a ranked set of candidate molecules, but this set is largely determined by the model's learned generation preference and may not fully capture the structures that best satisfy the observed spectral constraints. To address this limitation, we propose SpecCal, a training-free candidate calibration framework for IR-to-molecule prediction. SpecCal operates on the candidate outputs of existing base models and improves the prediction set by re-ranking current candidates while introducing additional structurally plausible alternatives guided by spectral consistency. The framework is plug-and-play and model-agnostic, requiring no parameter updates for integration with diverse base models. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SpecCal consistently improves top-k reconstruction at both SMILES and scaffold levels across different base models. Further analyses demonstrate that calibrating candidate sets under spectral ambiguity provides a practical way to improve molecular reconstruction from IR spectra. The code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecCal-B18A.

</details>

*Subinay Adhikary,Dwaipayan Roy,Debasis Ganguly,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDA系统，一个用于法律文献数据标注的基于Web的通用标注平台，核心功能是允许注释者动态创建新标签来标注法律案件中的概念或实体，从而将法律文档从平铺的bag-of-words表示转化为结构化、有语义的概念袋表示，可用于先例检索、判决预测等下游任务。该系统已在印度最高法院案件诉讼的标注任务中得到实际应用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文档采用结构化表示（而非简单的bag-of-words平坦文本表示）可以显著提升处理能力，因此需要从法律案件诉讼中识别和标注概念或主题，但目前缺乏支持动态创建标注标签的通用标注系统，尤其在不存在预定义本体的情况下，概念需要由注释者在检查文档过程中自行发现。

Method: 设计并实现LeDA（Legal Data Annotation）系统：1）提供基于Web界面的通用标注和裁决功能；2）核心创新在于允许动态创建新标签用于标注，适用于没有预定义本体、需由注释者自行发现概念的场景；3）系统构建文档的"概念袋"语义表示，支持多个下游任务。论文还描述了3位评估人员使用LeDA对印度最高法院案件诉讼的法律概念名称进行标注和裁决的过程。

Result: LeDA系统已成功应用于标注一组来自法律文档的概念，构建了文档的概念袋语义表示；同时通过3位评估人员对印度最高法院案件诉讼的实践应用，验证了系统在无预定义本体情况下动态标注法律概念的有效性。

Conclusion: LeDA提供了一个灵活、通用的法律数据标注平台，其动态标签创建功能特别适合概念本体尚未明确、需要标注者在标注过程中逐步发现和定义的场景。构建的概念袋表示可用于先例检索、判决预测等多个下游法律处理任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we mainly concentrate on finding concepts or topics from the legal case proceedings, since adopting a structured representation for legal documents, as opposed to a mere bag-of-words flat text representation, can significantly enhance processing capabilities. To achieve this objective, we put forward a set of diverse concepts for legal case proceedings. With this motivation, we propose LeDA, a system for Legal Data Annotation. The system offers the generic functionality of annotating and adjudicating entities or concepts within documents via a web-based interface. A novel feature of our system is that it allows to dynamic create new tags for annotation, which is a particularly useful provision for situations where there exists no pre-defined ontology for the entities (concepts) that need to be annotated - these being rather discovered by annotators as they continue examining more documents. The system that we demonstrate is currently in use to annotate a set of concepts from legal documents to construct semantic representations of documents as bags of concepts that can then be used for several downstream tasks, such as prior case retrieval, judgment prediction, and so on. Along with the system features in general, we also describe how LeDA was used by 3 assessors to annotate and adjudicate legal concept names from Indian Supreme Court case proceedings.

</details>


### [45] [MemeBench: What LVLMs Miss When Interpreting Culture-Dependent Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27798)
*Weihang Wang,Kainan Tu,Jielei Zhang,Run Yang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Yu Xie,Pengyu Chen,Peiyi Li,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemeBench，一个包含 1,253 个中英文梗图的诊断性基准测试，采用 VIKR 标注架构（视觉线索、身份链接、知识单元、推理机制）对多模态大模型的解释能力进行细粒度诊断；并提出基于实体引导检索的 KAR 基线，通过外部知识库 CultureBase 弥补模型在梗图理解中的"视觉-知识鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型虽能准确描述视觉内容，但当语义解读依赖像素之外的文化背景知识时，准确描述并不等于正确理解。现有梗图基准大多将理解简化为标签或整体评分，无法定位解释失败的环节，缺乏对模型中知识缺口进行细粒度诊断的能力。

Method: 构建 MemeBench 基准，包含 1,253 个中英文梗图及人工撰写的参考解释，配以质量控制下的 VIKR 四维标注架构（视觉线索 Visual clues、身份链接 Identity links、知识单元 Knowledge units、推理机制 Reasoning mechanisms），聚焦动漫、漫画、游戏及周边网络亚文化。在此基础上提出 KAR（实体引导检索），基于 CultureBase 知识库为模型提供针对性外部证据。

Result: 在 26 个 LVLM 上评测发现：所有模型对可见内容的覆盖均优于解读所需的知识覆盖；最强模型仍存在 22.6% 的视觉-知识差距。KAR 在四个受控模型上使 VIKR 成功率提升 3.6-7.4%，相比通用检索能修复更多答案且破坏更少。但两种检索条件均改善了身份链接与知识单元，同时降低了每个对比中的视觉覆盖。

Conclusion: MemeBench 不仅能揭示解释是否成功，还能指出缺失的具体环节，并检验针对性证据能否填补诊断出的知识缺口，为梗图理解提供了可诊断、可改进的基准框架。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have improved at describing visual content, but accurate descriptions do not ensure interpretation when meaning depends on knowledge beyond the pixels. Memes expose this gap because they rely on cultural entities, background knowledge, and community conventions. Most meme benchmarks reduce interpretation to labels or holistic scores, obscuring where an explanation breaks down. We introduce MemeBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,253 Chinese and English memes with human-written references and quality-controlled VIKR annotations, centered on anime, comics, games, and adjacent online subcultures. Its VIKR schema decomposes explanations into Visual clues, Identity links, Knowledge units, and Reasoning mechanisms. Across 26 LVLMs, every model covers visible content more reliably than the knowledge needed to interpret it, and even the strongest retains a 22.6% Visual-Knowledge gap. To test whether this diagnosis can guide improvement, we introduce KAR, an entity-guided retrieval baseline built on CultureBase. Across four controlled models, KAR raises VIKR Success by 3.6-7.4% and, compared with generic retrieval, repairs more answers and breaks fewer. Yet both retrieval conditions improve Identity and Knowledge while reducing Visual coverage in every comparison. MemeBench reveals whether an interpretation succeeds, what is missing, and whether targeted evidence fills the diagnosed gap.

</details>


### [46] [STEREODISCO: Discovering Stereotypicality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27824)
*Farane Jalali Farahani,Corina Dima,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Raphael H. Heiberger,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEREODISCO框架，通过将语义区分法（semantic differential method）适配到LLM内部表征，系统性地发现和验证大语言模型中编码的刻板印象语义轴。应用在LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT和MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT上，发现两个LLM对人类群体评分的共识度高于与人类的一致性，并发现了先前研究未涉及的刻板印象轴（如谦逊vs.骄傲、狭隘vs.开阔、怯懦vs.勇敢），这些发现得到了人类标注者的独立确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM中刻板印象的计算研究仅聚焦于社会心理学中少量已知的语义轴（如性别、种族），且多基于词嵌入而非模型内部表征。这留下了两个关键空白：LLM中还有哪些其他语义轴承载刻板印象关联？LLM内部如何表征这些轴？本文旨在系统性地回答这些问题。

Method: STEREODISCO框架包含三个步骤：(1) 从WordNet反义词集构建约2,000个候选语义轴；(2) 通过探针（probing）技术将每个候选轴恢复为LLM激活空间中的几何轴；(3) 通过对概念投影进行统计检验来识别具有刻板印象属性的语义轴。案例研究使用LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT和MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT两个模型。

Result: 两个LLM在社交群体评分上的相互一致性高于与人类评分的一致性，表明LLM编码的刻板印象内容与社会心理学文档存在分歧。同时发现了先前工作未探索的刻板印象轴，包括谦逊vs.骄傲、狭隘vs.开阔、怯懦vs.勇敢，这些新发现的轴获得了人类标注者的独立确认。

Conclusion: STEREODISCO提供了一种可扩展的系统性方法，用于揭示LLM内部表征中隐藏的刻板印象语义轴。研究结果表明LLM的刻板印象内容与人类的刻板印象存在系统性差异，且存在大量未被探索的刻板印象维度，这对评估和缓解LLM偏见具有重要启示。

Abstract: LLMs encode, convey, and perpetuate stereotypes. Prior computational research focuses on a small set of semantic axes investigated in social psychology, and operates on word embeddings produced by language models, leaving open which other semantic axes carry stereotypical associations in LLMs and how LLMs internally represent such axes. We introduce STEREODISCO, a framework that adapts the semantic differential method (Osgood et al., 1957) to the systematic study of stereotypes in LLM internal representations. STEREODISCO constructs approx. 2,000 candidate semantic axes from WordNet antonym synsets, recovers each as a geometric axis in the LLM's activation space via probing, and identifies stereotypical axes via a statistical test over concept projections. As a case study, we apply STEREODISCO to social group stereotypes with LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT and MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT. We find that the two LLMs agree with each other on social group ratings more than with humans, suggesting that LLM-encoded stereotype content diverges from that documented in social psychology. We also discover stereotypical axes not investigated in prior work -- including humble vs. proud, narrow-minded vs. broad-minded, and cowardly vs. brave, which human annotators independently confirm.

</details>


### [47] [Sign Language Question Answering: A New Task, Benchmark, and Baseline for Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27826)
*Shiwei Gan,Lichen Wang,Xiao Liu,Yafeng Yin,Kuizhuang Liu,Sanglu Lu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出手语问答（SLQA）任务，构建了基于PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily的SignQA基准数据集，并提出了一种带问题条件调制时间下采样模块和域内知识迁移策略的基线模型，在手语视频的任意自然语言问答上显著优于现有视觉-语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解（SLU）任务（如连续手语识别和手语翻译）都基于预定义目标，模型只需学习从手语视频到词汇或句子的固定映射，无法全面评估模型是否真正理解手语视频的语义内容。因此需要一个更灵活、更全面的评估框架来测试模型的多重推理能力。

Method: （1）提出SLQA新任务，要求模型回答关于手语视频的任意自然语言问题；（2）基于PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily，利用精心设计的模板从已有的gloss和句子标注中自动生成问答对，构建两个SignQA基准，覆盖五个问题类别：位置推理、结构推理、视觉搜索、gloss识别和翻译理解；（3）提出基线模型，包含Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling（问题条件调制时间下采样）模块和域内知识迁移策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，该基线模型在全部问题类别上均稳定优于代表性的视觉-语言模型，为SLQA的未来研究建立了强基准。数据集已公开在Hugging Face上。

Conclusion: SLQA作为新的手语理解评估范式，超越了传统识别与翻译任务的局限性，提供了更全面的手语语义理解评估。所提出的基线和数据集为后续研究工作奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language (SL) understanding (SLU) have led to remarkable progress in tasks such as continuous SL recognition and SL translation. However, these tasks are designed with predefined objectives, requiring models to learn a fixed mapping from sign videos to glosses or spoken-language sentences. As a result, they provide only a limited assessment of whether a model truly understands the semantic content of SL videos. To address this limitation, \textbf{we first propose a new task, Sign Language Question Answering (SLQA)}, which evaluates SL understanding by requiring models to answer arbitrary natural language questions about SL videos. Unlike previous SLU tasks, SLQA provides a more flexible and comprehensive evaluation framework that assesses multiple reasoning capabilities beyond recognition and translation. To facilitate this task, \textbf{we further construct two SignQA benchmarks} based on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily by automatically generating question-answer pairs from existing gloss and sentence annotations using carefully designed templates. The resulting datasets cover five complementary question categories, including position reasoning, structural reasoning, visual search, gloss recognition, and translation understanding. \textbf{Finally, we propose a simple yet effective baseline model} equipped with a Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling module and an in-domain knowledge transfer strategy, enabling effective knowledge transfer from existing SLU tasks while enhancing question-aware temporal feature modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our baseline consistently outperforms representative vision-language models across all question categories, establishing a strong benchmark for future research on SLQA. Datasets are available at:{https://huggingface.co/datasets/hulala/SignQA-2026}.

</details>


### [48] [MemTxn: A Transaction Boundary for Source-Supported Updates and Complete-State Recovery in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27834)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Fanshuai Meng,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemTxn是一个位于答案模型之外的治理层，为持久内存提供事务边界，支持可验证的更新、冲突解决和故障恢复，在多基准测试中显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久内存让长期运行的LLM Agent能够跨会话和任务复用信息，但可写内存中的错误会持续存在并污染后续行为。现有系统只改进存储和检索，缺少可靠更新和恢复的事务边界。

Method: MemTxn包含三个核心组件：1) Ordered PatchTest用于验证写入是否被其来源支持；2) Temporal Resolver用于在事实冲突时选择可见版本；3) 持久快照日志用于故障后恢复应用可见状态。

Result: 在item-disjoint审计中接受全部60个受支持原始项并拒绝全部179个硬负例；在多键持久故障下无需知道实际物理写入集合即可恢复完整声明的活动映射；在MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation中，MemTxn在全部12种答案模型配置下取得最高平均F1，在5个代表性设置中超出Dense方法17.06-24.07分。

Conclusion: MemTxn通过添加事务治理层，有效解决了持久内存Agent系统中的更新验证、冲突解决和故障恢复问题，为可靠的可写内存提供了系统化的保障。

Abstract: Persistent memory lets long-running large language model agents reuse information across sessions and tasks. Yet errors in writable memory can persist and corrupt future behavior. Existing systems improve storage and retrieval, but they do not provide a transaction boundary for reliable updates and recovery. We therefore propose MemTxn, a governance layer outside the answer model. MemTxn verifies whether an update is supported by its source. It also selects the visible version when facts conflict and restores the application-visible state after a fault. The system uses Ordered PatchTest to validate writes, a Temporal Resolver to select versions, and a durable snapshot journal to recover state. On an item-disjoint audit, MemTxn accepts all 60 supported originals and rejects all 179 hard negatives. Under persistent multi-key faults on LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo states, it restores the complete declared active map without knowing the actual physical write set. On MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation, MemTxn achieves the highest average F1 across all twelve answer-model configurations. It outperforms Dense by 17.06--24.07 points in five representative settings.

</details>


### [49] [Crossing the Margin Cliff: Toward Relearn-Robust LLM Unlearning via Margin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27836)
*Xiangyu Yin,Jiaxu Liu,Zhen Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型遗忘（unlearning）在再学习攻击（relearn attack）下的一致性脆弱性问题。作者发现无论采用何种后验方法，在TOFU数据集上仅用20个遗忘样例微调即可大幅恢复遗忘集ROUGE分数。他们追踪到这一脆弱性源于优化几何中的"margin cliff"现象——即14种方法在42种方法-规模组合中的41种，其逐token答案边际收敛到保留参考之上的一条窄带。作者提出Margin Calibration (MC)方法，通过非饱和边际铰链损失和KL探针恢复遗忘侧压力，在14个对口遗忘聚合指标和所有再学习单元上全面获胜，并将面板平均攻击后ROUGE-L从0.41降至0.18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的遗忘机制在再学习攻击下表现一致脆弱：即使使用极少量遗忘样例进行微调，也能显著恢复已被遗忘的内容。现有14种涵盖梯度、偏好和蒸馏家族的遗忘方法均无法抵御这种攻击。作者希望理解这一普遍脆弱性的根本原因，并开发出不依赖保留参考就能从根本上增强遗忘鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 作者首先通过实证分析揭示"margin cliff"规律：当保留耦合（retain coupling）将遗忘内容的诊断log-odds保持在某个底限之上时，逐token边际会收敛到窄带。他们证明了该规律的数学条件并验证了42个细胞中的34个。随后提出Margin Calibration (MC)方法：一种即插即用的抛光（polish）技术，使用以参考逐token边际为锚点的非饱和边际铰链损失，配合不相交指令语料上的KL探针，在原生损失饱和处恢复遗忘侧压力。在梯度支配条件下，其驻点位于悬崖跨越侧，提供了一个攻击预算上界。

Result: 在TOFU（三种Llama-3规模、三个遗忘层级）、MUSE-News（Llama-2-7B-hf）和Phi-3.5面板上，单一冻结配置在所有14个对口遗忘聚合指标和所有有数据的再学习单元上获胜：面板平均攻击后ROUGE-L从0.41降至0.18，并在13/14单元上降低原始成员推断AUC，主要代价是保留侧效用略有下降。部署变体无需保留训练参考即可实现相同增益。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：LLM遗忘的脆弱性并非方法特定的缺陷，而是优化几何的固有规律——margin cliff。所提出的MC方法通过恢复饱和损失下的遗忘侧梯度压力，能在无需重新训练的情况下跨模型、跨规模、跨遗忘场景一致性地提升遗忘鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了一种无需保留参考的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning is consistently fragile under relearn attacks. On TOFU, fine-tuning on twenty forget examples substantially recovers held-out forget-set ROUGE for every method we evaluate, and we trace this fragility to optimization geometry. The per-token answer margin of fourteen post-hoc methods spanning gradient, preference, and distillation families converges into a narrow band above the retain reference in 41 of 42 method--size cells, a regularity we call the margin cliff. We prove that this cliff follows whenever the retain coupling holds the diagnostic log-odds of forget content above a floor, a condition that token-saturating losses induce at stationarity and that we verify directly on 34 of 42 cells. Margin Calibration (\textsc{MC}) is a plug-in polish adding a non-saturating margin hinge anchored at the reference's per-token margin plus a KL probe on a disjoint instruction corpus, restoring forget-side pressure where the native loss saturates. Under a stated gradient-dominance condition, whose on-trajectory gradient signature we measure by instrumenting the polish, its stationary set lies on the cliff-crossing side, yielding an attack-budget upper bound on the relearn margin lift. Across TOFU (three Llama-3 sizes, three forget tiers), MUSE-News on Llama-2-7B-hf, and a Phi-3.5 panel, a single frozen configuration wins all 14 head-to-head forget aggregates and all populated relearn cells (panel-mean post-attack ROUGE-L $0.41$ to $0.18$) and lowers raw membership AUC on 13/14, with reduced retain-side utility as the main cost. A deployment variant matches these gains without a retain-trained reference.

</details>


### [50] [Virtual Process Dossier: A Process-Aware Data Catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27840)
*Lukas Kubelka,Alexander Bott,Frank Döhner,Saksham Kiroriwal,Georg Zeeb,Julia Butte,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出虚拟工艺档案（VPD），一个基于知识图谱的数据目录系统，能够捕获工作流溯源信息，用于多阶段制造场景中支持AI优化任务的数据管理，并以FAIR方式提供数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多阶段制造场景中，下游基于AI的优化任务需要区分各个工作流步骤生成的数据集，现有数据目录无法充分捕获工作流溯源信息，需要一种能够同时提供数据目录和工作流图谱的解决方案。

Method: 提出了三个贡献组件：(1) VPD本体，作为目录的语义核心；(2) VPD溯源框架，将本体实例化集成到生产环境中；(3) VPD用户界面，提供与VPD知识图谱的人机交互。

Result: VPD提供FAIR方式的数据集管理，同时显式表达前瞻性和回顾性工作流溯源信息，使多阶段制造中的数据集可区分且可追溯。

Conclusion: VPD通过知识图谱技术实现了一个兼容FAIR原则、具备完整溯源能力的数据目录，其本体和代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose the Virtual Process Dossier (VPD), a Knowledge Graph-based data catalogue that also captures workflow provenance. We developed VPD for multi-stage manufacturing use-cases where downstream AI-based optimization tasks require to distinct between datasets generated during individual workflow steps. VPD provides these datasets in a FAIR manner and makes both prospective and retrospective workflow provenance explicit. Our contributions are: (1) the VPD ontology that serves as the catalogue's semantic core; (2) the VPD provenance framework that integrates ontology instantiation into the production environment; and (3) the VPD user interface that provides human-centered interaction with the VPD Knowledge Graph. The ontology and code are available at https://github.com/kubeluk/VirtualProcessDossier .

</details>


### [51] [Simplifying Neural Networks During Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27854)
*Lorenzo Sciandra,Samuele Fonio,Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种受神经坍缩（NC）启发的训练框架，通过Inverse Fisher Criterion监测表征动态，在训练过程中识别特征提取与分类的分界点，将尾部层替换为轻量分类头，从而在不损失精度的前提下显著减少网络参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度参数化的深度神经网络训练动态难以理解，且存在大量冗余参数。现有证据表明神经坍缩（NC）揭示了类别表征和分类器的结构化几何特征，而隧道效应（Tunnel Effect）显示只有部分层对特征提取起关键作用。作者希望结合这两种视角，在训练过程中动态简化网络结构以减少参数冗余。

Method: 提出一个NC启发的简化框架：(1) 使用Inverse Fisher Criterion（IFC）作为变异性坍缩行为的稳定高效代理指标，实时监测表征动态；(2) 通过IFC识别特征提取与分类之间的分界点（split point），同时判断简化可行的训练阶段；(3) 将尾部层替换为轻量级分类头，继续训练缩减后的模型。在MLP、VGG和ResNet架构上进行图像分类实验验证。

Result: 在MLP、VGG和ResNet等多种架构的图像分类基准上，该方法在保持与完整模型相当精度的同时，实现了显著的参数缩减。

Conclusion: 该工作证明NC驱动的动态网络简化是可行的，通过监测变异性坍缩行为可以在训练过程中有效地裁剪冗余层，实现参数效率与分类性能的平衡。

Abstract: Understanding and exploiting the training dynamics of overparameterized deep neural networks remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Recent evidence on Neural Collapse (NC) shows that class representations and classifiers exhibit highly structured geometry, while the Tunnel Effect suggests that only a subset of layers is essential for feature extraction. We combine these two perspectives and propose an NC-inspired training framework for simplifying deep networks during training. Our method monitors representation dynamics through the Inverse Fisher Criterion, a stable and efficient proxy for the variability collapse behavior, to identify both the split point between feature extraction and classification and the training stage at which simplification becomes viable. We then replace the trailing layers with a lightweight classification head and continue training the reduced model. Experiments on image-classification benchmarks across MLP, VGG, and ResNet architectures show that the proposed method achieves substantial parameter reductions while maintaining accuracy comparable to that of the full model. Code to reproduce the experiments can be found at: https://github.com/LorenzoSciandra/NNS.

</details>


### [52] [Back to All-Entity Ranking: Sampler-Dependent Evaluation in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27861)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在连续时间动态图（CTDG）中，基于采样负样本的评估方法会产生有偏的排名结果。本文证明了非均匀负采样会改变贝叶斯最优排名，即使均匀有限采样也会扭曲模型排名和模块效应的测量。通过跨六个模型、四个数据集的实验，作者发现模型相对优劣和消融结论都依赖于候选集配置，因此推荐使用全实体排名（all-entity ranking）作为架构比较的主要评估手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTDG中的下一目的地预测通常会从负目的地中采样来评估模型，但评估得分条件依赖于负样本分布和候选集大小/数量，这可能导致模型比较结论不稳定且不可复现。作者希望揭示采样配置对评估结果的具体影响机制，并提出更稳健的评估方案。

Method: 作者通过三个层面分析：1) 理论分析非均匀负分布对贝叶斯最优排名的影响；2) 提出一个仅基于"对-历史成员关系"的最简评分器来隔离采样器影响的机制；3) 在六个模型和四个数据集（LastFM、MOOC、Reddit、Wikipedia）上进行因子化评估（重复/新增正样本 × 已见/未见负样本）及受控表征干预实验。

Result: 1) 非均匀负分布会改变贝叶斯最优排名；2) 在四个数据集中有三个数据集上，至少有一对模型的相对顺序在Uniform-20指标和全目录评估之间发生翻转；3) 同一模块的测量效应会随候选集大小和训练目标在幅度和方向上都发生变化。

Conclusion: 基于采样负样本的基准评估结论是条件性的，依赖于候选配置。全实体排名（all-entity ranking）对所有固定目录中的目的地进行评估，消除了负样本选择的自由度和采样方差，同时保留原始CTDG评分器，因此应作为可枚举固定目的地目录下CTDG架构比较的主要证据。

Abstract: Next-destination prediction in continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) commonly ranks an observed interaction against sampled negative destinations. The resulting score is conditional on both the negative distribution and the number of candidates chosen by the researcher. We show that a non-uniform negative distribution changes the Bayes-optimal ranking, while even a finite candidate set drawn uniformly can destabilize model rankings and measured module effects.
  Time-varying source-destination history membership and model operations that use this information directly transmit the sampler's influence to the evaluation score. We examine this mechanism using a factorial evaluation of repeated and new positives against seen and unseen negatives, a minimal scorer based solely on pair-history membership, and controlled representation interventions. Across six models on LastFM, MOOC, Reddit, and Wikipedia, at least one model pair changes relative order between the expected Uniform-20 metric and the full catalog on three of the four datasets. The measured effect of the same module also changes in magnitude and direction with the candidate-set size and training objective.
  These results establish that model-superiority and ablation conclusions from sampled-negative benchmarks are conditional on the stated candidate configuration. All-entity ranking evaluates every destination in a fixed catalog, eliminating negative-selection freedom and sampling variation while retaining the original CTDG scorer. We therefore recommend all-entity ranking as the primary evidence for architecture comparisons on CTDG benchmarks with an enumerable, fixed destination catalog.

</details>


### [53] [Orca: Neural Operators for Causal Reasoning in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27867)
*Gerrit Großmann,David A. Selby,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Orca框架，将结构因果模型扩展到连续时间动态系统。Orca中因果图的每个节点是时间的函数，每个机制是学习到的函数空间之间的映射，支持干预和反事实推理，处理不规则观测时间和反馈环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的结构因果模型只描述静态变量、通常限制变量只测量一次、且禁止循环依赖。然而现实中的关键系统（如患者、气候、经济）持续随时间演化、在非规则时间点被观测并包含反馈环。需要一个能在这类动态设置下进行因果推理的自然框架。

Method: Orca框架：每个因果图节点是时间的函数，每个机制是从父节点函数空间到子节点函数空间的学习映射。扩展神经算子架构以表达因果机制——机制接受多个父函数作为输入来计算节点函数值，尊重时间的方向，并将潜在外生噪声视为可被推断和复用于反事实推理的函数。论文形式化该模型类别并在合成连续时间示例上演示反事实推理。

Result: 形式化了Orca模型类别，并在合成连续时间示例上成功演示了反事实推理能力。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 神经算子学习为连续时间动态系统中的因果推理提供了自然基础。Orca框架将SCM推广到函数空间，支持时间序列中的干预与反事实推理，涵盖不规则观测和反馈回路。

Abstract: Structural causal models are the standard language for reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, but they describe static variables, typically measured once, and usually forbid cyclic dependencies. Many systems we care about, such as patients, climates, and economies, instead evolve continuously in time, are observed at irregular time points, and contain feedback loops. We argue that neural operator learning provides a natural foundation for causal reasoning in this setting, and propose Orca, a framework in which each node of the causal graph is a function of time and each mechanism is a learned map between function spaces. We extend existing neural operator architectures to express causal mechanisms: a mechanism computes the function value of a node from its parent nodes by taking several parent functions as input, respects the arrow of time, and treats latent exogenous noise as a function that can be inferred and reused for counterfactuals. We formalize the model class and demonstrate counterfactual reasoning on synthetic continuous-time examples. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/orca

</details>


### [54] [Search as Computation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27871)
*Alexander Tuisov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"终端计算分配问题"——算法在内部消耗计算资源后仅以最终决策质量被评估。通过Bellman方程刻画固定预算、按量计费和精确认证下的最优分配，并将计算价值(VOC)与信息联系起来：互信息等于对数损失下的短视VOC，简单遗憾下的VOC是知识梯度量。最后在显式的前沿消解与启发式误差模型下，最大化近似VOC可还原加权A*算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多算法在执行过程中消耗内部计算资源（如bandit拉臂、树模拟、节点扩展），但最终仅以终端输出的决策质量被评估。目前缺乏一个统一的理论框架来刻画这类"计算分配"问题，以及计算资源的价值与信息获取之间的精确关系。

Method: 作者将这类问题形式化为"终端计算分配问题"，其中昂贵计算产生观测、更新关于潜在环境的信念，并且仅通过终端决策损失产生作用。利用Bellman方程刻画固定预算、按量计费和精确认证三种场景下的最优分配策略。进一步推导VOC与信息论量（互信息、知识梯度）之间的解析关系，并在特定误差模型下（前沿消解与启发式误差）证明最大化近似VOC等价于加权A*搜索。

Result: (1) Bellman方程可为三类计算成本模型提供最优分配；(2) 对数损失下互信息恰等于短视VOC，而简单遗憾下的VOC是知识梯度量；(3) 信息增益对VOC的排序可能任意差，但提供单侧上界；(4) bandit拉臂、树模拟与节点展开可统一在同一模型下对应不同计算拓扑；(5) 在显式误差模型下，最大化近似VOC可恢复加权A*，A*与贪心最佳优先搜索为其极限情形。

Conclusion: 该理论识别出了这些看似不同的算法（A*、贪心最佳优先、bandit）背后共享的同一决策问题，但强调并不存在单一的普遍最优采集规则，不同场景需要匹配不同的VOC定义。

Abstract: Many algorithms spend an internal resource before returning a decision and are evaluated only by the quality of that terminal output. We formalize such procedures as terminal computation-allocation problems: costly computations produce observations, update beliefs about a latent environment, and matter only through terminal decision loss. Bellman equations characterize optimal allocation under fixed budgets, priced computation, and exact certification. We then relate value of computation (VOC) to information. Mutual information equals myopic VOC under log loss, whereas under simple regret VOC is a knowledge-gradient quantity; moreover, information gain can rank computations arbitrarily poorly, although it gives a one-sided upper bound on VOC. Bandit pulls, tree simulations, and node expansions illustrate the same model under different computation topologies. Finally, under an explicit frontier-resolution and heuristic-error model, maximizing approximate VOC recovers weighted A*, with A* and greedy best-first search as limiting cases. The theory identifies a shared decision problem without asserting that one acquisition rule is universally optimal.

</details>


### [55] [An Empirical Study of Coordination Mode as the First-Class Citizen in From-Scratch Multi-Agent Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27877)
*Yanyu Ren,Yunfeng Bai,Xizheng Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSEval基准，用于在真实全栈项目上评估多智能体编程系统的性能。通过LegoGent执行引擎测试10种协作拓扑，利用TAgent自动评分器联合衡量功能成功率、延迟和token成本，发现组织拓扑对速度-成本-质量权衡的影响甚至超过模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编程基准依赖合成环境，忽略了实际的时间和金钱成本，混淆推理与通信的界限，且只奖励表面的任务完成，无法真实反映多智能体团队在现实软件开发中的表现。

Method: 构建基于10个跨领域真实全栈项目的评估基准MSEval，采用分层需求与确定性评分标准；设计LegoGent执行引擎，测试10种协作拓扑，智能体通过周期性同步间隔协调并通过原生CI/CD流水线部署；使用自动化评分器TAgent动态探测实现，联合测量功能成功率、延迟和前缀缓存token成本。

Result: 在100次运行中，MSEval发现组织拓扑对速度-成本-质量权衡的影响与模型能力相当；对相同任务和模型，改变拓扑可使得分波动超过30分，且墙钟时间翻倍。结构化流水线收敛最快且质量最高，而过重的管理监督反而降低性能。
