We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/ROAD.

</details>


### [220] [MixFrag: Fragility-Guided Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28589)
*Md. Mehrab Hossain Opi,Robiul Islam Ryad,Md. Umar Faruk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixFrag是一个基于脆弱性引导的混合精度后训练量化框架，通过KL散度度量组件级量化脆弱性，并将位宽分配建模为多选择背包问题，在ViT上实现了SOTA性能，在COCO检测与分割任务上相比之前最优方法提升了最多9.6 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ViT后训练量化方法通常对所有Transformer组件采用统一的位宽，忽略了不同组件对量化的敏感度差异，导致精度分配效率低下。本文旨在通过组件的异构敏感度来指导混合精度量化，以实现更高效的位宽分配。

Method: MixFrag首先使用小规模校准集，通过测量全精度与孤立量化输出分布之间的KL散度来估计组件级量化脆弱性；然后，将位宽分配形式化为多选择背包问题（MCKP），在目标位预算约束下实现自适应的逐层精度分配。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上的多种ViT架构实验中取得了有竞争力的分类性能；在COCO目标检测和实例分割任务上达到了混合精度PTQ方法的SOTA性能，在MP3/MP3设置下相比之前最优方法提升了最高9.6 AP。消融实验验证了脆弱性度量的有效性及其与学习到的位宽分配之间的强相关性。

Conclusion: MixFrag被证明是一个有效的ViT混合精度后训练量化框架，其脆弱性引导的位分配策略能够显著提升量化性能，尤其是下游视觉任务上的表现。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as an effective solution for deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [221] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang,Yixing Li,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jianghao Lin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD），一种反事实目标重构算法，用于估计多模态on-policy蒸馏中教师修正的视觉可归属部分，通过投影重构学生锚定目标，在六个细粒度视觉基准上超越直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态on-policy蒸馏（OPD）利用特权视图教师监督学生生成轨迹以传递细粒度视觉知识，但其下一次词元修正存在源混合问题——结合了视觉信号、语言先验和教师特定效应。核心挑战在于估计哪些修正有视觉证据支持，而非仅仅在何处或多大程度上进行蒸馏。

Method: 提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD）：在学生生成的前缀上，用同一固定教师在证据存在与移除两种条件下分别评估，计算中心化对数概率的变化作为视觉证据方向的带符号代理ut。将原始修正投影到该代理向量上，得到干预对齐分量和代理无法解释的残差，从前者重构学生锚定目标作为主要监督信号，特权教师仅提供弱正则化。

Result: 在4B和9B参数量下、六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。词元级和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关视觉修正，产生更强的目标位移，尤其在证据反驳错误答案时更为显著。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重构是源混合监督的有效替代方案，通过估计教师修正中视觉证据可归因的部分，实现了更精准的知识蒸馏。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [222] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon智能体视觉推理模型，从模式适应性（MA）和工具效应（TE）两个维度重新审视智能体视觉推理，通过必要性感知自适应奖励和线索引导的能力扩展机制，在强化学习阶段提升模型的工具调用适应性与工具实际效果。实验表明Beacon在整体性能、模式适应性和工具效应上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型过度依赖工具调用，忽视了工具使用的必要性与有效性。作者发现现有模型在模式适应性上表现有限——模型难以判断何时真正需要工具；同时工具带来的收益在困难样本上被在简单样本上引入的错误所抵消。因此需要重新思考并改进智能体视觉推理的工具使用范式。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，核心包含两个机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward（必要性感知自适应奖励），根据任务实际需求鼓励模型自适应地调用工具，避免不必要的计算开销；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion（线索引导能力扩展），在强化学习阶段通过线索引导强化模型在最困难问题上的工具使用能力。整体框架在强化学习阶段同时优化工具调用决策和工具使用效果。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon在整体性能上表现优异，同时实现了模式适应性和工具效应方面的显著改进：模型能够准确识别工具调用时机，且在简单样本上避免引入额外错误，在困难样本上获得真正的工具增益。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉推理的目标应是提升MLLM在复杂任务上的成功率，而非仅仅配备复杂的推理范式。通过MA和TE两个维度系统地分析并优化工具使用，Beacon证明了"按需调用工具、让工具真正产生增益"是智能体视觉推理更有效的方向。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [223] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chimera，一个混合视觉扩散骨干网络，结合Kimi Delta Attention（线性复杂度）、交错Multi-head Latent Attention和模态感知短卷积来突破全注意力二次复杂度瓶颈；并提出HeteroP模块级缩放方案，用于拟合Chinchilla风格的计算最优律，最终训练出11B参数（2B激活）的模型，在扩散损失上相比Wan-2.1 2B基线实现7.3倍计算效率提升，并支持零样本5秒到30秒视频外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，而全注意力的二次方计算代价在高分辨率、长序列场景下难以承受，亟需设计兼具效率与表达能力的混合视觉扩散架构，以及配套的可扩展缩放策略。

Method: (1) 架构上采用Chimera混合骨干：以无位置嵌入的raster顺序流处理文本/图像/视频token，使用Kim Delta Attention(KDA)进行O(N)复杂度的长上下文状态追踪、交错Multi-head Latent Attention(MLA)提供直接全局交互、模态感知短卷积负责局部时空上下文，并引入稀疏Mixture-of-Experts(MoE)层扩展容量。(2) 缩放上提出HeteroP模块级方案，按张量功能扇入和模型深度在不同宽度和深度间迁移超参数，生成一致性调优的模型族，用于拟合Chinchilla风格的计算最优律（激活模型大小、训练token数、图像-视频数据比）。

Result: (1) 在扩散预训练损失上，稠密骨干比全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线计算效率高1.7倍，完整系统达7.3倍。(2) 无需长度特定微调，模型从5秒训练片段零样本外推至30秒视频，最后5秒仅6.5% FID退化。(3) 拟合的最优律显示：图像预训练在激活模型大小和训练token数之间近乎平均分配计算预算，而视频预训练在更高预算下略微偏向模型大小。

Conclusion: Chimera及其HeteroP缩放方法为高效长上下文扩散架构的设计与扩展奠定了基础，证明了混合架构配合计算最优律能够在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，并为图像/视频数据预算分配提供了可量化的指导原则。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [224] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一种以"物理语言"（一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示）为核心构建的物理世界模型。它采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）范式：先从野外视频中通过自监督学习物理语言，用其显式推理物理世界的未来演化，再将推理出的转换渲染为视频。实验涵盖生成和理解基准，验证了其对物理一致世界演化的建模能力，并展示了在交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型通常在像素空间直接预测未来视频，将底层世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式的物理推理能力。作者受人类能够从视觉经验中抽象预测结构并以自然语言组织显式推理的能力启发，希望引入一种紧凑的离散表示（物理语言）来显式推理物理世界的演化，从而提升世界模型的物理一致性和可解释性。

Method: PhiZero首先从野外视频中通过自监督学习获得"物理语言"——一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示。模型采用reason-then-render两阶段范式：(1) 推理阶段，模型推断未来世界演化为物理语言序列，实现显式物理推理；(2) 渲染阶段，将推理得到的物理语言序列转换为对应的视频帧。

Result: 在生成和理解两大基准上的广泛实验验证了PhiZero能够建模物理一致的世界演化。此外，实验还展示了其在真实感交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟以及零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: PhiZero证明了以紧凑离散表示（物理语言）进行显式物理推理再渲染视频的范式是可行且有效的，相较直接在像素空间预测的做法，能更好地保证物理一致性，并具备交互式建模、动作条件模拟和运动迁移等扩展能力。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [225] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ambient Capture Engine (ACE)，一个人类中心的数据引擎，将真实家庭环境转变为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室。基于ACE构建ACE-Data-0数据集，包含150小时、1700万视频帧、200个任务类别的75000个交互片段，并引入层级基准测试，揭示现有SOTA方法在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度下的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈。现有数据集将第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉等体验按照视角、模态或空间尺度进行碎片化切分，导致完整的感知-行动循环只能被部分观测，无法支撑模型捕获人类在目标驱动下多模态感知与行动协同演化的完整过程。

Method: ACE提供两种互补尺度的采集配置：桌面级配置解析手-物精细操作，房间级配置捕获全身运动、移动和跨家具交互。系统同步记录第一人称和多方外部视角视频、全身与灵巧手运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频及触觉信号，形成统一的多感官流。数据集通过目标级而非逐步指令引导参与者，保持自然行为变异性。此外构建了从信号到场景组件再到交互的层级基准。

Result: 构建了ACE-Data-0数据集：150小时、1700万帧视频、200个任务类别、50名参与者在2个环境中完成75000个交互片段，覆盖原子操作、长时程家庭活动链和人与场景交互。对SOTA方法的评估揭示其在接触、遮挡、自我运动及长时时间跨度下的显著性能差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供带有对齐感知、运动学和接触监督的同步人类演示，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统和具身AI提供可扩展的基础，弥合了现有数据在完整感知-行动回路上的碎片化缺口。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [226] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken是一种可学习的检索嵌入，它从预填充的视觉KV缓存中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉token，以解决长视觉上下文中视觉-语言模型性能退化与GPU内存受限的问题。仅用小型图像QA数据集训练即可在图像和视频基准上带来一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉-语言模型构成挑战：随着干扰项数量的增加模型性能会退化，且由于GPU内存限制，同时处理所有token在计算上不可行。

Method: 提出ReToken，一个单一的、可学习的嵌入，作为显式检索目标进行训练，从预填充的视觉KV缓存中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉token。采用轻量级设计，训练和长视频推理均可运行在单个H100上。

Result: 仅用小型图像QA数据集训练，ReToken在图像和视频基准上均取得一致提升：在Visual Haystacks上使Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4分、InternVL3.5提升12.4分（相对提升超过20%）；在LVBench上零样本迁移至长视频，使Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0分增益。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量的可学习检索机制，在保持单卡可推理的前提下显著提升了长视觉上下文下视觉-语言模型的性能，并展示了良好的跨模态（图像到视频）泛化能力。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [FAVA: Formal Authorization for Verified Agents with Evidence-Backed Permission Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27267)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FAVA是一个针对LLM智能体的形式化授权框架，通过LLM引导的权限中间表示（IR）将模糊的自然语言任务转换为结构化约束，再经确定性降级转换为带证据的权限图，利用SMT求解器进行数学验证以决定是否授权执行行为，最终通过运行时网关强制执行求解结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在动态环境中自主交织语义推理与复杂系统操作，静态的工具级权限无法满足安全需求，因为安全授权高度依赖上下文并随着运行时状态和数据流不断演变。

Method: FAVA包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM引导的权限中间表示(IR)，将自然语言任务转化为结构化约束；(2) 确定性降级掉，将IR转换为显式跟踪数据流、依赖关系和上下文标签的权限图；(3) 基于可满足性模理论(SMT)的授权器，在执行任何有效操作前数学验证当前图是否符合安全策略，并由运行时网关强制执行求解结果。

Result: 在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane三个场景上的评估显示，FAVA在聚合数据集上达到90.5%的决策合规率(DCR)，并能成功拦截所评估的trace-conditioned场景中的动态违规轨迹。

Conclusion: FAVA通过将形式化方法与LLM智能体结合，实现了上下文感知的动态授权机制，验证了基于证明的权限图和SMT验证方法在智能体安全执行中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents autonomously interleave semantic reasoning with complex system operations. In these dynamic environments, static tool-level permissions are fundamentally insufficient; safe authorization is highly context-dependent and heavily reliant on evolving runtime states and data flows. We present FAVA (Formal Authorization for Verified Agents), a permission-carrying authorization framework for agent execution. FAVA utilizes an LLM-guided Permission Intermediate Representation (IR) to translate ambiguous natural-language tasks into structured constraints. A deterministic lowering pass then converts this IR into an evidence-backed permission graph that explicitly tracks data flows, dependencies, and contextual labels. To provide strict security guarantees, a Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) authorizer mathematically verifies the current graph against security policies before any effectful action executes. A runtime gateway then enforces the solver's result, either authorizing the execution or intercepting it with a precise counterexample. We evaluate FAVA across OpenAgentSafety, OctoBench, and ActPlane scenarios. Our evaluation demonstrates that FAVA achieves a 90.5% Decision Compliance Rate (DCR) over the aggregate dataset, successfully intercepting dynamic violating traces in the evaluated trace-conditioned scenarios.

</details>


### [228] [Open Security Benchmark: Towards Autonomous Enterprise Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27288)
*Gal Engelberg,Michael Arenzon,Leon Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Open Security Benchmark (OSB)，一个用于端到端评估企业自主网络防御智能体（agentic AI）的框架。针对企业环境数据不可公开获取的"环境数据缺口"，OSB提供冻结的企业安全态势快照，并通过text-to-SQL和厂商原生API两种模态评估态势调查能力，以闭式ground truth锚定答案，支持公开比较与私有租户评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业正转向自主网络防御（agentic AI），但现有评估无法回答"代理是否值得信任"这一关键问题。真正的企业环境是私有、跨厂商、深度关联的，没有一个可公开查询的统一评估目标，这构成了"环境数据缺口"，阻碍了对这类智能体的端到端评测。

Method: OSB框架包含五大组件：数据层（合成的多规模企业环境数据集）、任务与评估集层、多维评分层、最小可审计评测框架（harness）、以及bring-your-own路径（同一底层架构同时支持公开比较和私有租户评估）。框架冻结环境为不可变快照，以闭式ground truth锚定答案，并通过两种模态（关系快照上的text-to-SQL、同环境实例上的厂商原生API）评估态势调查能力。文中用两个身份安全包和一族合成组织数据集实例化该框架。

Result: 论文实例化了OSB框架，提供了两个身份安全评测集和一个覆盖多规模尺度的合成组织环境数据集家族，并展示了框架向更多态势子域、调查模态以及从评估到修复的防御阶段的扩展路径。

Conclusion: OSB为自主网络防御智能体提供了首个可公开、可查询、可审计的端到端评估基础设施，填补了企业环境数据缺口，为判断智能体在安全态势管理中的可信度奠定了评测基础。

Abstract: Enterprises are moving toward autonomous cyber defense: agentic AI that builds situational awareness of an organization's security state and reasons from it to assessments, decisions, and actions. This rests on a holistic view of the enterprise's security state, the continuous, cross-vendor picture of identities, cloud and infrastructure, data, applications, and their configurations that security posture management assembles. As agents take on this work, what matters is not whether an agent can produce an answer but whether it should be trusted to. The field cannot yet answer this question. Real enterprise environments are private, cross-vendor, and deeply correlated, and none is exposed publicly as a shared, queryable target for evaluating such agents end to end. We call this the environment data gap. We present Open Security Benchmark (OSB), a framework that benchmarks agentic AI on this work. OSB surfaces a curated enterprise environment - a frozen, holistic view of the security state - and evaluates posture investigation across two modalities: text-to-SQL over a relational snapshot and each vendor's native API over a served instance of the same environment. Freezing the environment pins the target state as an immutable snapshot and anchors answers to a closed-form ground truth. OSB is built from five components: a data layer, a task and evaluation-set layer, a multi-dimensional scoring layer, a minimal auditable harness, and a bring-your-own path that serves public comparison and private tenant evaluation from one substrate. We instantiate the framework with two identity-security packs and a family of synthetic-organization environment datasets spanning multiple scales, and chart its extension to further posture subdomains, investigation modalities, and defense stages from assessment toward remediation.

</details>


### [229] [RoguePrompt: Dual-Layer Encoding for Self-Reconstruction to Circumvent LLM Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27373)
*Benyamin Tafreshian,Prathamesh Dhake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoguePrompt，一种将恶意提示进行分区并应用Vigenere+ROT13双重嵌套编码及自然语言重建指令的越狱流水线。在黑盒威胁模型下对313条真实被拒提示进行测试，平均过滤绕过率93.93%、指令重建率79.02%、执行率70.18%，并在各阶段提供了细粒度的失败证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击研究将可见接受、隐藏请求恢复和执行合并为单一的"攻击成功"指标，缺乏对多阶段提示转换攻击在可观察黑盒交互中具体失败位置的证据。研究者希望提供阶段级（moderation绕过、指令重建、执行）的成功率分析，以更精确地理解攻击流水线中的薄弱环节。

Method: RoguePrompt将禁止提示进行分区，应用两层嵌套编码（先Vigenere后ROT13）以及自然语言重建指令。在仅拥有API或用户界面的黑盒威胁模型下开发与评估，对313条真实世界中曾被硬拒绝的提示进行测试。成功采用三个阶段分别度量：审核绕过、指令重建以及执行，每阶段以自动化标准判断是否通过。

Result: RoguePrompt在313条真实被拒提示上实现了平均93.93%的过滤绕过率、79.02%的重建率和70.18%的执行率。结果证明分层提示编码的有效性，同时提供了每个阶段（审核绕过、指令重建、执行）失败位置的具体证据。

Conclusion: RoguePrompt证明了分层编码结合自然语言重建指令的越狱攻击具有较高成功率，且通过阶段级评估揭示了多阶段越狱在过滤绕过、指令重建和执行环节中各自的失败模式，为理解和防御此类攻击提供了更细粒度的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly integrated into mainstream development platforms and daily technological workflows, typically behind moderation and safety controls. Despite these controls, preventing prompt-based policy evasion remains challenging, and adversaries continue to "jailbreak" LLMs by crafting prompts that circumvent implemented safety mechanisms. Prior work has established cipher-mediated interaction, code-embedded decryption, prompt decomposition and reconstruction, and layered custom encryption as viable attack primitives. However, reported evaluations generally collapse visible acceptance, successful recovery of the concealed request, and subsequent execution into an aggregate attack-success outcome. This leaves limited evidence about where multistage prompt-transformation attacks fail within an observable black-box interaction. This paper introduces RoguePrompt, a jailbreak pipeline that partitions a forbidden prompt and applies two nested encodings, Vigenere followed by ROT13, along with natural-language reconstruction instructions. RoguePrompt was developed and evaluated under a black-box threat model, with only API or user-interface access to the hosted models, and was tested on 313 real-world, hard-rejected prompts. Success was measured in terms of moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution when the relevant stage exceeded its automated criterion. RoguePrompt achieved average rates of 93.93% for filter bypass, 79.02% for reconstruction, and 70.18% for execution. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of layered prompt encoding while providing stage-level evidence of where multistage jailbreaks fail during moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution.

</details>


### [230] [Low-Latency Bootstrapping for CKKS using Roots of Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27401)
*Jean-Sebastien Coron,Robin Koestler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRU（Sparse Roots of Unity）引导技术，一种面向CKKS同态加密方案的新引导算法。与原始CKKS引导依赖多项式近似模q约简不同，SPRU直接将模q的加法群嵌入复单位根中，利用CKKS方案原生支持的特性进行求值，显著降低了引导所需的乘法深度，从而支持使用更小的环维数并提升效率。在OpenFHE C++库中，SPRU引导在小槽数密文上实现了最高5倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKKS方案支持近似算术，但引导（bootstrapping）过程数值开销大，原始方法需要同态求值一个近似模q约简的多项式，导致乘法深度过高，限制了环维数的选择并影响整体效率。作者旨在设计一种新方法，绕过多项式近似，从根本上降低引导的乘法深度和计算开销。

Method: SPRU引导的核心思想是将模q的加法群直接嵌入复单位根（complex roots of unity）中，由于CKKS方案本身支持在复数域上进行编码和运算，因此这种根嵌入可以在CKKS中原生执行，无需通过高次多项式来近似模约简。通过这种方式，减少了引导所需的乘法深度，进而在保持安全性的前提下采用更小的环维数。

Result: 在OpenFHE C++库中实现后，SPRU引导在处理具有少量槽位数（small number of slots）的密文时，相较于原始CKKS引导方法实现了最高5倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: SPRU引导通过将模运算嵌入复数单位根这一更为直接的数学结构，有效降低了CKKS引导的乘法深度和计算成本，尤其在小槽数场景下表现显著，为CKKS同态加密的实际应用提供了更高效的引导方案。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse Roots of Unity (SPRU) bootstrapping, a new bootstrapping algorithm for the CKKS homomorphic encryption scheme for approximate arithmetic. The original CKKS bootstrapping method relies on homomorphically evaluating a polynomial that approximates modular reduction modulo q. In contrast, SPRU bootstrapping directly embeds the additive group modulo q into the complex roots of unity, which can be evaluated natively in the CKKS scheme. This approach significantly reduces the multiplicative depth required for bootstrapping, enabling the use of a smaller ring dimension and improving efficiency. In practice, using the OpenFHE C++ library, SPRU bootstrapping achieves up to a 5x reduction in latency when applied to ciphertexts with a small number of slots.

</details>


### [231] [Granite: A Modular Methodology for Foundational Verification of Hardware-Software Leakage Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27480)
*Stella Lau,Andres Erbsen,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Granite是一种用于RTL处理器模块化验证的方法论，同时验证功能正确性和非泄漏性。它通过泄漏感知精化（基于确定性）建立了指令集架构（ISA）级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行之间的模块化、基础性连接，证明流水线RISC设计（含推测、精确中断和I/O）的逐周期时序仅由ISA泄漏契约中的可观测量决定，从而排除了已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件验证工作通常只关注功能正确性，而忽略安全性（如时序侧信道泄漏）。将ISA级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行（含线级观察）之间建立起模块化且基础性的连接是此前未解决的挑战。

Method: 核心技术是"泄漏感知精化经由确定性"（leakage-aware refinement via determinism），通过相对于一族逐周期确定性规格机的迹等价同时建立正确性和机密性。对秘密无关的非确定性，通过对规格上的不可信确定性函数进行存在性参数化来处理（这些函数仅作用于公开数据）。子模块相对于各自的泄漏感知规格进行证明，并通过组合获得整个设计的保证。通过与认证静态分析组合，消除包括ISA契约在内的所有中间规格，从可信计算基中剔除。

Result: 针对含推测、精确中断和I/O的流水线RISC设计，证明了逐周期时序仅由ISA泄漏契约中指定的可观测量决定。对遵循密码学常数时间纪律（可观测量独立于秘密）的程序，排除了通过已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。最终推导出关于硬件-软件密码实现的逐周期机密性的单一Rocq定理。

Conclusion: Granite是首个实现ISA级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行（含线级观察）之间模块化、基础性连接的工作，其证明可通过组合紧凑到单一Rocq定理，消除了所有中间规格的可信计算基依赖。

Abstract: Granite is a methodology for modular verification of both functional correctness and nonleakage of RTL processors against ISA contracts. We prove that the cycle-by-cycle timing of a pipelined RISC design--with speculation, precise interrupts, and I/O--is determined solely by observables specified in an ISA leakage contract. For programs that keep observables independent of secrets (i.e., following the cryptographic-constant-time discipline), this result rules out information leakage through known and unknown timing side channels.
  Granite's specifications only constrain functional correctness and information-flow dependencies: not how many cycles an instruction takes, at which instruction an interrupt is handled, or the exact latencies of submodules such as multipliers and memory. Granite's central technique is leakage-aware refinement via determinism, which establishes correctness and confidentiality together as trace equivalence with respect to a family of cycle-level, deterministic spec machines. Secret-independent nondeterminism is handled by existentially parameterizing specs with untrusted, deterministic functions acting only on public data. Submodules are proved against their own leakage-aware specs, and these proofs compose into the whole-design guarantee--which therefore holds over a space of secure implementations.
  We believe this work is the first to achieve modular and foundational connection between instruction-set-level leakage contracts and microarchitecture-specific cycle-by-cycle execution with wire-level observations. Our proofs compose with a certified static analysis that recognizes cryptographic-constant-time code to derive a single Rocq theorem about the cycle-by-cycle confidentiality of a hardware-and-software cryptographic implementation--eliminating every intermediate specification, including the ISA contract itself, from the trusted computing base.

</details>


### [232] [Flock: Fast Proving for Batch Boolean Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27491)
*Benedikt Bünz,Ron D. Rothblum,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flock是一种基于哈希的SNARK系统，专为大规模布尔计算的快速证明而设计，特别针对SHA-256、Keccak和BLAKE3等标准加密哈希函数的批量求值。它通过新优化的lincheck和zerocheck协议，结合由编码智能体共同设计的激进优化实现，在单核M4 Max上每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销低于原生执行的250倍；十核并行下吞吐量超660k次BLAKE3压缩/秒，比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多SNARK应用的关键瓶颈在于证明大批量的标准加密哈希函数求值（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3），现有SNARK系统在此类布尔计算上的证明速度不够快，成为实际部署的主要障碍。

Method: Flock组合了以下关键技术：(1) 针对lincheck和zerocheck协议提出新的优化；(2) 用于证明同一R1CS电路批次的专用设计，支持哈希链和Merkle路径开证明；(3) 与编码智能体（coding agents）协同设计的激进优化原型实现。

Result: 在M4 Max单核上，Flock每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销低于原生执行250倍；十核吞吐超660k BLAKE3压缩/秒；SHA-256证明比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。

Conclusion: Flock证明了通过对底层协议（lincheck/zerocheck）进行专门优化，并借助编码智能体实现高性能代码，基于哈希的SNARK可以在批量布尔计算（尤其是加密哈希求值）上达到极为出色的证明速度，大幅超越现有方案。

Abstract: For many applications of SNARKs, a key bottleneck is proving large batches of standard cryptographic hash evaluations, such as SHA-256, Keccak, or BLAKE3. We introduce Flock, a hash-based SNARK for extremely fast proving of such batched Boolean computations. Flock proves batches of the same R1CS circuit (plus input/output relations between them), can prove hash-chains and Merkle path openings, and in principle can be extended to full-fledged hash-based signature verification. At its core, Flock combines new optimizations for the lincheck and zerocheck protocols with an aggressively optimized proof-of-concept implementation co-designed by coding agents.
  On a single core of an M4 Max processor, Flock proves 82k evaluations of the BLAKE3 compression function, 42k SHA-256 compressions, and 30k Keccak permutations per second --- less than a $250\times$ overhead over native execution. On ten cores, throughput exceeds 660k BLAKE3 compressions per second; in proving SHA-256, Flock is more than $9\times$ faster than Binius64, the prior state of the art, and more than $500\times$ faster than the fastest elliptic curve-based SNARK we measured against.

</details>


### [233] [Send and Pretend: Exploiting Transcript Consistency Issues in End-to-End Encrypted Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27510)
*Gabriel K. Gegenhuber,Moritz Grefner,Maximilian Günther,Matthäus Wininger,David Schmidt,Aljosha Judmayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了主流端到端加密（E2EE）消息应用（如Threema、WhatsApp、Signal和iMessage）在群聊中无法保证"对话一致性"（Transcript Consistency, TC）的问题。研究者证明，恶意群成员可以通过选择性省略、重排序或伪造内容，向不同接收者展示不同的对话视图，而不会触发任何用户界面警告。论文识别了多种"双向歧义向量"（equivocation vectors），并展示了具体攻击场景（如问卷操控、社会工程学、规避审核），同时提出了低开销的缓解措施和UI信号策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Signal和iMessage等主流E2EE应用引入群聊投票功能，用户自然期望所有参与者看到一致的对话结果。然而，现有E2EE协议在恶意参与者威胁模型下并未保证"对话一致性"（TC），这为攻击者提供了操控群聊视图的机会，威胁到群聊的完整性和公平性。本文的动机在于系统性地揭示这一问题并评估其严重程度。

Method: 研究在恶意参与者威胁模型下，系统地考察了主流E2EE消息应用（Threema、WhatsApp、Signal、iMessage）的群聊协议。作者从协议回退路径、成对（pairwise）投递通道等多个维度识别了"双向歧义向量"，并通过具体的攻击场景（如投票操控、社会工程、规避审核）进行实证演示。此外，还发现了具有隐私影响的实现特定行为（如设备操作系统指纹识别），最后将发现与既有TC研究进行对照分析。

Result: 研究发现所有被考察的主流E2EE消息应用均无法在群聊中保证任何形式的TC。恶意群成员能够在不触发用户警告的情况下，向不同接收者选择性省略、重排序或修改对话内容，甚至在不同接收者之间展示不一致的投票结果。此外还发现了跨服务的协议设计缺陷以及实现层面的隐私泄露问题（如设备OS指纹识别）。

Conclusion: E2EE群聊协议普遍缺乏对话一致性保证是一个系统性设计缺陷，而非个别实现问题。作者提出了实用的低开销缓解方法和UI信号策略，可集成到现有E2EE群协议中，以增强群聊的完整性和用户对对话视图一致性的信任。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted (E2EE) messaging apps are widely praised for their security and thus also used for sensitive coordination in group chats (e.g., by political decision makers). After Threema and WhatsApp, also Signal and iMessage have recently introduced polls to aid agreement processes in groups. This implicitly sets the expectation that all participants see the same outcome and thus have the same view of the conversation. This property is commonly referred to as transcript consistency (TC). In this work, we demonstrate that today's major E2EE messengers do not guarantee any form of TC for group chats, allowing a malicious group member to selectively omit, reorder, or present altered content to different recipients without triggering warnings in their user interface. We systematically investigate the extent of the problem under a malicious-participant threat model that targets the integrity of the shared transcript, or inconsistent delivery across a user's linked devices. We identify multiple equivocation vectors that range from protocol fallback paths to deliberate use of pairwise delivery channels within groups. We demonstrate concrete exploitation scenarios such as social engineering, evading moderation, and, in particular, rigging polls. Beyond these cross-service design issues, we also uncover implementation-specific behaviors with privacy implications (e.g., device OS fingerprinting). Finally, we contextualize our findings within prior transcript-consistency research and outline practical low-overhead mitigations and UI signaling strategies that can be integrated into state-of-the-art E2EE group protocols.

</details>


### [234] [ThreatForest: Multi-Agent Attack Tree Generation with Pluggable TTP Framework Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27528)
*Cristian Leo,Anton Dykyi,Danny Cortegaca,Daniel Begimher,Prakash Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatForest是一个多智能体系统，能够从源代码仓库自动生成结构化的攻击树，将攻击步骤映射到MITRE ATT&CK、CAPEC和云特定威胁矩阵等对手框架的TTP，并综合可操作的缓解措施。系统采用多阶段智能体流水线（仓库分析、上下文细化、威胁生成、并行攻击树构建、报告生成），以有向图编排并包含确定性验证门和人工介入点。研究发现嵌入阶段（而非流水线整体）是准确性的主导瓶颈：威胁语句、攻击树和缓解措施的质量评分达到0.63-0.68，而仅嵌入的TTP映射仅0.29，且此差距在所有七个领域中稳定存在。这是第一个将代码仓库端到端转化为跨对手框架的TTP映射攻击树及基于证据的缓解措施的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁建模对安全软件开发和云原生架构的安全性至关重要，但传统的手动分析方法速度慢且依赖稀缺的安全专家。现有的威胁建模方法无法高效地从源代码仓库自动生成结构化的攻击树并将其映射到标准的对手战术、技术和程序框架，亟需一种自动化、可扩展且具备评估基准的系统来应对这一挑战。

Method: ThreatForest采用多智能体架构，将威胁建模分解为多阶段流水线：仓库分析、上下文细化、威胁生成、并行攻击树构建（含TTP映射和缓解措施综合）以及报告生成。整个流水线以有向图编排，包含确定性验证门、有界重试和三个人工介入验证点。TTP映射采用领域特定的句子Transformer，通过余弦相似度将每个攻击步骤映射到候选技术。评估使用七种应用领域、十六维评分规则，由独立LLM评分器配合对抗验证和专家审查进行。

Result: 在16维评分规则上，ThreatForest在威胁语句、攻击树和缓解措施上的面板测得质量达到0.63-0.68（0-1尺度），但嵌入-only的TTP映射仅0.29，且该差距在所有七个领域中稳定存在。对照实验表明，单一调用的基线模型在相同模型下将映射辩护性提升了一倍以上，证明瓶颈在于嵌入编码器而非多智能体设计本身。

Conclusion: ThreatForest是首个端到端将代码仓库转化为跨对手框架的TTP映射攻击树及基于证据的缓解措施的系统。其核心发现是嵌入式编码器是TTP映射准确性的关键瓶颈，这一发现为未来改进指明了方向，同时该工作也提供了一个可复用的基准测试框架，用于评估此类威胁建模系统。

Abstract: Threat modeling is essential for secure software development, yet manual analysis of cloud-native architectures is slow and demands scarce security expertise. We present ThreatForest, a multi-agent system that generates structured attack trees from source code repositories, maps attack steps to adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from a pluggable set of frameworks (MITRE ATT&CK, CAPEC, and cloud-specific threat matrices), and synthesizes actionable mitigations. ThreatForest decomposes threat modeling into a multi-stage agent pipeline -- repository analysis, context refinement, threat generation, parallel attack-tree construction with TTP mapping and mitigation synthesis, and report generation -- orchestrated as a directed graph with deterministic verification gates, bounded retries, and three human-in-the-loop validation points. A domain-specific sentence-transformer maps each attack step to candidate techniques by cosine similarity; we show empirically that this embedding stage, not the surrounding pipeline, is the dominant accuracy bottleneck. We evaluate ThreatForest across seven application domains on a sixteen-dimension rubric, scored by a panel of independent LLM raters with an adversarial verification pass and expert review. Panel-measured quality reaches 0.63-0.68 (on a 0-1 scale) for threat statements, attack trees, and mitigations, but only 0.29 for embedding-only TTP mapping -- a gap stable across all seven domains that isolates the binding constraint. A controlled single-call baseline on the same model more than doubles mapping defensibility, pinning the limitation on the embedding encoder rather than the multi-agent design. To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [235] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AnchorMark是一种无需训练的鲁棒反演式水印方法，利用"旋转同步性"这一潜在空间性质，在初始潜在空间中嵌入同步锚点，从而在提取时精准估计并纠正旋转角度，有效提升复合有损后处理下的比特准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反演式水印虽保留了生成图像的视觉保真度，但在面对复合有损后处理（尤其是旋转）时仍然脆弱，因为旋转破坏了潜在空间解码所需的空问对应关系。

Method: 揭示潜在空间中的"旋转同步性"性质：图像域的旋转与恢复的初始潜在中的对应旋转共享相同角度。基于此性质，AnchorMark在初始潜在的中心区域嵌入同步锚点，用于提取时准确估计和纠正旋转角度。

Result: 实验表明，AnchorMark在旋转攻击和复合攻击下显著提高了比特准确率，且对图像质量影响有限。

Conclusion: AnchorMark成功克服了反演式水印在旋转攻击下的脆弱性，提供了一种训练免费、鲁棒性强的水印方案。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [236] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于图神经网络的交通流量预测系统的鲁棒性问题，指出现有对抗攻击与防御研究基于不现实的威胁模型和未目标化的目标。作者引入了一种物理感知的针对性攻击，该攻击仅操纵少量传感器即可在特定链路上放大预测错误而对全局影响极小，并提出了一个物理信息检测器作为防御，在15个模型-数据集组合中有13个超过对抗训练基线，且在未见攻击上优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测对抗鲁棒性评估基于不切实际的威胁模型（如全局范数受限攻击）和非目标化目标，无法反映真实攻击者的能力与意图。实际攻击者仅具备有限模型知识，能监控和操纵少量传感器，且攻击目标是局部的（如导致特定路段错误预计到达时间或不必要的绕行）。需要重新定义攻击和防御。

Method: （1）提出物理感知的针对性攻击，模拟真实拥堵模式，可限局于特定链路；（2）将鲁棒性重新框定为检测问题：训练一个物理信息检测器，其输出作为额外特征输入到加固后的预测器中；（3）在固定预测器的情况下，针对自适应攻击训练检测器；（4）在多种模型架构和数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: 物理感知攻击使目标链路误差放大数倍而网络整体误差几乎不变；传统对抗训练（针对范数受限扰动）对此类攻击几乎无效。提出的检测-缓解防御在13/15个模型-数据集设置中优于甚至对抗物理感知攻击本身训练的对抗训练，在保留攻击上优势最大，且干净数据上的性能几乎无损失。

Conclusion: 研究强调必须在应用特定约束下审视抽象的AI对抗攻击，才能评估其真实安全影响。物理学感知的攻击和检测-缓解防御范式比传统的范数受限对抗训练更符合实际部署场景。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [237] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SubCASP——一种基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的方法，通过融合入侵检测系统（IDS）数据日志，推断IEC 61850数字变电站网络攻击的当前阶段、下一阶段及回溯攻击路径，以弥补现有IDS因缺乏攻击阶段上下文而难以部署缓解措施的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEC 61850数字变电站虽提升了电力系统运行效率，但攻击者可利用其通信协议操纵断路器操作，造成严重系统影响。这些网络攻击基于多阶段策略展开，而现有IDS往往仅标记孤立的攻击症状，缺乏攻击阶段的上下文信息，无法有效支持缓解措施的部署。

Method: SubCASP采用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），融合IDS数据日志来推断当前攻击阶段、预测下一攻击阶段并回溯攻击路径。攻击阶段来源于基于ATT&CK框架的威胁建模。模型在可复现的攻击图数据集上进行了训练和评估。

Result: 测试结果表明SubCASP在不同的IDS可观测性水平以及IDS数据日志缺失场景下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubCASP通过HMM对多阶段攻击进行阶段性推理，为数字变电站提供了具备攻击上下文的检测能力，能够有效支持安全团队在攻击过程中部署针对性的缓解措施。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [238] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了分布式点函数（DPF）的定义、构造和应用，并讨论其向函数秘密共享（FSS）的推广，指出高效的FSS方案可将DPF的大多数应用进行类似推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF作为一种轻量级密码原语，能够实现秘密权重-1向量的压缩加性共享，在私有信息检索、匿名消息传递、带预处理的保密计算以及伪随机相关生成器等领域具有广泛应用。由于DPF重要性日益凸显，需要一个系统性的综述来梳理其定义、构造与应用，并探讨其向更一般函数类的推广（即FSS）。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，系统梳理DPF的形式化定义、现有构造方案及其在各应用场景中的使用方式，并进一步讨论DPF向函数秘密共享（FSS）的扩展，分析FSS如何将点函数推广至更丰富的函数类，以及高效FSS方案如何继承DPF的大部分应用能力。

Result: 文章全面总结了DPF的构造与应用现状，并指出FSS作为DPF的推广，能够支持更丰富的函数类；高效的FSS方案使得DPF的大多数应用（如PIR、匿名通信、预处理安全计算和伪随机相关生成器）可以相应推广到更广泛的函数场景。

Conclusion: DPF是一类具有轻量级结构和广泛应用的密码原语；其推广形式FSS能够支撑更丰富的函数类，并且可以复用DPF的大部分应用场景，为该领域未来的理论研究和工程实践奠定了基础。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [239] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CHARGE是一个自动化框架，利用CWE分级语义和大型语言模型为未验证的RTL模块生成安全属性。在Hack@DAC18/19/21的SoC设计上，它检测到42个已知bug中的27个，并发现了1个此前未识别的新bug。在Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton上，89%的生成SVA可在JasperGold中运行，92.2%非空洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件安全属性生成通常依赖可信的设计规格或大量人工工程，成本高且覆盖面有限。作者希望利用CWE条目的层次化特性来引导LLM识别未验证RTL模块中的安全关键资产并推断安全行为，从而无需可信规格即可自动生成安全属性。

Method: CHARGE框架通过两步推理过程工作：(1)利用CWE条目的层次化结构，在未验证RTL模块中识别安全关键资产（如寄存器、数据通路等）；(2)从已识别的资产和CWE语义中推断预期安全行为，并使用LLM（OpenAI GPT-4.1）生成形式化的SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA)属性。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18、19、21的开放源码SoC设计上评估：检测到42个已知bug中的27个；Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton中89%的生成SVA可在Cadence JasperGold FPV中运行，92.2%非空洞；与手工编写的开源属性对比，CHARGE为3个手工属性写错的bug正确生成了属性，并额外发现了1个此前未知的新bug。

Conclusion: CHARGE证明了结合CWE层次化语义与LLM可以在无可信设计规格的情况下有效生成硬件安全属性，检测漏洞的能力与手工属性互补甚至更优，并能发现新的安全缺陷，显著减少人工工程投入。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出NANOEDGEGUARD，一种面向医疗物联网边缘网关的内核平面闭环控制器，通过内核流量控制钩子强制执行多级速率策略，利用滞回机制实现快速恢复，相比用户空间防火墙基线将报警RTT的P99降低13.3%，并将多余突发流量削减46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网服务依赖边缘网关转发常规遥测数据与罕见但时间关键的报警消息。即使短时流量突发也可能放大最坏情况延迟并干扰紧急消息，现有静态防火墙规则或用户空间控制环路响应迟缓，难以在突发场景下保障报警的及时性。

Method: 设计NANOEDGEGUARD内核平面闭环控制器：在每个数据源观测流量强度，利用内核流量控制钩子强制执行可审计的多级速率策略；通过滞回（hysteresis）机制实现快速动作与显式恢复，并记录策略转换日志以支持审计。实验环境为搭载MQTT代理的Raspberry Pi网关，配两个ESP32端点产生生命体征、报警及时序突发流量。

Result: 相比用户空间防火墙基线，自适应内核平面速率控制将报警RTT的99分位延迟降低13.3%，且保持与无实施时相同的RTT水平；相比无实施场景，多余准入的突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 早期结果表明，网关处的自适应、可审计执行可提升医疗物联网的韧性，未来可扩展至按需策略部署的边缘智能场景。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [241] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
*Jin-Seong Kim,Han-Ju Lee,Seok-Won Hong,Takeshi Takahashi,Chansu Han,Tomohiro Morikawa,Seok-Hwan Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GradLock，一种训练时注入攻击方法，通过在模型参数中秘密嵌入敏感训练数据，实现像素级完美的数据重建和即时提取，且对量化、剪枝和微调等部署优化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演（MI）攻击大多依赖训练后优化来恢复私有数据，但受限于目标模型的泛化瓶颈，往往只能恢复通用特征而非特定身份，尤其是在高维数据集上效果不佳。作者旨在突破这一局限，探索训练时注入攻击的可能性。

Method: GradLock采用两种核心机制：（1）无状态确定性索引建立隔离的数据保险库（data vaults）；（2）动态梯度锁定防止优化过程中有效载荷退化。攻击在供应链受损害的上下文中运行，可在无需保留训练环境访问权限的情况下从最终模型中提取像素级完美数据。

Result: 在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA上的实验表明，GradLock实现近无损重建（SSIM约1.0）和即时提取（小于1.0秒）。相比现有训练时注入方法，对量化、剪枝和微调等标准部署优化表现出更强的鲁棒性。用户部署研究显示93.3%的参与者未能检测到恶意逻辑。

Conclusion: GradLock揭示了现代AI供应链中严重的安全盲区，表明训练时注入攻击能够实现远超传统模型反演攻击的攻击效果，且具有高度的隐蔽性，对现有防御机制构成重大威胁。

Abstract: Existing model inversion (MI) attacks predominantly rely on post-training optimization to recover private data from model outputs. However, these methods are fundamentally constrained by the target model's generalization bottleneck, often yielding generic features rather than specific identities, particularly on high-dimensional datasets. In this paper, we introduce GradLock, a novel training-time injection attack that stealthily injects sensitive training data directly into the model parameters. Operating within a compromised supply chain context, GradLock leverages stateless deterministic indexing to establish isolated data vaults and employs dynamic gradient locking to prevent payload degradation during the optimization process. This mechanism allows the adversary to extract pixel-perfect data from the final model without retaining access to the training environment. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Imagenette, and CelebA demonstrate that GradLock achieves near-lossless reconstruction (SSIM ~ 1.0) and instant extraction (< 1.0s). Compared to existing training-time injection methods, our approach exhibits superior robustness against standard deployment optimizations, including quantization, pruning, and fine-tuning. Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [242] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双生成器学习的干净标签"未学习激活后门"攻击框架，通过双层优化协同建立持久潜在关联（触发器-目标）与可移除抑制（伪装样本），使后门在训练后保持休眠、仅在少量数据被遗忘后被激活。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上验证了该方法能在多个遗忘算法下实现低攻击前成功率与强攻击后激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击在植入后立即生效，容易被防御检测暴露。未学习激活的潜伏后门虽然能避免这种暴露，但现有方法难以在干净标签约束和真实遗忘请求下同时实现低植入后攻击成功率与强遗忘后激活效果。这需要同时建立持久的潜在关联和可移除的抑制影响，是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出基于双生成器学习的干净标签未学习激活后门框架，将其形式化为双层优化问题：一个生成器学习样本特定触发器以建立潜在触发-目标关联，另一个生成器学习标签一致的伪装样本以提供可移除的抑制。通过模拟潜在后门建立和机器遗忘过程进行交替学习，当少量伪装样本被遗忘后抑制被解除，潜伏后门被激活。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多个未学习算法下比代表性后门基线实现了更低的植入前攻击成功率（保持潜伏）和更强的遗忘后攻击激活效果，验证了通过协调持久潜在关联与可移除抑制能够实现可靠的休眠到激活转换。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在干净标签和现实删除约束下，通过协调持久潜在关联与可移除抑制可以实现可靠的休眠-激活转换，为后门攻击的隐蔽性与触发可控性提供了新的设计范式，同时也警示深度学习模型在数据删除场景下存在新型安全风险。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [243] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型安全防护中一个根本性问题：模型在答案使用前就决定是否响应，而攻击者可以模仿良性请求和交互历史。作者从理论上推导出可复制证据下攻击者援助的下限，得出"有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问"三者不可兼得的安全三难困境，并提出可信凭证可作为补充方案来打破该困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全防护机制（safeguards）在回答前就做出决策，无法预先知晓答案将被如何使用。对于双用途（dual-use）任务，同一答案可能帮助合法专业人士也可能帮助攻击者，而攻击者完全可以模仿良性请求模式，使得基于对话历史的防护失效。

Method: 作者将模型释放的能力与下游使用的证据分开分析，对可复制证据场景进行了理论推导，给出攻击者援助的精确最坏情况下限。随后提出通过可信凭证（trusted credential）引入难以复制的信息来预测真实下游使用，并给出了消除该下限所需满足的更强条件。

Result: 理论推导得出安全三难困境：有用能力、可靠安全与开放访问三者无法同时满足。可信凭证方案可以提供现有防护之外的互补能力，通过增加难以复制的信息来突破可复制证据导致的援助下限。来自双用途评估、自适应攻击和部署中的可信访问项目的实证证据支持了这些条件的实际相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究从理论上阐明了LLM安全防护的根本局限，指出仅依赖对话内容和可复制证据无法实现可靠安全与开放访问的兼得，需要引入可信凭证等外部硬信息来弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [244] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究脉冲神经网络（SNN）在原生事件驱动二进制输入下的"海绵攻击"（sponge attack）安全威胁。作者提出两种攻击模型：（1）基于梯度优化的逐样本海绵攻击，可将每次推理的突触操作数（SynOps）提升1.5-2.6倍，同时保持至少98%样本的预测类别不变；（2）首次提出面向原生事件驱动SNN输入的通用海绵攻击——离线计算固定二进制扰动，通过XOR应用到所有后续输入，可将SynOps提升1.09-1.24倍。在NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture三个数据集及三个SNN模型上验证有效性，估算在Loihi-1硬件上的能耗开销为14μJ到13.24mJ。结果表明事件驱动SNN易受输入空间效率攻击，且可重复使用的通用扰动可在持续部署的边缘系统中累积成显著的电池消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN通过稀疏二进制脉冲事件通信实现低能耗推理，被广泛应用于常开型电池供电的边缘系统。这种效率优势同时引入了独特的安全风险：海绵攻击可以通过增加推理时的脉冲活动和突触工作量来膨胀能耗，而仅靠基于正确性的监控难以检测。以往对SNN的输入空间效率攻击仅限于速率编码（rate-coded）设置下的逐样本优化，缺乏对原生事件驱动二进制输入和通用（universal）攻击的研究。

Method: 本文研究了两种攻击模型：（1）逐样本海绵攻击：针对每个输入样本通过梯度优化定制对抗性脉冲序列，最大化脉冲活动以增加SynOps；（2）通用海绵攻击：离线计算一个固定的二进制扰动，通过XOR操作应用到所有后续输入，无需逐输入优化。两种攻击均在原生事件驱动二进制输入上实现，并在三个SNN模型上对NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture三个数据集进行验证。SynOps膨胀进一步映射到Loihi-1硬件的能量估算。

Result: 逐样本海绵攻击将每次推理的SynOps提升1.5-2.6倍，同时在至少98%的评估样本上保持预测类别不变。新提出的通用海绵攻击将SynOps提升1.09-1.24倍，虽然在效果上弱于逐样本攻击，但因无需逐输入优化而更符合实际部署威胁。映射到Loihi-1能耗后，每次推理的开销为14μJ至13.24mJ。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动的SNN对输入空间效率攻击存在实际漏洞。尽管逐样本攻击效果更强，但通用海绵攻击因其可复用性（固定扰动可反复使用）和无需逐输入计算的特点，在持续部署的边缘系统中构成了更现实的威胁，能够累积成显著的电池功耗消耗。这凸显了在事件驱动SNN安全设计中需要关注效率维度的防御措施。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [245] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并评估了针对脉冲神经网络（SNN）的干净标签时间投毒后门攻击。该方法仅对目标类训练流施加固定时间戳变换而不改变标签，通过保持逐像素、逐极性的事件计数不变，使得时间聚合后的干净样本与触发样本无法区分，但SNN处理的序列顺序已被改变。在三个神经形态数据集和卷积/Transformer两种架构上，最强配置下攻击成功率（ASR）达1.00。作者同时研究了投毒预算和触发形状的影响，并评估了现有后门防御方法在该场景下的表现——塌缩时间轴的速率类防御方法天然失效，特征空间方法仅在特定设置下有效。作者提出的基于逐步骤事件质量的无模型检测器能够检测所评估的时间变换，揭示了攻击的隐蔽性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SNN后门攻击主要基于脏标签投毒假设，即触发样本被重新标记为攻击者选择的类别。真实场景中干净标签攻击更具隐蔽性和现实威胁，但此前尚未有工作在SNN上评估干净标签后门攻击。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索时间域变换在SNN后门攻击中的潜力及其防御边界。

Method: 攻击方法：对目标类训练流施加固定的时间戳变换（temporal transformation），保持标签不变；变换精确保持逐像素、逐极性的事件数量，使时间聚合后的样本与干净样本在特征上不可区分，但改变了SNN处理的事件序列。防御评估：采用投毒预算（poison-budget）和触发形状（trigger-shape）消融实验分析攻击特性；评估现有后门防御方法（包括时间轴塌缩类速率防御和特征空间方法）在SNN上的适应性。检测方法：提出基于逐步骤事件质量（per-step event mass）的无模型检测器。

Result: 在三个神经形态数据集上，卷积和Transformer两种受害者架构下，最强配置的攻击成功率（ASR）达到1.00。消融实验揭示了投毒预算和触发形状对攻击效果的影响规律。防御评估显示：塌缩时间轴的防御方法在构造上无法检测该攻击，特征空间方法仅在特定设置下能检测投毒。提出的无模型检测器成功检测了所评估的时间变换，展示了攻击隐蔽性的边界。

Conclusion: 这是首个在SNN和神经形态事件数据上评估的干净标签后门攻击。研究证明时间域操作可以在保持标签和事件统计数据完全不变的情况下实现高成功率后门攻击，同时揭示了速率塌缩类防御的固有盲区，并验证了基于事件时间分布的检测方法的可行性。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [246] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化方法分析AWS IoT Core的访问控制策略，通过构建信息流图并利用SMT求解器验证设备间是否存在不期望的信息流动，从而识别潜在安全漏洞。实现工具为IOT:POKER，在真实场景和多个真实策略上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务商（如AWS IoT Core）提供的IoT访问控制机制需要正确配置才能保证安全，但在设备具有不同信任级别或分属不同子系统的场景下，仅验证单个权限是不够的。需要识别由访问控制策略导致的不期望的设备间信息流动所产生的安全漏洞。

Method: 1. 对AWS IoT Core的组件进行形式化建模；2. 定义信息流图（information flow graph）来刻画访问控制策略所允许的设备间通信；3. 利用SMT求解器构建信息流图的有限表示，从而支持设备间信息流的验证；4. 在工具IOT:POKER中实现该方法。

Result: 该方法在IOT:POKER工具中实现，并在一个现实场景和多个真实世界的访问控制策略上进行了评估，能够有效识别信息流相关的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过形式化建模和SMT求解器支持的有限图表示，该方法可以系统地验证IoT访问控制策略中的信息流安全问题，弥补了传统权限隔离验证的不足。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [247] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，研究自主多智能体系统（AMAS）中推理时harness的IP泄露安全问题，通过黑盒交互提取目标智能体的harness能力，并提出了基于欺骗的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的工作只研究了静态多智能体系统中预配置架构的IP泄露，但AMAS中harness行为在推理过程中动态涌现，是否面临类似风险尚不清楚。推理时harness是重要的知识产权，需要系统性地研究这一安全威胁。

Method: AHD框架包含两个阶段：预蒸馏阶段（pre-distillation）从目标智能体的响应中推断harness行为并构建初始harness；后蒸馏阶段（post-distillation）通过迭代优化使初始harness与目标智能体的行为模式对齐。该框架通过黑盒交互实现，不依赖对目标系统的内部访问。

Result: 在多个真实AMAS和多种骨干LLM上的实验表明AHD能够有效提取harness能力，揭示了显著的IP泄露风险。同时提出的基于欺骗的防御方法能够在降低harness提取有效性的同时保持受保护智能体的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了AMAS中一个此前未被充分探索的安全威胁——推理时harness的提取导致的IP泄露，并提供了量化评估框架和防御策略，为后续AMAS安全工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [248] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击，提出指令增强与场景隐藏技术使恶意音频指令可"搭便车"于用户语音，构建了首个综合基准AudioAgentSecurity（8个真实任务场景、10种攻击模式），并引入基于声源分离与一致性分析的级联音频解耦验证（CADV）防御机制，在11个SOTA智能体上验证了攻击效果与防御有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多模态智能体通过连续音频交互执行自主任务日益普及，但音频输入天然包含用户无法控制的环境噪声，这引入了此前未被充分探索的攻击面。传统的声学攻击研究主要针对语音设备，亟需系统研究针对多模态智能体的音频提示注入攻击及其防御。

Method: 提出指令增强与场景隐藏两种新型攻击技术，使恶意音频指令可隐蔽地叠加在用户语音上劫持智能体执行恶意操作；构建首个综合基准AudioAgentSecurity，涵盖8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式；提出CADV防御机制，利用声源分离和跨模态一致性分析来检测音频指令注入。

Result: 在11个SOTA智能体（包括Gemini 3 Pro和GPT-4o-audio）上评估，攻击方法对Gemini 3 Pro的平均攻击成功率（ASR）达69.10%；CADV防御在不同攻击向量上检测成功率超过90%；在豆包AI智能手机上的真实世界实验证实了攻击的高隐蔽性与有效性以及防御的可靠性。

Conclusion: 音频提示注入攻击对多模态智能体构成严重且现实的安全威胁，现有提示级防御不够鲁棒，基于声源分离与跨模态一致性分析的CADV防线能有效缓解此类漏洞，为多模态智能体的音频安全提供了系统性的评估基准与防御框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [249] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向临床EEG数据的隐私保护联邦学习框架，以基于掩码的安全聚合为核心机制，结合图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗掉线聚合、更新裁剪、布隆过滤器隐私记录链接及辅助公证人可验证性，支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置，并在Flower框架及TUH EEG数据上进行了跨机构模拟评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多机构在不交换原始临床EEG数据的情况下训练共享模型，但单个模型的更新仍可能泄露隐私信息，现有方法未能完全防止这种泄露。因此需要在联邦学习框架中引入更强的隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出以掩码技术为基础的安全聚合框架，核心组件包括：基于图的通信机制、阈值秘密共享、抗掉线聚合、本地更新裁剪、可选的布隆过滤器隐私保护记录链接初始化模块，以及基于辅助公证人的可验证性机制。框架同时支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置，并使用Flower联邦学习框架实现，在模拟跨机构医疗场景下基于TUH EEG数据评估。

Result: 安全聚合变体在声明假设下可对聚合服务器隐藏个体更新，且与联邦模型训练兼容。恶意设置的安全保障和轻量级一致性检查机制会引入额外的计算、通信和轮次时长开销。半诚实变体开销最低，恶意和辅助公证人变体以更高成本提供更强的一致性、完整性和轻量级验证支持。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了基于掩码的安全聚合在临床EEG联邦学习中的可行性和隐私保护有效性，但需在隐私强度与性能开销之间进行权衡，不同安全设置适用于不同威胁模型和实际需求场景。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [250] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统调查了具身AI世界模型（world models）的安全威胁，跨越从数据构建到执行再到长期适应的完整生命周期，提出了生命周期分类法、攻击映射、评估协议与防御框架，并揭示世界模型的双重性——既可充当安全屏障，也可能在受损或过度信任时产生"预测性安全幻觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供预测核心，但这一预测层开辟了新的安全边界：数据、传感器、提示或反馈中的妥协可能传播到物理动作中。现有研究往往将世界模型视为孤立组件，缺乏对其全生命周期安全威胁的系统性理解。

Method: 采用调查（survey）方法：提出生命周期分类法，从数据构建与表示学习、状态接地与想象、轨迹评估与执行、以及通过记忆和工具的长期适应等阶段追踪威胁；将既有攻击家族（投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击）映射到世界模型的安全属性；设计安全失败评估协议；构建涵盖来源验证、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障的防御体系。

Result: 揭示了七大类熟悉攻击家族在世界模型语境下被破坏的特定对象（世界状态、学习动力学、可用性估计、安全成本）；指出世界模型可作为运行时安全屏障，但受损或过度信任时会生成预测性安全幻觉；提供了完整的安全威胁分类、攻击-属性映射关系、评估协议与结构化防御方案。

Conclusion: 世界模型安全需要从孤立的模型视角转向全生命周期视角，其同时作为安全机制与攻击面的双重角色要求审慎的信任管理，防御策略应在来源验证、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障等多层面协同设计。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [251] [Implementing Homomorphic Encryption-Based Logic Locking in System-on-Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28542)
*Ye Ziyang,Makoto Ikeda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二进制环学习误差（Ring-LWE）算法的逻辑锁定方案，应用于RISC-V系统级芯片设计中，用于保护特权切换过程中的关键逻辑路径，无需向用户暴露原始锁定参数。该模块消耗3519个查找表和2645个寄存器，整体开销为查找表6.0%、寄存器6.9%；解锁过程约需2.6微秒，对系统级操作产生适度性能影响，但保持了用户级计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑锁定方法需要向用户提供原始锁定参数，存在参数暴露导致的安全风险。本研究旨在通过基于二进制Ring-LWE算法的方案，在不暴露敏感参数的前提下，保护RISC-V SoC中特权切换过程的关键逻辑路径。

Method: 采用二进制Ring-LWE算法实现逻辑锁定机制，并将其集成到RISC-V System-on-Chip设计中。锁定模块重点防护特权切换过程的关键逻辑路径，解锁过程约需2.6微秒。

Result: 锁定模块消耗3519个查找表和2645个寄存器，与基线系统相比产生6.0%的查找表开销和6.9%的寄存器开销。解锁时间约2.6微秒，性能影响主要体现在系统级操作，用户级计算效率得以保持。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于二进制Ring-LWE的逻辑锁定方案在硬件开销可控（6%左右）的前提下，实现了对特权切换关键路径的安全保护，避免了传统方法中锁定参数暴露的问题，同时仅在系统级操作中引入有限的性能开销，是一种可行的安全增强方案。

Abstract: This study presents a logic locking scheme based on the binary Ring Learning With Errors algorithm, implemented in a RISC-V System-on-Chip design. Unlike traditional logic locking methods that require providing users with raw locking parameters, the proposed approach secures critical logic paths in the privilege switching process without exposing these sensitive parameters. The implemented locking module itself consumes 3519 Look-Up Tables and 2645 Registers, leading to an overall overhead of 6.0% in Look-Up Tables and 6.9% in Registers compared to the baseline system. The unlock process requires about 2.6 us, introducing moderate performance impact, primarily affecting system-level operations while preserving user-level computational efficiency.

</details>


### [252] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了证明助手在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学领域的应用，强调其用于验证设计和实现满足预期安全属性，并支持认证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手被广泛用于验证设计和实现是否满足预期安全属性，同时支持安全认证。本文旨在系统梳理证明助手在这些关键安全领域的应用。

Method: 本文是一章综述性文章，覆盖四个主要领域：系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译和密码学，讨论证明助手在各领域中的应用方式与价值。

Result: 本章概述了证明助手在多个安全领域的应用场景，包括验证系统安全属性、语言安全属性、安全编译的正确性以及密码学协议的形式化验证。

Conclusion: 证明助手不仅用于验证安全属性，还作为支持安全认证的重要工具，在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学等方向发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究固定表示神经网络在合并数据池上重拟合时，是否保留CBDT组合公理所要求的源偏好排序，提出Gram不匹配度量、几何导向正则化及三阶段审计框架，在多领域代理任务上验证了组合可靠性可测量且可操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络越来越多地跨群体、时间段和运行条件合并数据进行训练以提升泛化能力，但合并后模型是否保留各源数据共同支持的动作排序这一可靠性问题尚未被系统研究。CBDT的组合公理为此提供了形式化要求，作者希望研究固定表示神经网络加OLS输出头时该性质何时成立。

Method: 1) 理论上证明合并重拟合会重新计算用于加权源证据的逆Gram几何，可能导致共享偏好反转，并推导出精确和近似的保持条件；2) 引入尺度不变的Gram不匹配度量用于候选池优先级排序，提出几何导向正则化用于训练中塑造源几何；3) 开发三阶段审计流程，通过决策变化追踪严格成对反转直至任务定义的效用损失。
