Result: 在负荷竞价代理、医疗和金融决策代理等实验上，发现存在稳定和易反转的池化机制：负荷审计在代理效用下识别出可测的非零类源共识相对有害决策；跨领域审计表明可比的不匹配度可对应差异显著的保持率；几何导向目标占据不同的描述准确性-一致性-几何-危害操作点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛查、解析认证、几何导向训练和决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可测量且可操作，为跨源数据合并的神经网络可靠性提供了系统化的保障手段。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [254] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对鲁棒优化中部署解的期望存活时间进行了理论分析，针对各向同性高斯环境动态，将存活建模为离散首出问题，推导出严格下界和可计算的多步上界，并通过蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROOT（随时间鲁棒优化）是进化动态优化的新兴分支，强调解的持续性而非追踪移动最优。尽管领域发展迅速，但多数贡献停留在算法和实证层面，存活时间（解在未来环境中保持质量阈值的环境数）作为时间鲁棒性的重要度量，其期望值如何依赖环境动态、部署质量等问题特性缺乏理论理解。

Method: 将解的存活建模为离散首出问题（discrete first-exit problem），在固定部署解和各向同性高斯环境动态假设下，推导存活期望的严格下界和可计算的多步上界。理论分析揭示了在缓变环境中期望存活以Θ(σ^-2)规模增长，在高维环境中趋近于最小值1（即一个未来变化）。随后通过全面的蒙特卡洛实验验证理论预测，并检验对建模假设和参数不确定性的敏感性。

Result: 期望存活时间在缓变环境中以Θ(σ^-2)规模增长，在高维情形下逼近其最小值（一个未来环境变化）。蒙特卡洛研究证实了理论预测，并展示了该界可用于优化后的部署决策支持，识别部署时间要求何时可被保证、何时可被排除、何时在分析上仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该框架为部署寿命提供了分析性刻画，建立了从优化结果到部署决策之间的理论桥梁，为ROOT领域提供了首个系统的存活时间理论分析，并明确了其在保证部署时限方面的能力边界。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [255] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上等距回归（isotonic regression）的最坏情况自由度给出了完全尖锐的有限样本刻画，并通过解析数论改进了自由度上界（主导项为 (3/(4π²)^(1/3))·n^(2/3)），进而首次推导出等距回归的期望校准误差（ECE）的非平凡无分布保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等距回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但其在有限样本下的自由度行为尚未被精确刻画。已有的自由度上界不够尖锐，且等距回归在分布自由（distribution-free）条件下的校准误差保证此前缺乏非平凡结果。

Method: 作者利用解析数论方法，识别出使等距回归产生最多不同拟合值的二元序列，从而建立自由度（即不同拟合值数量）的尖锐上界；将该确定性自由度界应用于校准问题，在仅假设Y∈{0,1}的条件下推导出ECE的无分布保证。

Result: 得到了自由度最坏情况的精确前沿界，其主导项系数为 3/(4π²)^(1/3) ≈ 常数倍 n^(2/3)，优于此前结果；并基于该界首次给出了等距回归ECE的非平凡、完全模型无关和分布无关的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作同时回答了等距回归自由度极值组合问题，并为等距回归作为校准后处理方法提供了首个分布自由的ECE理论保证，具有理论性与实用性的双重价值。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [256] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation），一种基于径向Huber中心的非线性聚合块均值的鲁棒估计方法，在有限二阶矩条件下建立Hilbert空间多数定理和MOM阶偏差界，在有限三阶矩下支持通常参数速率的均值推断，并在重尾与高斯数据上兼顾鲁棒性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均值估计对重尾数据缺乏鲁棒性；几何中位数（MOM）虽然鲁棒但缺少一个能向均值效率靠拢的阈值参数，导致其在数据干净时效率不足。作者希望设计一种方法，既能像中位数一样抵抗重尾污染，又能通过调节阈值逼近样本均值的效率。

Method: HOMER将数据分块后计算块均值，再通过径向Huber中心聚合这些块均值。其包含两种形式：canonical HOMER和pseudo-HOMER，二者都对每个块的得分进行有界约束，并在中位数式鲁棒性与经验均值之间进行插值。canonical形式在二次区域内恢复样本均值，pseudo形式在阈值增大时趋近样本均值。文中建立了Hilbert空间中的多数定理和MOM阶偏差界，并证明了渐近线性性与一致的sandwich协方差估计。推断部分要求块大小和块数同时增长，且块大小增长更快，以支持有限维投影在通常参数速率下的均值推断。

Result: 重尾模拟显示，当少数块摘要被位移时HOMER保持稳定；在干净高斯数据上，两种版本都接近经验均值的效率。有限块sandwich区间存在覆盖不足问题，尤其是在偏斜的函数型数据上。进一步实验表明，当污染影响大多数块或破坏普通块内均值时，方法会失效。

Conclusion: HOMER在均值估计中有效实现了鲁棒性与效率之间的可调权衡，理论上在有限矩条件下具有保证，但实际中有限块推断的覆盖性能仍需改进，且方法的鲁棒性依赖于污染仅影响少数块且不破坏块内均值的假设。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [257] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的波长区域选择方法，将近红外光谱中糖含量预测的波长选择问题建模为二元黑箱优化问题，通过稀疏二次代理模型和Thompson采样结合模拟或量子退火求解，实验表明该方法相比遗传算法和模拟退火能提高偏最小二乘回归的预测精度，并得到更一致、更稳定的波长选择结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析中，波长选择是提升预测精度和可解释性的关键预处理步骤，但传统方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）容易陷入过拟合，且所选波长区域不稳定。作者希望提出一种更鲁棒的组合优化方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 将波长区域选择建模为二元黑箱优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化框架：构建稀疏二次代理模型来近似目标函数，通过Thompson采样顺序提取感兴趣的波长区域，将采集函数最小化转化为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，并使用模拟退火或量子退火求解。

Result: 在糖含量预测任务中，该方法相比遗传算法和模拟退火提升了偏最小二乘回归（PLSR）的预测精度；所选波长区域在单比特局部扰动下均方根误差波动极小，表明收敛到更平滑的误差曲面，避免了孤立的过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化为光谱预测任务中的鲁棒特征选择提供了一个有效框架，能够获得更稳定、更一致的波长选择结果，具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [258] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文对基于深度神经网络的Engression方法进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三个组成部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下建立了收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression方法通过能量评分（一种严格适当的评分规则）学习条件分布，但此前缺乏对其理论性质的深入分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，为Engression方法提供严格的理论误差分析框架。

Method: 将Engression的超额风险分解为三个组成部分：近似误差（approximation error）、随机误差（stochastic error）和蒙特卡洛误差（Monte Carlo error）；基于该分解，在假设目标条件生成器具有组合光滑性（compositional smoothness）结构的前提下，推导收敛率。

Result: 成功建立了Engression在深度神经网络实现下的收敛率，通过误差分解将问题转化为可独立分析的三部分，并在组合光滑性假设下给出了收敛速率的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为Engression方法的理论性质提供了系统性分析，其误差分解框架和收敛率结果补充了该方法在深度网络实现下的理论基础，有助于理解其在实际应用中的性能表现。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [259] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RPC（Randomized Projection with Clipping）方法，解决多物品用户的本地差分隐私均值估计中的中毒攻击鲁棒性问题。通过随机投影和裁剪限制攻击者能力，并基于精确的偏差分析提出修正方法，消除了偏差-方差权衡，从而进一步缩小输出空间以增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP协议易受投毒攻击，已有的防御策略主要针对单物品用户。实际场景中用户常拥有多个物品（稀疏向量），此时输出空间更大，攻击者可以进行更强大的攻击且不易被检测，因此需要针对多物品用户的鲁棒防御方法。

Method: 提出Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC)方法：服务器向每个用户发送随机二值向量，用户将本地数据投影到该向量上并进行裁剪以限制攻击者的能力。针对裁剪引入的偏差，通过精确分析得到偏差的闭式表达式并设计修正方法，避免了偏差-方差权衡，从而可以进一步降低裁剪阈值、缩小输出空间并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 提供了在所有可能攻击下的估计误差的严格理论保证。数值实验表明：在可信环境下，新方法与现有方法性能相当或更优，本身就是高效的估计器；在不可信环境下，对投毒攻击具有显著更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RPC方法不仅能够有效防御多物品用户场景下的LDP投毒攻击，而且在无攻击场景下也表现出优秀的估计性能，是一种兼顾鲁棒性和效率的均值估计方法。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [260] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在RKHS框架下研究了对抗训练（adversarial training）的泛化误差界，发现对抗训练的最优泛化率可能慢于极小极大预测基准，其统计精度损失源于对抗稳健性与观测噪声的交互作用。为此提出两阶段噪声去偏方法，消除混合项中的噪声贡献，使估计器达到（对数因子内的）极小极大多项式率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是保护模型免受对抗攻击的强有力手段，但在非参数框架下其泛化行为尚不清晰，特别是对抗稳健性与泛化之间的权衡缺乏系统性理论刻画。

Method: （1）在RKHS框架下，通过核积分算子推导源一致的泛化误差上界，刻画其与稳健性水平、样本量、源光滑度和核谱的依赖关系；（2）在固定的多项式谱模型上建立匹配下界，揭示统计精度损失；（3）提出两阶段噪声去偏程序，估计并从混合稳健性项中移除噪声贡献。

Result: 推导出RKHS对抗训练估计器的源一致泛化误差界；证明最优平衡泛化率可比极小极大预测基准更慢，揭示对抗训练存在统计精度损失；提出的噪声去偏估计器在适当选择稳健性水平时达到（至多对数因子）极小极大多项式率；数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 对抗训练中稳健性与泛化之间的权衡源于稳健性混合项中噪声贡献与逼近项的矛盾：噪声贡献降低估计复杂度但拖慢逼近率。两阶段噪声去偏方法可有效解决这一问题，为对抗训练在非参数框架下的泛化行为提供了新解释和原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [261] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展用于非平稳非线性回归问题中的联合特征子集和子空间识别。EOMR具有线性复杂度的迭代与内存开销，在混沌和流体动力学问题（Lorenz-96系统、Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克等离子体模型）上显著优于梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络及TabPFN等先进AI/ML工具，预测误差和模型复杂度均低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习工具在高维、小样本、非平稳非线性的复杂物理系统（如混沌和等离子体动力学）预测中表现不佳，需要一种能够同时进行特征子集筛选和子空间识别的鲁棒学习方法。

Method: 将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展为熵最优流形回归（EOMR），实现特征子集和子空间的联合同时识别。算法在迭代和内存复杂度上均呈线性扩展，适用于非平稳和非线性回归问题。

Result: 在Lorenz-96强混沌（F=8）和极强混沌（F=12）以及Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克边缘等离子体数据上，EOMR相对于通用梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN等工具，均实现了数个数量级更低的均方根预测误差和更小的模型复杂度。对Hasegawa-Wakatani模型，EOMR仅需8个参数的线性、因果、弱平稳自回归过程即可蒸馏出前导EOF动力学的简洁描述。

Conclusion: EOMR在高维小样本的非线性非平稳动力学预测问题上展现出显著优势，能够以极低的模型复杂度获得高精度的预测，并可从数据中提取出物理上可解释的简洁动力学模型。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [262] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了深度学习中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，聚焦于基于集成和近似贝叶斯的方法及其输出的度量汇总。相比现有综述，其贡献在于深入探讨高效集成近似与单次前向方法，并统一区分"生成预测分布的方法"与"汇总不确定性的度量"。方法按五个家族归类：贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、末层/单次前向方法；同时涵盖证据网络、共形预测、校准、OOD检测与选择性预测等相邻工作，最后讨论了LLM中的不确定性及开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统架构缺乏有原则的不确定性量化手段。现有UQ综述在高效集成近似和单次前向方法上的覆盖不够深入，且缺乏对"方法"与"度量"的清晰区分，因此需要一篇结构化的批判性综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 本文将UQ方法组织为五个家族：贝叶斯神经网络、Monte Carlo Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、以及末层/单次前向方法。综述对每个方法考察理论动机、实现方式、实证表现和局限性；同时介绍集成多样性理论、不确定性度量及其分解（对比熵分解与成对散度度量），并统一评估方法论。最后专节讨论LLM中的不确定性和开放研究方向（高效分类认知度量、末层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性与校准、混合架构）。

Result: 该综述建立了统一的评估基础，使定性比较具有共同依据；系统区分了产生预测分布的方法与汇总不确定性的度量之间的关系；并指出在高效认知度量（尤其分类任务）、末层多样性、偏移下多样性与校准、以及混合架构等方面仍存在开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 本文为深度学习UQ领域提供了深度聚焦于集成近似与单次前向方法的批判性综述，澄清了方法-度量两分解框架，巩固了评估体系，并为不确定性量化的未来研究指明了方向，尤其是大语言模型中的不确定性问题。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [263] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-FRL（双稳健函数表示学习），一种将不规则、函数型纵向历史转化为目标估计量状态表示的交叉拟合工作流。通过函数编码器、时序编码器、扰动头及EIF靶向诊断，DR-FRL确保状态表示足以支持估计方程，使表示误差进入与普通扰动误差相同的二阶乘积残差，从而在显式条件下实现均值估计量的渐近线性。模拟与VitalDB实际数据审计均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究中的历史记录常表现为不规则函数型片断（如实验室值、生理信号、传感器流、图像衍生汇总），在不等且信息性的时间点被测量。标准双稳健估计器通常需要标量汇总，而序列学习器优化的预测损失未必能稳定高效影响函数（EIF）。需要一个能将不规则历史转化为估计目标准确状态表示的框架。

Method: DR-FRL采用交叉拟合工作流，包含：(1) 函数型与时序编码器将点云和先前历史映射为状态表示；(2) 扰动头估计结局、治疗和删失函数；(3) EIF靶向验证、校准、重叠、尾部及消融诊断，评估状态是否支持估计方程。Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计器单独处理，而非替代Wald推断。

Result: 模拟实验表明，当函数型混淆维度高、测量具信息性、支持较弱或伪结局重尾时，DR-FRL表现出增益。VitalDB实际数据审计显示，DR-FRL能利用不规则的实验室点云，并得到有用的阴性发现：对于该ICU转归端点，标量实验室汇总已携带大量端点相关信息。

Conclusion: 若所选状态保留了EIF所需的扰动信息，表示误差与普通扰动误差进入相同的二阶乘积残差，均值估计量在显式的速率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下是渐近线性的。DR-FRL为不规则函数型纵向数据的因果推断提供了理论保证且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Discrete to continuum limits in Bayesian inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27408)
*Alexander Katsevich*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了贝叶斯反问题中后验测度的离散-连续极限理论，证明了离散高斯先验和后验在适定极限下弱收敛于连续定律，并在网格细分与小噪声联合极限下证明MAP估计收敛、后验集中及尺度化波动收敛于高斯分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯反问题的连续先验理论通常"连续优先"假设先验和后验已给定，再研究离散化。然而实际数值计算中，有限维离散测度才是真正被使用的对象。本文旨在从后验层面出发，反向构建连续极限，填补离散-连续理论中的空白。

Method: 方法分为两步：(1) 固定总信息参数τ和正则化强度κ，证明L²上重构离散高斯先验与后验测度的弱收敛；(2) 考虑N→∞且τ_N,κ_N→∞（κ_N/τ_N固定）的耦合极限，在N与τ_N满足显式增长条件时，证明重构离散MAP估计收敛到连续代价泛函的唯一极小元u_*，后验测度集中于u_*，且中心化重标度波动收敛到N(0,Q^{-1})，其中Q为连续代价泛函在u_*处的Hessian。

Result: 证明了离散后验测度的弱收敛性；建立了MAP估计在耦合网格细分与小噪声极限下收敛于连续极限极小元；建立了后验测度集中性与高斯波动定律（以连续代价泛函Hessian的逆为协方差）；并证明了先取连续后验再取小噪声-高正则化极限与直接取离散极限两种路径得到相同的确定性极限和高斯波动律。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了"后验层面"的离散到连续理论，表明无论先构建连续后验再取极限，还是直接对离散测度取极限，得到的确定性极限与高斯波动定律一致，为贝叶斯反问题数值方法的理论基础提供了严格保证。

Abstract: We develop a posterior-level discrete-to-continuum theory for Bayesian inverse problems whose finite-dimensional priors arise from local finite-difference regularization and whose likelihoods are of a general convex GLM-type form. In contrast to continuum-first approaches, the continuum prior and posterior are not assumed at the outset but are constructed as limits of the finite-dimensional probability measures used in numerical computation.
  First, with the total information parameter $τ$ and the regularization strength $κ$ fixed, we prove weak convergence on $L^2$ of the reconstructed discrete Gaussian priors and posterior measures to well-defined continuum laws. We then consider a coupled grid-refinement and small-noise limit in which $N\to\infty$ and $τ_N,κ_N\to\infty$, while $κ_N/τ_N$ remains fixed. Under explicit growth conditions relating $N$ and $τ_N$, we prove that the reconstructed discrete MAP estimates converge to the unique minimizer $u_*$ of the limiting continuum cost functional, that the reconstructed posterior measures concentrate at $u_*$, and that their centered and rescaled fluctuations converge to $\mathcal N(\vec 0,Q^{-1})$, where $Q$ is the Hessian of the continuum cost functional at $u_*$. Finally, we show that the same deterministic limit and Gaussian fluctuation law are obtained by first constructing the continuum posterior and then taking its small-noise, high-regularization limit.

</details>


### [265] [Sample Complexity for the 2-Gromov-Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27514)
*Pui Kuen Leung,Riku Okada,Samuel Lok-Hei Wong*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧支撑概率测度之间2-Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）距离的经验plug-in估计量的样本复杂度，证明其期望误差以n^{-2/((d_x∧d_y)∨4)}的速度收敛（临界维度含对数因子），该速率是sharp的。证明基于欧氏距离作为半空间特征图平方L²距离的几何表示，以及边际居中技巧恢复成本函数的凹性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov-Wasserstein距离在度量空间之间的形状匹配、域适应等任务中应用广泛，但其经验估计量的样本复杂度此前尚未被精确刻画。由于GW距离不依赖具体度量空间结构、天然具有置换不变性，其样本复杂度的推导比经典Wasserstein距离更困难，需要新的理论工具。

Method: 1) 将欧氏距离表示为半空间特征映射的平方L²距离之差，从而得到GW泛函的变分对偶形式（一族由无限维辅助参数索引的经典最优传输问题）；2) 对所得成本函数引入边际居中（marginal recentering），恢复所需的凹性/半凹性结构，从而获得sharp的度量熵界；3) 结合经验过程估计，得到由两个环境维度中较小者（但至少为4）决定的收敛速率。

Result: 证明了对独立样本的经验plug-in估计量满足E|D_2²(μ̂_n,ν̂_n)-D_2²(μ,ν)| ≲ n^{-2/((d_x∧d_y)∨4)}(log n)^{1_{d_x∧d_y=4}}，该速率在临界维度下关于对数因子是sharp的。

Conclusion: 该工作为GW距离的经验估计建立了第一个sharp的样本复杂度理论结果，揭示了GW距离的收敛速率由较小的环境维度决定（下限为4），并展示了变分对偶与边际居中技术在非凸/非凹最优传输问题分析中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the sample complexity of the empirical plug-in estimator for the $2$-Gromov-Wasserstein distance $D_2$ between compactly supported probability measures on Euclidean spaces. Let $μ$ and $ν$ be supported on compact subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ and $\mathbb{R}^{d_y}$, respectively, and let $\widehatμ_n$ and $\widehatν_n$ be their empirical measures based on independent samples of size $n$. We prove that \[ \mathbb{E}\left|D_2^2(\widehatμ_n,\widehatν_n)-D_2^2(μ,ν)\right| \lesssim n^{-2/((d_x\wedge d_y)\vee 4)} (\log n)^{\mathbf 1_{\{d_x\wedge d_y=4\}}}. \] This rate is sharp up to the logarithmic factor in the critical dimension. The proof is based on a geometric representation of the Euclidean distance as a squared $L^2$-distance between half-space feature maps. This yields a variational dual formulation of the Gromov-Wasserstein functional in terms of a family of classical optimal transport problems indexed by an infinite-dimensional auxiliary parameter. Although the resulting cost functions need not be semiconcave in either argument, we introduce a marginal recentering of the costs that restores the concavity structure needed for sharp metric-entropy bounds. Combining this representation with empirical-process estimates gives a rate governed by the smaller of the two ambient dimensions.

</details>


### [266] [Selfnormalization for relevant inference with supremum-type statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27818)
*Patrick Bastian*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自归一化方法，用于推断函数型时间序列中以supremum范数衡量的相关变化。由于supremum范数不具有Hadamard可微性，作者将其替换为光滑的log-sum-exp逼近，并基于其导数构造投影自归一化器，从而得到渐近枢轴量的检验统计量。通过多级平滑组合消除主导偏差项，无需直接估计极值集合的几何量，最终在温和正则条件下得到渐近精确的相关变化检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型时间序列中相关变化（relevant changes）的推断是重要问题，但supremum范数作为衡量变化的工具存在根本性困难：它不是Hadamard可微的，因此标准的基于投影的自归一化方法不适用，且极限分布可能依赖于极值集合的几何结构和长期协方差结构，严重阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 1) 将不可微的supremum范数替换为光滑的log-sum-exp逼近；2) 从该逼近的导数构造投影自归一化器；3) 推导孤立非退化极值和正测度极值集合两种情形下的显式平滑偏差展开；4) 通过组合多个平滑水平消去主导偏差项，避免直接估计极值数量、曲率或测度等几何量；5) 得到渐近枢轴的检验统计量，其分布仅依赖于断点位置。

Result: 所得检验统计量具有渐近枢轴分布，与长期协方差冗余参数无关，仅依赖于断点位置；在温和正则条件下，该检验对相关变化是渐近精确的（asymptotically exact）。

Conclusion: 提出的平滑与偏差校正原则提供了一个通用框架，可将自归一化方法与supremum型统计量相结合，适用于涉及相关假设（relevant hypotheses）的各类问题。

Abstract: We develop a selfnormalized approach to inference for relevant changes in functional time series measured by the supremum norm. The main difficulty is that the supremum norm is not Hadamard differentiable, so standard projection-based selfnormalization does not apply and the limiting distribution may depend on the geometry of the extremal set and the long-run covariance structure. We address this problem by replacing the supremum norm with a smooth log-sum-exp approximation and constructing a projected selfnormalizer from its derivative. The resulting statistic has an asymptotically pivotal distribution that is free of long-run covariance nuisance parameters and depends only on the break location. We derive explicit smoothing-bias expansions for both isolated nondegenerate extrema and extremal sets of positive measure. To avoid direct estimation of geometric quantities such as the number, curvature, or measure of the extrema, we combine several smoothing levels to cancel the leading bias terms. This yields an asymptotically exact test for relevant changes under mild regularity conditions. More generally, the proposed smoothing and bias-correction principles provide a framework for combining selfnormalization with supremum-type statistics in problems involving relevant hypotheses.

</details>


### [267] [OT-FairBoost: Optimal Transport-Guided Gradient Boosting for Fairness Regularization on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28014)
*Veronika Shilova,Abdoulaye Sakho,Younes Boumoussou,Laurent Risser,Jean-Michel Loubes,Emmanuel Malherbe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出OT-FairBoost，一个将Wasserstein-2距离惩罚直接嵌入梯度提升树目标函数的in-processing公平性框架，通过在LightGBM中扩展Wasserstein-2距离的样本级梯度估计到离散分布和Hessian对角线，在二分类、回归和多组敏感属性场景下均取得最佳准确率-公平性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于树的模型（如梯度提升）在表格数据上仍具竞争力且被广泛使用，但同样存在跨人口群体的算法偏见问题。现有的公平性缓解方法中，针对梯度提升模型的pre-processing和post-processing方法较多，而in-processing方法却很少，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: OT-FairBoost将Wasserstein-2距离惩罚直接整合到梯度提升树的损失函数中，作为一种in-processing公平性约束策略。具体做法是将Wasserstein-2距离在组间预测分布上的样本级梯度估计扩展到离散分布情形，并考虑Hessian对角线的加权，随后将该机制集成到LightGBM的训练流程中，支持二分类、回归和多组敏感属性多种设置。

Result: 在二分类、回归和多组敏感属性三种实验设置下，OT-FairBoost均实现了最优的准确率-公平性权衡，优于现有的替代方法。

Conclusion: OT-FairBoost成功弥合了梯度提升模型in-processing公平性方法的空白，通过最优传输理论有效优化了Demographic Parity和Equalized Odds等群体公平性准则，同时保持了模型预测性能。

Abstract: Although neural-based machine learning models have received a lot of attention recently, tree-based models such as gradient boosting are competitive for tabular data and therefore remain widely used in various applications of AI. As when using other machine learning predictive models, they can however yield discriminative predictions across demographic groups, due to so-called algorithmic biases. These undesirable phenomena have motivated the emergence of new regulatory frameworks and various AI fairness strategies. While several pre-and post-processing methodologies exist to mitigate such bias on gradient boosting models, only a few in-processing methods have been proposed. To bridge this gap, we introduce OT-FairBoost, a novel in-processing framework that incorporates a Wasserstein-2 distance penalty directly into the objective function of gradient-boosted trees. This OT-based mitigation strategy has been shown to efficiently optimize group fairness criteria such as Demographic Parity and Equalized Odds on neural-based predictions. To adapt this approach for gradient boosting, we extend the sample-wise gradient estimation of the Wasserstein-2 distance between group predictions to discrete distributions and hessian diagonals. We then integrate our approach into the LightGBM training procedure and evaluate it across binary classification, regression, and multi-group sensitive attribute settings. Experimental results in each of these settings demonstrate that OT-FairBoost achieves best accuracy-fairness trade-offs against alternatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种递归Transformer范式（Tiny Recursive Model、Depth Recursive Transformer和简单递归Transformer）的硬件感知评估，用于替代昂贵的工程仿真代理建模。研究发现递归权重共享Transformer在低维小数据工程设计中，能在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供有效的权衡，避免过拟合并降低内存与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在工程设计的低维小数据集上往往参数过多，导致过拟合而非精度提升，同时带来不必要的内存和计算开销。工程仿真数据生成成本高昂，亟需转向关注额外计算而非额外可学习参数的架构。

Method: 对三种递归Transformer范式进行系统的硬件感知评估：(a) Tiny Recursive Model，(b) 作者提出的Depth Recursive transformer，(c) 简单递归transformer。在预测性能（Recall、Mean Reciprocal Rank）、参数数量、计算复杂度（FLOPs）方面进行系统比较，并在两个低维工程预测任务上验证：1）先进半导体封装的熱机可靠性分析（应力与翘曲预测）；2）Laplace PDE迭代数值求解器的电容场计算。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在低维小数据工程代理建模场景下，能够有效减少过拟合和参数开销，同时在预测精度（Recall、MRR）和计算效率（FLOPs）之间达到良好平衡，为资源受限场景下选择递归Transformer架构提供了实用设计指南。

Conclusion: 递归权重共享Transformer为小数据工程代理建模提供了一个有效且可泛化的精度-参数效率-计算成本权衡方案，可替代昂贵的传统Transformer和第一性原理仿真。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [269] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）问题，指多模态增量学习中各模态相对贡献及交互在增量任务间不稳定变化，并提出CMCDR正则化方法（含基于回放和无回放两种版本）来保持旧任务模态贡献结构，缓解多模态持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续学习（MMCL）方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似度，但忽略了各模态的相对贡献及其交互在增量任务间是否保持稳定的问题——即模态贡献漂移（MCD）。这一忽略导致现有方法无法可靠缓解该类漂移，造成任务间的知识遗忘。

Method: 提出用MCD分数量化模态贡献漂移，该分数结合了在受控模态子集干预下的贡献强度变化和相对依赖变化。基于此提出CMCDR正则化：回放版使用模态子集干预作为诊断探针作用于存储的旧样本，比较当前模型与冻结旧模型之间的贡献分布并约束旧样本的模态特定及交互贡献变化；无回放版使用当前任务样本作为探针，蒸馏冻结旧模型的旧任务贡献响应来正则化贡献分布。

Result: 在多模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上的实验验证了CMCDR的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 模态贡献漂移是MMCL中一个被现有方法忽视的关键遗忘因素，CMCDR通过显式保持模态贡献结构能够有效缓解该类漂移，且能适应是否可用旧样本的不同设置。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [270] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍DoTime——一个开放、可扩展、理论可溯源的多元时序结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，支持干预与反事实采样，并发布了冻结评估套件及基线实现，实证表明干预训练相比同等容量的观测模型在方向准确性上具有可测量的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断基准大多为观测性、小规模或领域特定的，在医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键场景中缺乏对干预性和反事实估计的支持。作者希望填补这一空白，提供一个可扩展的、理论上扎实的时序因果模型生成器，并推动因果基础模型的发展。

Method: 设计并实现了一个多元时序结构因果模型（TSCMs）生成器DoTime，以PyPI包(dotime)形式发布，并附带四个冻结评估套件。新增能力包括：连续时间干预窗口、带正性保护的反事实采样模式、作为间断时间序列严格泛化的切换式SCM、通过切换SCM参数保证非平稳动态、以及将趋势和结构断裂置于评估窗口内的确定性斜坡与正弦干预轮廓。套件涵盖10万条轨迹的训练规模快照和8种命名识别结构，提供精确的真实标签（同一SCM下的配对干预轨迹及连续时间套件中的共享噪声反事实），同时提供参考基线与评估框架，并进行三种子交叉验证的干预训练与观测模型的对比测试。

Result: 在结构匹配的留出情节评估中，基于干预先验拟合网络（PFN）的准确率差距在所有结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下均为正，即干预训练在每种测试条件下都对观测模型取得了方向准确性的可量化优势，支持了论文提出的可证伪声明。

Conclusion: DoTime为时序因果推断提供了可扩展的干预与反事实基准生成能力，可作为因果基础模型的先验来源；干预训练带来的方向准确性优势在所有测试条件下得到验证，为其有效性提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [271] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid，这是首个从统一广告平台视角设计的自动出价综合基准，涵盖同质竞争、异质竞争和促销竞争三种真实场景，并系统评估了多种现有自动出价方法。此外，论文还提出基于流匹配的新型出价方法BidFlow，利用其表达力强的策略表征应对动态竞争环境，在快手在线实验中获得+0.68%的目标成本改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动出价算法仅聚焦DSP侧（需求方平台），只关注最大化广告主转化，但当前大型广告平台（如社交媒体和电商公司）已内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange功能。从平台视角出发，自动出价算法不仅要最大化广告主转化，还要最大化平台总收入。由于缺乏以平台为中心的评估框架，且自动出价研究亟需推进，因此需要构建统一的平台视角评估基准。

Method: 论文提出PlatformBid基准，定义三种代表性设置：(1)同质竞争——广告主使用相同算法；(2)异质竞争——广告主采用多样化算法策略；(3)促销竞争——部分广告主在促销活动（如黑色星期五）期间大幅提高预算。系统评估涵盖经典控制方法、基于强化学习的方法和近期生成式方法。此外提出BidFlow，基于流匹配（flow-matching）的自动出价方法，利用其表达力强的策略表征应对动态竞争环境。

Result: 系统评估了多种已有的自动出价方法在三种竞争设置下的表现，并提出了BidFlow方法。在快手的在线实验中，BidFlow实现了+0.68%的目标成本提升，为PlatformBid的离线-在线一致性提供了部署证据。

Conclusion: PlatformBid是首个从统一广告平台视角出发的综合自动出价基准，弥补了现有评估框架的缺失。BidFlow方法验证了流匹配在自动出价任务中的有效性，且在线实验证明了该基准的离线评估结果与在线部署表现具有一致性，为后续自动出价研究提供了重要参考平台。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的"功能重建"方法（Functional Reconstruction），用于将MHA/GQA模型转换为MLA（多头潜在注意力）后，恢复其与原始模型的推理一致性，从而提升投机解码中草案token的接受率。实验覆盖192种配置，结果显示在64个任务单元中有37个显著改善接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA因其紧凑的潜在状态减少KV缓存和内存带宽，对长上下文LLM推理越来越重要。但由于现有开放权重模型多为MHA/GQA架构，需要转换才能获得MLA的缓存效率。直接转换会导致注意力函数误差，虽然对独立生成影响不大，但会大幅降低投机解码中草案token的接受率，从而削弱投机解码的加速效果。

Method: 将MLA草案构建问题从"缓存压缩"重新定义为"功能重建"。具体做法是：对每个转换后的MLA注意力模块进行端到端优化，使其在校准隐藏状态上复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块的输出投影（output projection）响应。该方法与具体转换器无关，是转换后的后处理步骤，且不需要验证器logits或监督信号。

Result: 在64个匹配任务单元（192种配置包括4个Llama/Qwen草案-目标对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA两种转换器、HF和vLLM两种后端、4个200提示词任务）中，以0.5个百分点为报告容差，Functional Reconstruction在37个单元中显著提升接受率，26个基本不变，仅1个显著下降。

Conclusion: 通过功能重建而非单纯缓存压缩的方式构建MLA草案，可以有效恢复转换后模型与原始模型之间的一致性，显著提升投机解码的草案接受率，是一种可行且有效的MHA/GQA到MLA转换后优化方法。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [273] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLPF（Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback），通过将执行结果转化为分级奖励（stage reward）来训练代码智能体生成更快的正确程序。实验表明，在PerfCodeBench上，Qwen3-32B的正确可运行解决方案从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%，训练后的模型可与更强的开源系统竞争，且优化行为可迁移到EffiBench-X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型训练大多仅以"正确性"作为监督信号（如是否通过测试），但两个通过相同测试的程序在运行时可能差异巨大。对于系统代码而言，效率是重要的优化目标，但当前训练范式未将效率纳入训练信号，而仅将其视为评估指标。此外，运行时作为奖励信号存在脆弱性：仅在程序正确后才有意义、跨任务差异大、且当大多数采样程序无法编译或运行时几乎无指导作用。

Method: 提出RLPF框架，将执行结果转化为分级奖励（staged reward）：失败的代码按执行进度排序获得反馈；正确的代码按其相对于基线的改进（以专家参考为锚点）进行排名。这确保在程序正确前也有有用的训练信号（进度信号），正确后则提供性能敏感的信号（效率信号）。模型基于Qwen3-32B，在PerfCodeBench上使用RLPF进行微调。

Result: 在PerfCodeBench上，正确可运行解决方案从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。训练后模型与更强的开源权重系统竞争力相当。优化行为可适度迁移至EffiBench-X。进一步消融显示：模型生成的参考提供有用但较弱的监督；完整的分级组合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时的基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 结果表明代码智能体不仅可以被训练通过测试，还可以被训练优化其编写的程序。RLPF通过分级奖励机制成功弥合了"正确性"与"效率"之间的训练差距，为代码生成模型的高效性训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [274] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDO（Structure-Aware Data Organization），一种即插即用的数据组织框架，通过暴露驱动的反馈机制根据表示空间结构组织mini-batch组成和样本暴露，在SFT、DPO和GRPO上加速收敛，产生更一致的梯度和更均衡的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型后训练效率优化主要关注样本选择或训练调度，但数据组织本身被视为静态预处理步骤，基于嵌入的固定分组无法适应优化过程中样本暴露的动态演变。这导致所有样本获得相似的暴露量，尽管它们的优化需求不同，造成某些样本冗余更新而其他样本优化不足。

Method: 提出SDO框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 逐epoch在冻结的外部嵌入上操作，避免模型预热训练开销；(2) epoch内通过KNN邻域遍历的局部感知批处理形成连贯的mini-batches；(3) epoch间通过暴露平衡调度记录每个样本的参与度，降低过度暴露样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种训练范式上，SDO均加速了收敛，其中在早期到中期阶段收益最大；产生更一致的梯度，并实现不同问题类型间更均衡的准确率，同时不会永久排除训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO作为一种即插即用的数据组织框架，通过暴露驱动的动态反馈机制有效解决了静态数据组织无法适应训练动态的问题，在不永久丢弃样本的前提下提升了训练效率和最终性能的均衡性。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [275] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一种两阶段框架，将实例表示学习与受试者级别监督解耦，用于基于EEG的疾病诊断。第一阶段通过时间邻近窗口和受试者内子袋对齐进行无标签预训练，第二阶段将编码器迁移到注意力MIL聚合器并在受试者级别施加监督。在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个骨干网络上达到76.57%的平均准确率，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG疾病诊断管线将记录切分为实例并继承受试者标签进行实例级分类，这假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据；MIL虽避免了继承标签，但EEG数据集中受试者远少于实例，端到端MIL学到的表示质量受限。

Method: 两阶段框架BridgeMIL：阶段1通过时间邻近窗口对齐和受试者内子袋采样对编码器进行无标签预训练，使用方差和协方差正则化防止表示坍缩并减少冗余，不依赖负样本对；阶段2将编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅对受试者预测施加监督，并通过特征保留限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个代表性骨干网络中，BridgeMIL在15个数据集-骨干设置中的14个取得最高平均准确率，总体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。分析发现实例的继承标签可靠性差异显著，性能对受试者稀缺性比对实例稀缺性更敏感，且学习到了更结构化的表示空间。

Conclusion: 将监督信号与受试者级别的预测目标对齐，同时从大量EEG实例中学习而不给单个实例分配疾病标签，是EEG疾病诊断中更有效的方法，能够提升诊断准确率并获得更结构化的表示。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [276] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了4比特权重量化在多轮工具调用智能体场景中是否真的无损。在τ²-bench上，标准度量下量化看似免费，但过程分析揭示量化将模型原有的失败（如工具名幻觉）放大了2.5倍，只是被基准测试的十个错误预算掩盖了。缩小预算至两个错误后，分数差距（17分）重新显现，且仅出现在量化增加错误量的单元中。作者建议连同任务奖励一起报告每通道错误率和缩减预算下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化到4比特权重被广泛报道为几乎无损，但这一说法主要基于标准评测指标。随着多轮工具调用智能体成为量化最重要的应用场景，作者希望验证在真实智能体任务中，量化是否真的无损，还是仅仅是标准指标掩盖了过程层面的退化。

Method: 在τ²-bench上，对两个开源模型家族（含密集和MoE变体）、两个领域共八个单元进行实验，每个单元456个回合，分别在16位、8位和4位权重下评估。使用标准指标进行多重比较校正和等价性检验，同时对过程层面进行分析：量化前后失败模式的变化、失败集的相似度（秩相关）、错误体积增量。还引入了两个诊断方法：缩小错误预算至两个以重新暴露分数差距，以及针对性的错误修复提示测试。

Result: 标准指标下，所有八个单元在4比特量化下均未显示出通过多重比较校正的显著分数变化，最大的过程损伤单元内变化被等价性检验界定在±7.5分内。但过程分析显示，量化将模型已有的失败模式（电信领域的工具名幻觉，零售实体错误也有同样趋势）在体积上放大到2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），同时几乎不创造新失败（0.18%全新事件，秩相关≥0.94）。分数持平是因为基准的十个错误预算吸收了额外失败。缩小至两个错误预算后，17分的差距在量化增加错误体积的唯一单元中重新出现。针对性的错误修复提示恰好只在该单元中消除了损伤。

Conclusion: 4比特量化对工具调用智能体的标准分数看似无损，但过程层面存在被掩盖的真实损伤：量化放大了模型原有的系统性失败模式，只是被宽松的错误预算所掩盖。标准指标不足以评估量化对智能体的影响，应同时报告过程级诊断（每通道错误率和缩减预算下的成功率），这些可从日志中直接获得。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [277] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型中能力形成的瓶颈在于多个电路子部分必须在单次随机尝试中联合对齐（无部分分数），并证明这种联合对齐是能力形成的限速步骤。作者通过实验指纹、预注册的率方程模型、前向/后向预测实验，以及受控门控注意力模型的机制证明，系统性地支持了这一理论，并指出标准训练监控方式（验证损失）对此过程完全无感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的观点认为语言模型的能力是逐步涌现的，但作者观察到一种奇怪现象：只有全部电路部分都对时能力才出现，少一个部分则能力完全为零（"no-partial-credit"）。他们想弄清这种"全有或全无"的联合对齐是否是能力形成过程中真正的瓶颈/限速步骤，而非仅仅是一个现象配角。

Method: 方法分为五层：（1）两个"指纹"实验——无损设备上五部分电路缺三较三部分电路缺三等待时间相同（缺件数决定等待，而非规模），以及Pythia模型上7种能力×3规模中ablation一个部分保留中位17%能力，远低于部分分数预测的50-83%；（2）推导率方程 sites×attempts×drive×exp(-βK) 减去破坏项，并以冻结常数做三种预注册解读；（3）前向预测：能力在浓度阈值之上的混合物会按计划点火（10/10成功，0/12失败），且在点火前可从先兆预测到达时间（误差中位5%，6个保留模型）；（4）后向实验：学习被扣留能力的延迟随等待增长，超过临界步后永不点火，但验证损失平滑下降——标准监控完全盲区。定位损伤为头部承诺到基础数据，而只重新初始化query-key切片即可恢复可学性（6/6），value切片无效（0/6）；（5）在受控门控注意力模型中证明机制：占用导致截止期限，无需混合假设。此外发现SGD噪声未通过涨落-耗散检验，因此安装了该检验并用于退火、熔化和固定电路。

Result: 实验结果表明：联合对齐确实是能力形成的限速步骤；率方程以冻结常数预注册后在前向和后向方向上均验证成功——前向点火有明确的浓度阈值（10/10 vs 0/12），后向有临界延迟点；恢复可学性的干预（query-key切片重初始化）与无效对照（value切片）的6/6 vs 0/6对比清晰指向损伤位置；受控门控注意力模型证明了占用导致的截止时间机制。

Conclusion: 结论是：一个稀有事件（多部分联合对齐）足以解释能力涌现的速率与延迟，且其屏障随缺失部件数增长。这构成了能力形成的统一理论框架，同时揭示了标准训练监控（验证损失）对这类过程的不可见性，并提供了可操作的恢复手段。适用范围内为transformer中至1.4B规模的合取电路。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [278] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"long-horizon failure"需要有明确的定义和测量方法：必须将完整任务的实际成功率与基于短、独立阶段构建的基线预测进行比较，两者之差的对数比率称为"horizon residual"（地平线残差）。仅观察代理在长任务中表现更差不足以解释失败原因，需要区分错误累积、阶段难度增加、以及前期执行导致后期任务更难的"轨迹诱导退化"（trajectory-induced degradation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野基准测试普遍显示代理在任务变长时失败更多，但这一观察本身无法解释失败原因。三种可能的机制包括：更多阶段导致普通错误累积、长任务包含更难的单个决策、以及前序执行使后续工作更难（即轨迹诱导退化，其中文本累积尤为突出称为context rot）。作者认为要声称存在"long-horizon failure"，必须区分这些不同可能性。

Method: 提出方法论框架：基准测试须比较完整任务的实际成功率与由短、独立阶段构建的基线预测值。定义二者对数比率（log-ratio）为horizon residual。要求比较中使用相同的代理配置，并预先指定阶段划分、检查点、信息和预算的选择方式。

Result: horizon residual能够显示完整展开与所选基线之间的差异，但作者明确指出该残差本身只能揭示差异存在，仍需设计针对性实验来解释差异产生的原因。

Conclusion: 本文是position paper，主张在长视野基准测试中建立更严格的"long-horizon failure"判定标准：必须通过对比完整轨迹成功率与短阶段基线的预测来量化地平线残差，才能为后续的归因实验提供可靠依据。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [279] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE是一个风险引导的子空间专家混合模型，将模态可靠性定义为单模态预测器的预期损失，通过稀疏模态-专家路由和共享路径保留多模态互补性，在四个生物医学数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合通常假设更多证据带来更好预测，但实际中某些模态可能不可靠、冗余或与专用专家不匹配。现有模型虽能实现跨模态交互和样本自适应融合，但融合过程中赋予模态的影响力并不能揭示该来源是否真正可信。

Method: TIER-MoE引入风险引导的子空间专家混合模型：(1) 定义样本特定的模态可靠性为单模态预测器的预期损失，通过out-of-fold预测学习该风险；(2) 结合估计风险与专家特定子空间兼容性进行稀疏模态-专家路由；(3) 始终激活的共享路径保留多模态互补性。

Result: 在四个公共多模态生物医学数据集（阿尔茨海默病、皮肤病变恶性、视网膜分类）上，TIER-MoE在预测性能和概率校准方面优于最先进方法，Macro-F1和Brier分数一致提升，并在外部队列中展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: TIER-MoE证实了基于风险的模态可靠性估计能够有效指导模态-专家路由，在生物医学多模态分类任务中同时提升预测性能与不确定性校准，并具备良好的外部泛化性。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [280] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCause，一种利用专家历史诊断标签通过大语言模型（LLM）迭代精化因果图的根因分析（RCA）方法，并发布TeleRCA基准数据集。实验表明EvoCause在合成数据和真实生产数据上均显著优于未精化的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电信、云和微服务系统在组件故障时会发出相关的告警级联。RCA的目标是识别触发每个级联的少量根源告警。现有方法通常从观测日志中学习一个固定的因果图并预测零入度告警，但该图无法从历史事件的专家诊断中获益，缺乏利用专家知识闭环迭代优化的机制。

Method: EvoCause的工作流程为：专家标签仅约束哪些告警应为源节点，但不指定具体的边编辑。EvoCause利用LLM提出语义上合理的图编辑方案，同时通过确定性代码验证节点身份和确保无环性，并在标注的对齐集上保留最优图。在测试时仅使用精化后的图即可产生透明的预测，无需调用LLM。

Result: 在合成数据上，以PC因果发现算法初始化的EvoCause相比未精化的PC基线，Node F1提升11.59个百分点、Case EM提升9.40个百分点、Graph F1提升4.59个百分点，且降低了0.2379的nSHD。在TeleRCA上，将可读告警标题替换为匿名标识符使Node F1下降6.12个百分点、Case EM下降8.04个百分点，说明告警名称中的语义信息对图精化有重要贡献。TeleRCA包含485,681个告警事件，涵盖194种告警类型、5,621个资源。

Conclusion: EvoCause通过与专家标签闭环交互，利用LLM的语义理解能力和确定性验证机制实现了对因果图的有效精化，显著提升了RCA的性能。实验还揭示了告警名称的语义信息在因果图精化中的关键作用。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [281] [THGFM: Dual-Branch Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27303)
*Yixin Peng,Diego Collarana,Er Jin,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: THGFM是一个面向大规模时序异构图（temporal heterogeneous graph）的融合模型，通过双路径架构（共享空间的时序注意力 + 关系类型分区的时序注意力）结合类型条件的非竞争门控融合机制，并在注意力核内直接引入旋转时序注意力（Rotary Temporal Attention）编码相对时间，统一解决跨类型参数高效迁移与关系感知特化之间的权衡问题，在多个学术图基准上取得一致且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序异构图学习方法存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 无法同时兼顾参数高效的跨类型分享与关系感知的特化表达，二者存在矛盾；(2) 时间信息仅作为加性特征注入，而非融入注意力核内部的交互计算中，限制了时间建模的表达力。

Method: THGFM采用统一双路径架构：共享空间时序注意力分支负责参数高效的跨类型迁移；关系类型分区时序注意力分支负责关系感知特化。两条分支通过双路径关系-共享融合（Dual-Path Relational-Shared Fusion）集成，具体实现为类型条件非竞争门控求和融合（Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion），该机制为共享分支和特化分支分配独立、类型条件的特征级门控，允许两者独立增强或抑制，避免零和竞争。此外，引入旋转时序注意力（Rotary Temporal Attention），通过将查询和键按相对时间的一半相位移旋转后再进行匹配，将相对时间直接内嵌到注意力打分中。

Result: THGFM在多个学术图基准上一致超越基线图Transformer模型：六任务平均提升+3.25%；具体在OAG-CS PV上取得+12.37%的峰值相对增益，在PF-L2上+4.87%，PF-L1上+1.18%，OGBN-MAG、HTAG-ArXiv和HTAG-DBLP分别提升+4.24%、+3.73%和+4.61%。

Conclusion: THGFM通过双路径融合架构与旋转时序注意力，有效解决了时序异构图学习中跨类型分享与关系特化的矛盾以及时间注入方式局限的问题，验证了将时间直接编码进注意力核以及采用非竞争门控融合策略的有效性，展示了在大规模真实世界时序异构图上进行Web-scale建模的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Temporal heterogeneous graphs offer a natural abstraction for dynamic relational systems in which diverse node and relation types co-exist and evolve over time. Learning on such graphs requires jointly modeling cross-type structural heterogeneity and the temporal dynamics of interactions, yet existing methods still struggle to reconcile parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with relation-aware specialization, and typically inject time only as additive features outside the attention kernel. We propose \textbf{THGFM}, a web-scale temporal heterogeneous graph fusion model that addresses both limitations within a unified dual-path architecture. THGFM couples a \textit{Shared-Space Temporal Attention} branch for parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with a \textit{Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention} branch for relation-aware specialization, and integrates them through \textit{Dual-Path Relational--Shared Fusion}, instantiated with \textit{Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion}: a adaptive mechanism that assigns independent, type-conditioned feature-wise gates to the shared and specialized branches, allowing both to be amplified or suppressed without zero-sum competition. To directly incorporate relative time into the attention score, THGFM further introduces \textit{Rotary Temporal Attention}, which rotates queries and keys by half-phases of relative time before matching. THGFM consistently outperforms baseline graph transformer models on academic graphs benchmarks, delivering a $+3.25\%$ six-task mean gain, with peak relative gains of $+12.37\%$ on OAG-CS PV, $+4.87\%$ on PF-$L_2$, and $+1.18\%$ on PF-$L_1$, and $+4.24\%$, $+3.73\%$, and $+4.61\%$ on OGBN-MAG, HTAG-ArXiv, and HTAG-DBLP, respectively.

</details>


### [282] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal,Subhrajyoti Basu,Aritra Gir Mahanta,Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoT-Mediate行为学框架，通过扰动模型自身生成的思维链推理中的单一临床属性，并测量预测结果是否跟随编辑后的推理，来评估医学视觉语言模型（VLM）推理的因果中介性。在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上对1,000个VQA-RAD样本的评估显示，前缀强制续写比重新提示产生更高的中介保真度，且推理的上下文位置而非声称的来源决定了模型是否使用其生成推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答临床问题前会生成链式思维推理，但这种推理是否因果性地影响模型预测尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏一个行为学框架来严格区分推理中介与谄媚行为（sycophancy），需要建立可量化的评估方法。

Method: CoT-Mediate框架包含两个核心组件：（1）双臂协议，比较重新提示（re-prompting）与前缀强制续写（prefix-forced continuation）两种推理注入方式；（2）来源受控干预（provenance-controlled intervention），在保持推理内容完全一致的情况下仅改变归属来源，以分离推理中介与谄媚效应。

Result: 在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上各评估1,000个VQA-RAD样本。前缀强制续写始终比重新提示获得更高的中介保真度；来源分析揭示了不同模型特有的服从行为；移除视觉证据会增加对注入推理的依赖；侧向性（laterality）是最不被忠实跟踪的临床属性。

Conclusion: 推理注入的机制显著影响测量的保真度，且上下文位置而非声明来源是决定医学VLM是否使用其生成推理的主要因素。该发现对临床决策中VLM推理的可信度和可解释性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [283] [ZUNA1.1: A more flexible EEG foundation model for Denoising and Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27308)
*Christopher Warner,Jonas Mago,JR Huml,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZUNA1.1是一个380M参数的扩散自编码器，用于灵活重构EEG信号，支持可变长度序列（最长30秒）、任意数量的EEG通道、任意头皮位置以及通道内任意时间间隔的重构，性能至少与ZUNA1持平且显著优于标准方法，已以Apache 2.0协议开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG信号重构方法（如球面样条插值）灵活性有限，难以应对可变长度序列、任意通道数量与位置、以及通道内部分时间片段的重构需求，需要更通用和灵活的解决方案。

Method: 采用380M参数的扩散自编码器架构ZUNA1.1，支持(i)最长30秒的可变长度序列重构，(ii)任意数量的EEG通道与任意头皮位置，(iii)不仅可重构完整通道，还能重构通道内任意时间间隔。

Result: ZUNA1.1在多种重构任务上表现至少与之前的ZUNA1相当或更优，同时显著优于标准EEG去噪与重构方法（如MNE包中广泛使用的球面样条插值），且灵活性大幅提升。

Conclusion: ZUNA1.1在保持与ZUNA1相当性能的同时大幅增强了灵活性，可处理广泛的重构任务，并作为开源模型（Apache 2.0协议）发布，有望成为EEG信号重构领域的通用工具。

Abstract: We introduce ZUNA1.1, a 380M-parameter diffusion autoencoder for flexible EEG signal reconstruction. ZUNA1.1 is capable of reconstructing variable length sequences of up to 30s, with an arbitrary number of EEG channels at arbitrary scalp locations, and can reconstruct arbitrary temporal intervals within channels in addition to reconstructing entire channels. We demonstrate that ZUNA1.1 performs at least on par with our earlier ZUNA1 model, while being far more flexible and capable of handling a wide range of reconstruction tasks. ZUNA1.1 continues to substantially outperform standard EEG denoising and reconstruction methods such as spherical spline interpolation, which is ubiquitously deployed in the MNE package. The ZUNA1.1 model is released open source under the permissive Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [284] [Modeling Decisions in Blockchain Analytics: A Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Tree-Based vs. Sequential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27350)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种泄漏感知的以太坊Sybil机器人检测框架，包括Blind-Spot协议和Transaction Grammar表示方法，通过比较Transformer、BiLSTM序列模型与XGBoost、SVM表格模型，发现XGBoost在泄漏减少后的检测中表现更优，且延迟和能耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sybil机器人通过模仿合法用户在以太坊上提取空投奖励或影响治理。现有深度学习方法将区块链活动视为类语言序列，但存在两个问题：复杂序列模型计算成本高，难以实时监控；且其报告的性能可能因高信号智能合约导致的标签泄漏而被夸大。因此需要探索泄漏感知条件下不同模型的真实表现。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Blind-Spot协议，用于消除与高信号合约相关的捷径；(2) Transaction Grammar表示，从节奏(rhythm)、EVM执行结构和意图(intent)三个维度建模钱包行为。在此基础上比较Transformer和BiLSTM两类序列模型与XGBoost和SVM两类表格模型的性能。

Result: 在泄漏感知评估下，XGBoost在以太坊行为者分类任务中优于基于Transformer的序列模型，同时具有更低的推理延迟和估计能耗。

Conclusion: 贡献了一套泄漏感知的以太坊行为者分类框架和Transaction Grammar钱包行为表示方法。研究结论表明，在消除标签泄漏后，传统的基于树的表格模型在检测性能和部署实用性（延迟、能耗）上均优于复杂的深度序列模型。

Abstract: Sybil bots are Ethereum actors that imitate legitimate users to extract airdrop rewards or influence governance. Recent Sybil detection methods increasingly use deep learning and treat blockchain activity as a quasi-linguistic sequence. However, complex sequence models are computationally expensive for real-time monitoring, and their reported performance may be inflated by label leakage from high-signal smart contracts. We ask whether and how organic users, Sybil bots, and MEV bots differ in the structural complexity of their transaction histories; whether sequential models outperform tree-based tabular models once leakage is reduced; whether transaction order or timing provides the stronger behavioral signal; and whether the resulting models are practical for low-latency deployment. Our approach to leakage-aware Sybil bot detection consists of a Blind-Spot protocol and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. The former eliminates shortcuts associated with high-signal contracts, whereas the latter models wallets using rhythm, EVM execution structure, and intent. We evaluate this approach on Ethereum actor classification by comparing Transformer and BiLSTM sequence models against XGBoost and SVM baselines. We contribute a framework for leakage-aware Ethereum actor classification and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. Our results demonstrate that, under leakage-aware evaluation, XGBoost outperforms Transformer-based sequence models while providing lower latency and estimated energy use.

</details>


### [285] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压缩的相似性方法（Gzip-NCD结合EVM交易语法）来检测区块链中的Sybil攻击者，无需直接资金关联和监督训练，并通过Blind-Spot协议过滤高信号合约，实现对机器人钱包、有机用户和套利机器人的行为图构建与Sybil发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sybil检测方法依赖钱包间的代币转移构建图，或使用封闭集分类的机器学习算法，前者需要直接金融关联，后者对攻击策略频繁变化和规避手段缺乏鲁棒性。作者希望探索无需直接金融链接、无需监督训练的压缩式相似性方法，以应对攻击者策略的动态演变。
